# Layout rules: the Foundation pages' rulebook

Status: **rules**, written 2026-10-01, updated 2026-11-23.

The measured rules every card, field and list on the Foundation pages (Identity, Brand, Business and Strategy Systems) follows, the tokens and classes that carry them, the closed set of sizes, and the automated check that proves them. A page that breaks a rule here is a bug; a new pattern is added here first, then built. Every rule is carried by a shared token or class (`src/styles/global.css`, `card-folding.css`, the folders `conversation/` and `section-tiles/`), never by a per-page number: to change a rule, change its token.

Each rule is in one place; the sections are in reading order; the token table (section 2) is kept equal to the stylesheets by `npm run docs:values:check`; the values are the current ones; a change is written in the log (Records 1). The Customer Engagement tables and the action markers are in section 11. Rules are cited by section and number ("rule 14 of section 6"), so the numbers are kept as they are.

**Folded on 2026-11-21** (one owner per fact): the tile and card pattern (tile-and-card-pattern.md) went into sections 3, 6, 8 to 11, 13, 16 and 23, and the Customer Engagement layout (customer-engagement-layout.md) into section 11; sections 21 and 22 went into their home sections, each rule under its own bold words, and section 23 keeps its number. An older citation of them reads: 21.1, 21.2, 21.5 and 22.36, section 8; 21.12, 21.17 and 22.32, section 9; 22.13, 22.19 (the pop-ups), 22.29 to 22.31 and 22.34, section 10; 21.3, 21.4, 21.8, 21.9, 22.16, 22.21 and 22.22, section 11; 21.13 to 21.15, 22.1 to 22.6, 22.15, 22.17, 22.18, 22.20, 22.24 to 22.26, 22.28, 22.35 and 22.38, section 6; 22.8 and 22.27, section 3; 22.37, section 7 ("The Workbook"); 22.39, section 12; 21.6, 21.7, 21.16, 21.18, 22.9 to 22.12 and 22.19 (Quillfield), section 13; 22.7, section 15; 21.10, 21.11, 22.14, 22.23 and 22.33, section 16.

Where the rules need a decision the code could not give, section 20 lists it.

## 1. Principles

Five questions to ask of every card before calling it done. The rules
below are these, made measurable.

| Principle | What it means here | Checklist question |
|---|---|---|
| **Proximity** | Things that belong together sit together; things that do not are kept apart by the standard gaps, never by chance. A label is 6px from its control, a field 12px from the next, a card 12px from the next. | Can a reader tell, without reading, which label names which control and which fields form one group? |
| **Right-sized controls** | A control is as wide as the longest answer it must show, taken from a small closed set of widths, or it fills its column. Never wider "because there is room", never so narrow its value is cut. | Is every control one of the standard widths (or its column), and does its longest value show in full? |
| **Readability** | Text never overflows or is clipped; long names wrap and grow; text beside a control sits on the control's centre line; the order of fields is the order a founder thinks in. | Can every word be read without scrolling a box, and does the eye travel down one left edge? |
| **Noise** | Nothing is drawn that carries no information: no headers over rows that name themselves, no empty boxes, no second border inside a card, one reaction per kind of element. | If this element were removed, would the reader lose anything? |
| **Related to the answers** | Only information related to the founder's answers is shown (owner, 2026-11-15 15:10: "only information that is related to the answers should be presented. That covers the fees"). The app keeps its full reference data (every listed provider's fee in each of the six countries, `ChannelFeeData.ts`); a screen shows the part the answers use: the Legal Entity country's rows, for what a subchannel or an expense card names, and what the user made. | Does every row on this screen follow from an answer or from the user's own entry? |

**One margin: 12px** (the owner's rule). The side margins, the top and bottom margins and the gaps between things are all 12px (`--space-field`, `--card-padding`). The exceptions are the label of a field and its box (6px, `--space-label`) and the Description label with the text cell that opens under it.

---

## 2. Tokens

| Token | Value | Use |
|---|---|---|
| `--space-label` | 6px | a label to the control it names |
| `--space-field` | 12px | one field (label and control) to the next |
| `--space-card` | 12px | one card to the next in a list |
| `--bg-card-tint` | #f3f4f6 / #23272c / #f3e8d8 (light / dark / sepia) | the neutral card ground: every card without a hue (Legal Entity, Founders Profile, Brand Style, Business cards), so the white boxes stand out; every card text keeps 4.5:1 on it (faint text as `--text-faint-on-tint`) |
| `--card-padding` | 12px | inside every card **and every section box**, border to content (sides) |
| `--space-row` | 6px | one row of a table to the next |
| `--space-cell` | 12px | one cell of a row to the next inside a card: a field grid's columns, a pair row, a list's cells, a table's columns |
| `--space-column` | 12px (= `--space-card`) | one column to the next, wherever columns stand side by side: a page's answer columns (Identity's four, Brand's three, Strategy's four, Inventory's two), Legal Entity's paired section boxes, a Segment's three columns, its Description's two: a column is the same distance from its neighbour across as a card is down |
| `--list-number-gap` | 12px | every numbered list in a cell, editable (`NumberedListInput`: Roles, Skills, Action list, Initiatives, SWOT...) or shown (`NumberedListStatic`, the Value Proposition's Needs): the number's right edge to its text. The number starts `--space-cell` (12px) from the list box's inside left edge and is right-aligned in a column as wide as that list's widest number (the rows share the list's grid, subgrid), so a list under ten has its first digit at 12px, "10." widens the column for every row, and all texts of a list start at one x. (The Needs lists shown on Business Foundation are held 12px in by their own margin, so no extra inset. A card head's number-to-name stays 8px.) |
| `--bullet-size` / `--bullet-gap` | 6px / 12px | a shown (read-only) bulleted list (`.business-engagement-channel-list`: Customer Profiles, relationship types, channels per stage): the dot is drawn (`li::before`), its left edge is the list's left edge, and its right edge is `--bullet-gap` from the text |
| `--card-label-col` / `--side-label-short` | 177px | the label column of every side-label row, so Start date, Group, Team, Responsible and Frequency start their cells at one x on every Business card; `--side-label-short` is this token |
| `--qty-w` | 56px | the Quantity box of the cost row (three digits and the cell's padding) |
| `--tile-radius` | 8px | a tile, a box and the Add Card tile |
| `--card-x-column` | 32px (44px on a channel card) | the x's column of an opened card |
| `--tick-radius` | 4px | the corner of a tick box (the box is 24px) |
| `--field-line` | 1.5 | the line height of the text typed or listed in a control: a field, a list's line, a chip, a menu's option (one owner since 2026-11-14 12:32; prose keeps `--body-line`, 1.6) |
| `--rail-dot-overhang` | 8.5px (12px x (1.45 - 1) / 2 + 4px x 1.45) | how far the lit rail bullet and its bloom reach past its 12px box (`--rail-dot-grow` 1.45, `--rail-bloom-width` 4px): a rail tab's padding and the arrow's gap count it |
| `--chip-height` | 32px | a chip that stands as a row of its own: a channel expense's linked action (#12, #14 ...; owner, 2026-11-14 12:33) |
| `--size-cell-x` | 24px | the in-cell x (`CellRemoveButton`, library BTN.XCL.001.003): its target, the X alone, no box |
| `--hover-lift` | 1.2 | the factor of the glow a tile, a cell or a list row takes under the pointer (18% becomes 21.6%) |
| `--completed-dim` | the Completed ink at 55% over the Completed card ground | the one text tone inside a completed card |
| `--card-body-indent` | 44px | a card body's left edge from the head's left (the arrow's 36px plus 8px) |
| `--side-label-gap` | 12px | a side label to its control |
| `--side-label-short` | 177px | the SHORT side-label column (`BusinessField side`): wide enough for the tick, "Responsible" and its "i" on ONE line (22 + 8 + 100 + 8 + 22 = 160px), so the short labels of a Capability card (Frequency on a Resource, Responsible on a Partner / Provider) stand in one column and their dropdowns start at one x (see "Capability cards: short side labels") |
| `--section-tile-size` | `max(--section-tile-min, (100cqw - (max(--tile-columns, 3) - 1) * 12px) / max(--tile-columns, 3))` | a section tile (see "Section tiles and dialog"): a true square, this wide and this high, in every group of tiles: up to three across a third of the tile box's width less the two gaps (a 3 x 3 set exactly fills it), four or more across the width less its gaps over their number, never under `--section-tile-min` (`100cqw` is the width of `.section-tiles-box`, the tiles' size container; `--tile-columns` is the group's shape, set by `SectionTiles`) |
| `--title-arrow-size` | `calc(var(--font-h2) * 0.75)` (21px) | the long side of the glyph of the `.title-arrow` button of the capability overview menu (`title-arrow.css`); the arrows beside a label or a card title are the label arrow (`--label-arrow-size`), not this one |
| `--section-head-height` | 36px | a section heading's height in a card of tiles (the height the old fold arrow gave it), and the height of the heading-height spacer that lines Offering's Capability's tiles up with Infrastructure's |
| `--section-box-size` | 160px | a founder's closed square box (more than 8 founders): the tile component at this fixed side (the subcategory boxes are sized for two rows instead, "Subcategory boxes") |
| `--open-card-width` | 492px | an opened card (a floating card, a card box pressed): centred, this wide at most; the content is 466px |
| `--founder-marker` / `--founder-inset` | 35.5px / 4.5px | a founder's marker column, left of the content edge, where the head's number, the roles' bullets, the fold arrows and the list numbers hang: a single-digit head number in tabular figures (18.3px) plus the head's 8px gap, so the number stands exactly 8px before the name; the inset keeps the standard 36px fold arrow's 4px focus ring inside the card (its box reaches 12.5px left of its glyph). The card's left padding is `--card-padding` + `--founder-inset` + `--founder-marker` (42.8px) |
| `--founder-cell` / `--founder-card-min` | 108px / 297px | a founder's four cells (Salary, Frequency, Working Hrs, Delivery Hrs), one width: what the Frequency picker's longest option ("Annually") needs with padding, chevron and border; and the width of a founder's card: what its content needs and no more (42.8px + 108 + 12 + 120, the second column as wide as the label "Frequency (i)", + 12 + the 2px border) |
| `--section-tile-line-height` | 1.45 | a tile title (and a box title) that wraps, a tile's list, a founder's list items: room between lines; a single line looks the same |
| `--tile-title-top` / `--tile-list-gap` | 0.25 / 8px | a tile's or box's title row: a quarter of the square (the title's own first line stands 24px from the top, 12px on a dialog's small tile: `--tile-title-gap`, section 9); the gap from a list's bullet to its text (the list itself starts 12px under the title's rule: `--space-card` less the tile's `--tile-lift`, which `TileTitle` measures) |
| `--box-light` / `--box-shade` / `--box-inset` | white 75% / black 18% (dark: white 10% / black 50%; sepia: cream 80% / brown 20%) / `inset 3px 3px 6px light, inset -3px -3px 6px shade` | THE EDGE OF A BOX (page tile, subcategory box, card box, Completed box): no stroke, an inset light from the top left and an inset shade to the bottom right |
| `--plus-fill-saturation` / `--plus-fill-lightness` | 36% / 38% (sepia 34% / 36%; dark 42% / 68%) | the darker tone of a tile's hue: the stroke and the "+" of a "+" box at rest, its fill under the pointer (the hue is the tile's own) |
| `--cell-light` / `--cell-shade` / `--cell-inset` | white 50% / black 12% (dark: white 7% / black 45%; sepia: cream 55% / brown 14%) / `inset 2px 2px 3px light, inset -2px -2px 3px shade` | THE EDGE OF A CELL (an input, a picker, a text box, a name cell, the x) in an opened card of Infrastructure & Capability: the lighter twin of the box's edge, no stroke |
| `--cell-fill` | #faf9f6 (dark and sepia: their surface) | THE CELL'S FILL: a warm off-white in light, so no cell is pure white; its edge stays the soft light and shade (a 1px edge reaching 3:1 was tried and taken away by the owner the same evening) |
| `--icon-size` | 24px | THE ICON IN A BUTTON (owner, 2026-11-10: "The icons in the buttons should all be the same size (24 px)"): the header tools' icons, Export's, the reading mode's options, the funnel's; the calendar and the link symbol were 24 already ; each header icon's box cut round its drawing, so every drawing stands 24px tall with its line, one screen weight, 1.8px (non-scaling) |
| `--shadow-drop` / `--shadow-menu` / `--shadow-tile-lift` | 0 8px 28px -8px / 0 6px 20px -6px / 0 4px 16px -6px, ink 16% | THE DROP SHADOWS: one soft haze each, pulled in by a negative spread so it traces no outline and settles under its box, thinning out within about 16px; `--shadow-drop` under what stands over the page (a dialog, a floating card), `--shadow-menu` under what drops from a control (a dropdown list, a menu, a funnel window, a popover, a toast) and under the header, the page tab and the rails, `--shadow-tile-lift` under a tile grown under the pointer |
| `--card-band-ink` / `--tint-row-band` / `--tint-box-title` | hsl(card hue, `--theme-bg-ink`) / 12% of it / 24% of it | THE CARD'S BANDS: the channel card's saturation and lightness in the card's own hue, on every card and dialog |
| `--floating-scrim` / `--floating-caption-ink` | transparent / per theme | a floating card never darkens the page (a dialog does: `--bg-overlay`); the ink of the box names standing over it (13.1:1 dark, 5.7 light, 6.4 sepia over the worst pixel behind a name) |
| `--section-tile-min` | 120px | the smallest a section tile gets: a group too narrow for three of them wraps to fewer across |
| `--control-xs` | 80px | XS width step |
| `--control-code` | 128px | Code width step |
| `--control-date` | 160px | Date width step |
| `--control-s` | 220px | S width step |
| `--control-m` | 300px | M width step |
| `--control-l` | 420px | L width step |
| `--control-square` | 44px | the square variant; also the one control height |
| `--name-number-columns` | `minmax(0,1fr) min(var(--control-s),40%) 32px` | the Name / Number list |

The numbers in this table are read from the stylesheets and kept equal to them (`npm run docs:values:check`); a row says what the code has, never what an older round said.

---

## 3. Anatomy

### Card head (`.fold-card-head`, every folding card)

```
| arrow 36 | 8 | "1." (own width) | 8 | name (the rest) ............ | x 32 |
             ^ body indent 44px: the number starts where the body starts
```

- The number starts at `--card-body-indent`, the same x as every label and
  control in the body, and is only as wide as its digits.
- The name follows the number at **8px** on every card.
- The arrow, number, name and x sit on the name's **first line**; a long
  name wraps downward and nothing else moves. (A FOLDED Capability card's
  name does not wrap: one line, ellipsis, fixed card height; see Capability cards.)
- **A folded card is one fixed height** (`.fold-card-fixed` on the head, opt-in;
  card-folding.css): its written name on ONE line cut by an ellipsis where it does
  not fit (the full name is its tooltip), its one-line summary under it cut the same
  way, so every folded card of a kind has the one height (a card with no summary:
  the head only); open, the name box wraps and grows as before. It applies to the
  flat card lists only: Business Foundation's Profile, Competitor and Offering cards;
  Infrastructure & Capability's Resource, Activity, Partnership & Provider and
  Infrastructure cards; Brand's colour and typeface cards; the Legal Entity's
  requirement and expense cards; the Founders Profile's cards; the cards of the
  Financial pages (`BusinessCard`). NOT Customer Engagement (tables), Strategy
  (nested boxes), Page 1's Segment card, a section card (its title carries an "i")
  or Brand's Inventory. The probe checks it ("a folded card's name / summary is one
  ellipsis-cut line", "folded cards of a kind are equally high").
- **A folded card's head is the open card's head.** The written name
  (`.strategy-name-static`) takes the name box's own line, padding and
  (unseen) edge, so folding moves nothing but what is under the head.
- A folded card's one-line summary sits under the name, from the name's x
  (a Profile card shows none: number, name and x only).
- **A group that folds inside a card** (a Profile's Description,
  Motivations and Needs; a Competitor's Description and SWOT
  Analysis; an Offering's Description and Terms & Service): the app's fold arrow before its
  title, hanging in the card's arrow column (the title stays at the
  body's edge), the row the arrow's 36px high; open until folded, and
  folded it shows only its title's line.
- **Head to body: 12px** (`--space-field`), from the head's lowest edge to
  the first thing under it, on every card (an Objective's Start box too:
  its Description label is set down onto the Start box's centre line
  instead of the Start box being lifted into the head's gap).
- **Head buttons** (a Strategy card's filter and x): 12px (`--space-cell`)
  after the name box, 12px apart, the last at the card's 12px side margin.
- **A grip and no fold arrow** (a Metric box, a Situation Theme box): the
  number's first character follows the grip at the head's 8px gap (no
  empty list-number column), and the body starts at the number's x
  (number-left = body-left), 32px in (the grip's 24px and 8px).

### Content starts at the title (numbered or not)

Under every head with a fold arrow, the content -- text, labels, cells,
boxes, tables, nested cards -- starts at the left edge of the title's
text: its NUMBER where it has one ("1. Segment", "2. Customer
Relationship", "1. Offering -", "1. Goal", "1. Metric"), its first letter
where it has none ("Sales Forecast", "Electricity", "Establishment").
Fold arrows and drag grips are not content: they stand in front of that
edge. Where the block sits at its card's body edge, its arrow hangs in the
card's arrow column (Customer Engagement's sections, Infrastructure's
sections); where there is no arrow column before it, its
content moves in past the arrow and its gap instead (Demand &
Conversion, Infrastructure's subsections, Financial Foundation's cost
groups). The card rule (number-left = body-left) is this rule for cards.
Named exceptions: a Brand colour or typeface card's body (under its name
box); a Competitor's or Offering's Type (under its name box, ending at the
name box's right edge rather than starting at its left). A table's
band rows are set by their cells (the Customer Relationship's Offering
blocks, below). The check holds every head on every page.

### Card body

- Everything starts at the body's left edge (`--card-body-indent`).
- **One vertical rhythm** in every card and section box: a label 6px
  (`--space-label`) above its control; a control, list row or add button
  12px (`--space-field`) above what follows it; two groups divided by a
  rule, 12px either side of it (a group gap of 24px). A side label is part
  of its control's row. The same everywhere: a head stands 12px over the first label or cell, 12px under the last (a tinted band has 12px over and under what it holds). A tick box's row is as tall as its box (24px; 2026-11-19 reconciliation), so the gap between two tick boxes is 12px box to box, as between two cells.
- **Gaps are 12px (the common one) or 6px (vertically connected: a label and its cell, a name over its page over its text)**; a card is checked for them when it is made (the colour picker's parts 12px, a cell and its small label 6px).
- **Vertical fields**: a label over its cell stand 6px apart, everywhere (measured: 85 of 85 label-over-cell pairs on the nine pages, and the founder's card); a label beside its cell stands 12px from it.
- **A frozen field's hint** ("Choose a country first.", FrozenUntil) stands
  on the label's own line, right of the label and its "i", in the small
  hint style, vertically centred on that line -- never on a line of its own,
  so the label stays 6px above its control. Legal Entity labels only
  (`.legal-entity-label .field-status-line`).
- The Legal Entity's consumption-tax row (Tax & Business Registration) is
  drawn only once a country is chosen that has a profile with its own
  consumption tax; none before, none for an unprofiled country.
- A card's two columns (`.card-field-pairs-grid`): `repeat(2, minmax(0, S))`
  `--space-cell` (12px) apart, one column under 300px.
- An optional field (tick + growing box) has its tick at the body edge and
  its box under it, from the same edge.
- The Workspace card's optional **Deposit** (shown at Status Costs): the same
  `OptionalField` tick (label gap 6px), its body one row (`.business-capability-price
  .business-capability-deposit`): the amount box at the Code step (128px), then the
  card's currency as plain text 8px after it, on one centre line.

### Cards and section boxes

Every card and every section box (Legal Entity's numbered sections, the
Financial Foundation's need and offering-cost cards, Strategy boxes) has
the same inner side margin: `--card-padding` (12px).

---

## 4. Sizes: the closed set

### Widths (six steps, plus "its column")

| Step | Width | Holds | Used for |
|---|---|---|---|
| XS | 80px | 3-4 digits, or "33.333" | Quantity, a person's hours, calendar weeks (`.delivery-number`), a founder's Ownership % |
| Code | 128px | 10 characters ("10001-1234" 98px, ten digits 107px, "£250,000") | a founder's Capital, the activity code, the Workspace's Deposit amount |
| Date | 160px (`--control-date`) | a whole date with its calendar button, or a month and year with its calendar button ("Sept 2027") | every date cell and month cell in every card, wherever it stands |
| S | 220px | 18 characters | numbers and amounts in fields, short pickers (Status, Role, Responsible), the Number column of a Name / Number list |
| M | 300px | an amount with its currency | price + currency pairs |
| L | 420px | a long option | pickers whose longest option needs it (OptionPicker picks S, M or L itself) |
| Column | its column | anything | text fields and pickers that fill their column (Country, State / Region, the Address) |

**The hug exception.** A picker marked to hug (`hug`,
`data-size="hug"`: a Competitor's and an Offering's Type beside their
label, a Legal expense's Frequency under its label) is exactly as
wide as its longest option (or placeholder) with its padding, chevron and
border, rather than the step that holds it. The check measures that
width.

The two-column grids are built from these: a pair row is two S columns, a
Name / Number list is "the rest" plus S. A width outside this list fails
the check, except a control that fills its column, its container, or one
track of the grid it stands in.

### The page's sizes (one set for every page)

The owner asked on 2026-11-23 (19:48, 20:58 and 21:05 UTC+2): "Can we be consistent and use the same sizes?"; "The tiles should always be square … The frame chapter should hug its tiles … I do not want the user, specially on landscape size screens to scroll down"; "Looks good, let's refine it adjusting the text size".

This table owns the sizes of a page of tiles. Every System page and every Capability page takes them, and no page sets its own.

**The page follows its tiles.** The window gives one master side. The page is drawn as wide as two chapters of that side plus the rails, and is centred in the window. Inside the page everything stands at the left.

The code that sets these sizes:
- `masterSide` and `tilesPageWidth` (SectionTileGeometry.tsx) work out the master side and the page width.
- `setTilesPage` gives the page its width through `--tiles-page-width` and `data-tiles-page` on `.conversation-page`.
- `SectionTiles` and the Capability pages draw the tiles at that size.
- `arrowLevel` places the arrows from it.

| What | Size | Where it is set |
|---|---|---|
| Master tile side | Always square. The smaller of two limits: the height (two rows between the page title and the footer, with their 12px gaps) and the width (four tiles of the widest page the window allows, 1400px at most). 150px at the least. Examples: 301px at 1920 x 950, 211px at 1440 x 770, 191px at 1536 x 730, 150px at 1366 x 640 (the visible area). | `masterSide` |
| Page width (the title rule) | 2 x (2 x side + 12 + 26) + 12 + 2 x 54: two chapters, the gap between them and the rails' room. It never exceeds the widest page the window allows. The page is centred in the window. | `tilesPageWidth`, `setTilesPage`, bullet-rail.css |
| Rail room at each end of the rule | 54px (the dots-only tab) | `--rail-inset`, bullet-rail.css |
| A frame's padding and edge | 12px + 1px on all four sides: the frame hugs its tiles | `--card-padding`, global.css; `SectionTiles` (hug, `data-hug-pair`) |
| Between two chapters, between two tiles, under a frame's head | 12px | `--space-card`, global.css |
| A frame's head (its title) | 36px | `--section-head-height`, global.css |
| The page's head | The header 64px (`--app-header-height`; 56px on a phone in landscape); 24px from the header to the title's capitals, 12px from the letters' foot to the underline, 12px from the underline to the chapter; the page's symbol level with the capitals (owner, 2026-11-24 00:56 UTC+2) | global.css, 01-interview.css |
| First tile row's top | The rule's bottom + 12 + 61 (edge and padding 13, head 36, 12) | `masterSide`, `arrowLevel` |
| A lone chapter | Keeps the two-chapter page width. It hugs its tiles at the page's left, against the left rail. | `SectionTiles` |
| The page title | One line. On a wide screen it steps down 2px at a time to 24px at the least, and its line keeps its height. | `InterviewHeader` |
| The type | One fluid scale in `rem` (global.css): h1 24 to 36, h2 20 to 28, h3 18 to 24, body 16 to 18, small 13 to 14, moving with the window (the responsive layout, docs/proposals/responsive-layout-2026-11-23.md, section 4.2). A master tile's title and list are the body step; a word too wide for its tile takes the small step (`data-title-small`); a chapter's heading steps down to stand on one line (`fitHeading`) | global.css, `TileTitle`, `fitHeading` |
| The footer's band | One button high (64px), with Back, "+ Add Segment", Inventory and Completed in it. At least 12px between the chapter's foot and the band. "+ Add Segment" starts on the chapter's left edge. | `--tiles-page-width` page foot, bullet-rail.css |
| Rails and arrows | Together, inside the page, under the title rule, at its ends. Each arrow is centred on its rail. The page's symbol is on the left rail's line. The right rail stands against its chapter. | bullet-rail.css |
| Arrow level | The first row's top + side + 6 (the middle of the gap between the rows) | `arrowLevel` |
| A text tile's last line | A whole line, ending in "…" where the text runs on; a list ends at a whole item | `TileList` (line-clamp) |
| A tile with too little room | A list or a text cut to less than two lines is not shown: the tile keeps its title and its symbol (owner, 2026-11-23 21:49 UTC+2) | `TileList` (`data-no-room`) |
| The page tags' place | Always 12px under the top of the chapter they stand against (a Segment's chapter included), measured again when the page title settles | `useRailTab` |
| A rail too long for the window | Its bullets lie in a row in the footer's band, by its middle (the Pages rail before it, the Sections rail after it), without a tab; its arrow keeps its place (owner, 2026-11-23 21:49 UTC+2: "place them horizontally at the footing") | `BulletRail` (`data-across`), bullet-rail.css |
| The tiers | Side by side, no scroll: a landscape window 900px wide and 640px tall or more. Stacked (one chapter a row, the page scrolls, tiles at most 260px, the rails in the footer's band): under 900px, a portrait window or under 640px tall. A phone turned to landscape (under 480px tall): header 56px, band 48px, four tiles a row. The safe areas pad the bands (`env(safe-area-inset-*)`, `viewport-fit=cover`) | `VERTICAL_QUERY`, `ROW_OF_FOUR_QUERY` (useVerticalLayout.ts), bullet-rail.css, app-header.css |
| A Resources tile short of room | Its nine categories as 24px symbols in a 3 x 3 grid, as many whole rows as fit, each named in its tooltip; under one row, the title alone (owner, 2026-11-24 00:21 UTC+2) | `TileList` (`data-symbols`), data/resourceIcons.ts |
| A touch screen (a tablet) | Its rails always in the footer's band, its page keeping no rails' room (`--rail-inset: 0`); side by side, Inventory, Completed and "+ Add Segment" stand on the band, 6px above it; Back stands first in the page title's row on any window wider than a phone's where the band holds the rails (owner, 2026-11-24 00:21 UTC+2) | `BAND_QUERY` / `railsInBand` (useVerticalLayout.ts), `AppFooter`, bullet-rail.css |
| The page title | "System — Page" on one row; where it does not fit, the page's symbol and name alone (`.interview-header-page-only`), then steps down to 24px at the least (owner, 2026-11-24 00:21 UTC+2) | `InterviewHeader` |
| A tile's content | On the tile's midline, the same line on every tile of a row (owner, 2026-11-24 04:24 UTC+2: "The blue lines are the mid points"), never above 12px under the title's rule nor past the room's foot (or its +), its items up to 8px further apart where there is room; where three lines do not fit, the title alone, centred in the tile (owner, 2026-11-24 03:14 UTC+2) | `TileList` (`--list-centre`, `--list-air`, `data-no-room`, `--title-centre`) |
| Titles on a page | One size for every master tile's title on every page of a window: the large size, a long title wrapping onto its lines; the small size on every page only where the app's longest title word (RELATIONSHIP) would not stand whole in a tile (owner, 2026-11-24 03:14 and 04:54 UTC+2: "All the tiles' titles should have the same size!"); one size for a page's chapter headings (the smallest a heading steps down to) | `TileTitle` (`data-titles-small`), `unifyHeadings` |
| The Workbook and the Handbook | Side by side, as wide as a page of tiles at the window, their rule as long; with a mouse, the rail's tab inside the rule's left end, the panel 54px in from both ends, the symbol over the bullets (owner, 2026-11-24 03:14 UTC+2) | `Workbook.tsx` (`setTilesPage`), glossary.css |
| The Landing page | The name's cell ends with the Organisations' cells, before their arrow; on a phone, Add, Export, Import and Reload are their symbols alone in the header buttons' box (owner, 2026-11-24 03:14 UTC+2) | landing.css |
| A tile under the pointer | Grows 4% about its centre, inside the 12px gap, never over a neighbour (owner, 2026-11-24 04:47 UTC+2) | 04-tile-slot-and-floating-card.css |
| The page's symbol | Its ink as tall as the title's capitals (0.727 of the title's size) and its middle on theirs, on every page: the square sized from the drawing's own ink (--icon-box), its 36px column centred on the rail's bullets (owner, 2026-11-24 05:18 and 05:27 UTC+2) | `InterviewHeader` (ink measured), 01-interview.css, bullet-rail.css |
| A System's tile on its Capability's page | Its title and symbol alone, centred; what it holds is on its Introduction (owner, 2026-11-24 05:32 UTC+2) | `SystemSelection`, workspace.css |
| Spacing between list lines | Up to 8px, one amount for every list of a page or dialog -- the least any list allows, 0 where one is full -- and never cutting a line (owner, 2026-11-24 04:28 and 05:16 UTC+2) | `TileList` (`data-air-allows`, `--list-air`) |
| One place per part per screen size | The owner's table of 2026-11-25: the page title in the header centred, the System's symbol and the page's name (the Handbook and Workbook the open tab's name); rails at the sides wherever the chapters stand side by side (a laptop never has its rail in the footer), the band where they stack; Back left of the page at the first card's top on a tablet in portrait, first in the title's row on a phone in portrait; dialogs as long as their tiles, never cut (2026-11-26); Inventory and Completed one master tile wide under the first two tiles; tables and documents as wide as the window allows | app-header.css, bullet-rail.css, back-button.css, AppFooter, BusinessCapabilityEditor, Workbook, capability-overview.css |
| A dialog tile's symbol | Where a dialog's titles do not fit three across on a tablet, or on any touch screen (`data-title-symbols`): with room for the symbol 12px clear of the "+" across, the title at the top (two lines' room, small size, the weight's tracking) and the symbol centred under it; with less, the symbol alone, centred across in the room above the "+", its name in the tooltip (a mouse only). The symbol 48px, Material's display size, never nearer the "+" than 12px (owner, 2026-11-26 16:02 and 17:04 UTC+2) | `TileTitle` (`data-symbol-layout`, `--title-symbol-size`), 01-tiles-and-dialog.css |
| A touch screen | Read from the input, `(pointer: coarse)`; a laptop with a touch screen answers as its mouse. Every small control presses over 44px (`--press-area`; 24px with a mouse), its drawing unchanged; a rail's bullets 44px across, 24px down (their pitch); a tile is one target, its list lines read in its dialog; no hover look (every `:hover` behind `(hover: hover)`, one build step); a dialog at least 640px wide where the page has the room, its tiles three across (owner, 2026-11-26 17:04 UTC+2) | global.css (`--press-area`), tools/scripts/vite/hover-only-with-mouse.mjs, `dialogFrame`, `TileGrid`, `TileTitle` |
| A card's length (floating cards and dialogs) | As long as what it holds, a dialog never shorter than the card area it opens over (owner, 2026-11-26 20:38 UTC+2: "They should cover the cards behind them"): it ends 12px under its last field or tile (its own padding; a dialog's hidden foot and its tiles' hover room take none), and one longer than the window runs on past its foot, the window scrolling it, never cut; its end in its last band's colour (owner, 2026-11-26 18:01 UTC+2: "I meant that it should not be cut, but continue after the screen's end. The floated cards, should have specific size (12 px) as margins"; 18:01, "All the cards have 12 px margin"); the page-wide dialogs (Inventory, Completed) keep their frame and scroll inside | `FloatingCards` (`fullLength`), cardEndGround.ts |
| A phone | Its layers stand alone under the header, as a phone app's full-screen dialogs: a dialog (the Inventory too) and a floating card 12px under the app header, over a plain screen -- the page's ground, not its shade --, a card hiding the dialog under it; a side label above its field, full width; the Landing's list at the top; Back's arrow 24px (owner, 2026-11-26 18:12 UTC+2: "Work on the mobile version, it needs far improvement. This is how the other apps look like?") | `dialogFrame`, `FloatingCards`, 07-floating-cards-and-page-layout.css, 18-strategy-metrics-and-side-labels.css, landing.css, 01-interview.css |
| A phone's page of chapters | In portrait, a page of two chapters or more, each of four tiles at the most, shows two chapters at once: the master tiles square, 2 x 2, centred in the chapter, as large as lets two chapters stand between the page title's rule and the footer's band, never under 96px and never so small that a title's longest word is cut (Brand's "COMMUNICATION" keeps 160px); a hairline in the ink's 40% from side to side opens every chapter after the first (owner, 2026-11-26 20:26 UTC+2, his drawing) | `SectionTiles` (`fit`), 07-floating-cards-and-page-layout.css |
| One rule for a screen's tiles | Where one master tile of a page or a dialog is short of room (its list cut, or a grid of symbols missing rows), every one there shows its title alone, its rule on the tile's middle (owner, 2026-11-24 10:12 UTC+2) | `TileList` (`data-room-short`) |
| A dialog's titles that do not fit | Two across, and a Resource category's title as its symbol (32px), its name in the tooltip (owner, 2026-11-24 10:12 UTC+2) | `TileTitle` (`data-title-symbols`), 01-tiles-and-dialog.css |
| The page title in the header | A window 601px wide or more that is 700px tall or less, 1100px wide or less, or a touch screen: the title, its symbol in the header, the TILES word hidden, the Organisation's square mark before the buttons; on a small laptop the rails, arrows and Back in the footer's band (owner, 2026-11-24 10:12 UTC+2) | app-header.css, bullet-rail.css, `BAND_QUERY` |
| A dialog's height | It ends 12px above the footer; what does not fit scrolls in its body (owner, 2026-11-24 10:12 UTC+2) | 01-tiles-and-dialog.css |
| A phone's page title | `--font-h2`, one line (owner, 2026-11-24 03:14 UTC+2) | 01-interview.css |
| A phone in landscape | The Tiles logo without its word; the page's title, symbol and Back in the header on one line (`--font-h2`; the page's name alone, then an ellipsis); no footer band, no bullets, the arrows at the bottom corners; the Organisation's square Brand icon (else its logo) at the header's right end; the header's sides clear of the notch, `max(28px, env(safe-area-inset-*))` (owner, 2026-11-24 03:14 UTC+2) | app-header.css, `BrandMark` |

The layout check measures these sizes:
- `checkLandscapePages`: no page of tiles scrolls at 1920 x 950, 1536 x 730, 1440 x 770 or 1366 x 640, and every master tile is square and one size.
- `checkCapabilityArrows`: the Capability pages' arrows stand between the rows.
- The probe's tile rules.

### Heights

- **One single-line control height: 45px** (drawn 44.33px, the 1px border
  snapped by the browser). Inputs, pickers, date inputs.
- **Square** (`--control-square`, 44 x 44): a 2-3 digit value with its unit
  after it; the Text Hierarchy's px boxes. Value centred.
- **Text boxes** grow with their text and are never resized by hand:
  at least **2 lines** for a written answer, **2 lines** for the Legal
  Entity's Main Activity and Address (side by side, one column each, the
  same size), **5 lines** for an image's description (Brand Inventory).
  One-line text boxes (a card's name, a founder's name, a registration's
  name) wrap and grow instead of clipping.

### Compact items

- The x (remove) and the "+" (small): 32 x 32, on every row of a list in one column. The calendar button inside a date cell is the same box.
- A tick box: 24 x 24, corners 4px (`--tick-radius`).
- The fold arrow: 36 x 36. (The exception is the label arrow beside a label or a title: a triangle of the label's own size with a hit area never under 24px; see "The label arrow" and "Section tiles and dialog".)
### The caps title

Every capitalised card kind word and group heading takes ONE calm style,
the card-head kind word's ("1. SEGMENT"): the body size, weight 600, the
0.05em tracking, `--caps-title-color` (the muted text colour), in
capitals on screen only (`text-transform`: the words read aloud and
every accessible name unchanged). Business Foundation's "1. SEGMENT",
PROFILES, COMPETITORS, OFFERINGS and VALUE PROPOSITION; Customer
Engagement's "1. SEGMENT", CUSTOMER CHANNEL and CUSTOMER RELATIONSHIP;
Strategy's THEME, GOAL, OBJECTIVE, METRIC and INITIATIVES wherever they
name a card. No bolder, full-ink variant. `--caps-title-color` holds
4.5:1 or more on every ground a caps title stands on, in light, sepia and
dark (5.8:1 at the least: sepia, on a Goal card and the Value Proposition
panel); a ground that fails darkens the token, never one title. A button
inside a heading (an "i", "Expand all") keeps its own case. The Roadmap
and the documents are unchanged.

### Card-list add buttons (an explicit list)

These add buttons span their list of cards, from the cards' left edge to
their right (the list's content width while it is empty), label centred
(`.card-list-add`, as `.legal-entity-list-add`):

- Legal Entity: "+ Add legal requirement" (5), "+ Add expense" (6).
- Business Foundation: "+ Add Profile", "+ Add Competitor", "+ Add
  Offering".

Every other add button keeps its own rule: as wide as its label (Goal,
Objective, Metric, Theme, Segment, Resource, Activity, Cost, Funding
Source...), the whole cell in a table cell, control to x under a
[control][x] row. The check holds only the buttons listed here.

---

## 5. Alignment

### The in-row cell gap

Cells side by side in one row inside a card are `--space-cell` (12px)
apart, everywhere: field grids, pair rows, Name / Number lists, tables.
Gaps between cards, sections and page columns (`--space-card`,
`--space-column`) are not cell gaps, but they are the same 12px: **every
gap between cells, cards, boxes and columns, side by side or stacked, is
12px.** The only 24px left between two blocks is a group gap across a
dividing rule (12px either side of it); the Roadmap, Capability, Financial
and Business Foundation's lower sections keep their own page layouts.

### The mid-line rule

- **Text beside a control** has its first line centred on the control's
  centre line (a side label, "px" after a box, the words of a fixed list
  row). A second line hangs below.
- **A tick box** is centred on its words' first line.
- **An "i"** follows its words' last word at 6px (`--info-gap`, one rule for every "i"), on that line's centre
  (glued to the last word, `.info-note-glue`); after a badge, on the
  badge's centre.
- Exception: a Text Hierarchy level's row (see "Declared exceptions") centres the tick, the
  name's whole block and the px box on the row's centre line.

### Connected components align as one

Components that belong together align as one, wherever they stand: side by side in one row, or joined to one field
(a label and its cell; a cell and its x, plus, calendar, link symbol or unit word; a tick and its words; a price with its
currency and its frequency; a date cell and its calendar).

1. **One centre line.** Their centres are on the same horizontal line, whatever their heights (the Responsible row: the tick, its words, the "i" and the dropdown cell share one middle, within 1px): a cell is 44px, a box button
   32px, a link symbol 24px, a tick 24px, an "i" 24px; each is centred on the row's centre line, and a text on its first line.
2. **Same height, same top.** Components of the same height in a row (the cells: 44px) share one top edge and one bottom edge.
3. **One gap.** The gap between connected components is the in-row gap (12px); an "i" and a label gap are 6px (see above).
4. **Column lines.** A connected component starts and ends on the card's column lines: the control column starts at one x in
   every row, and a row that holds several cells splits that width between them.
5. **No special case.** A component placed beside another follows this rule without an exception; a new component is
   checked against it before it is placed (docs/design/library/01-library-system.md).

The check: `node tools/scripts/catalogue/align-check.mjs` opens every master tile and the first two cards of each view, and lists
every pair of controls side by side whose centres differ by more than 1px, or whose tops differ when their heights are equal.
It must list none.

---

## 6. Shared components and states

The owner's rules on the components every card shares, then the reactions of each kind, the picker options, tracking and the label arrow.

### Click areas and focus (WCAG 2.2 AA)

- **A button takes the click across at least 24 by 24px** (2.5.8 Target Size): where it is smaller, an invisible box of 24px centred on it takes the click and nothing is drawn (the rail dots, `bullet-rail.css`; the Back arrow, `back-button.css`), or it stands clear of every other button by a 24px circle round its middle. The layout probe checks every small button (rule E). Known and kept until their pages become Tiles (owner, 2026-11-11): the tiles' list links (20px rows) and the page-3 "show only what … is connected to" buttons (12px); for these, centres 24px apart (the spacing exception) or one target with their label.
- **A field reached by the keyboard is never hidden under the sticky title** (2.4.11 Focus Not Obscured): when a field takes the focus under the title band, the page scrolls on until it stands 8px below the band, measured at that moment (`src/components/interview/stickyFocus.ts`); a field already clear, or inside a dialog or a floating card, is left alone. The layout probe checks it (rule F).

### The shared components (rules 6 to 20)

The rule numbers start at 6 and are kept: other places cite them.

Rules 6 and 7 are about a dropdown alone in a row and fields that pair; 8 to 20 about what every kind of field, card and tile has in common.

6. **A dropdown alone in its row takes the row.** When a card's row holds one field and it is a dropdown, its right side is the available space's: 12px after the x's left side, which is the name cell's right edge, or, on a card with no x, 56px (12px gap + the 32px x + 12px side gap) from the card's right edge. It does not size to its longest option. (Built in `OptionPicker`: a hugging picker with no other control in its row is "full".)
7. **A field with the label above its holder usually has a partner beside it.** The common pairs: Working Hours with Delivery Hours; Salary, Cost, Price or Expense with its Frequency. A currency dropdown, where there is one, comes after the amount; a quantity (QTY) cell, where there is one, comes before it. (Applying it card by card is still to do: section 20.)
8. **The common fields are one thing everywhere.** A field kind (text cell, dropdown, date or month cell, number, tick, colour, x, plus, i) has one style, behaviour, size, shape and layout in every system, page and card; a difference is a defect, and is fixed in the library and the shared component, not in the page.

9. **One Responsible field** (`ResponsibleField`, `BusinessCardFields.tsx`): the label with its tick on the side-label row and the founders' dropdown, on an Activity, a Partner or Supplier, a Legal expense and a Strategy Objective alike. What a founder is responsible for is read back from them into the founder's Responsibilities: Activities, Partners & Suppliers and Legal expenses (an Objective's owner is on the Strategy page and is not read back yet). A line says what it is with its letter (A. Activity, P. Partner, S. Supplier, L. Legal). On a Strategy Objective whose Start and End no longer fit on one row, the two stand one over the other, their cells at one x, and the Responsible stands beside them with its tick and word one cell high on the Start's line and its dropdown level with the End; narrower still, it stands under them, its label still over its cell (owner, 2026-11-12; the container `objective-top`, the section tiles stylesheet).
10. **A card opened from another keeps its own colour**: an Offering's card opened from a founder is the Offering's, a channel's card the channel's teal (`FloatingItem.hue`).
11. **An incomplete card lights its tile in every system** (`SystemCardCompleteness.ts`, `CardCompleteness.ts`): a card with something written but not what it needs makes its tile flash red; a card just made and still open, or one holding nothing, is not flagged.
12. **While a payment plan is ticked** the card has no Start or End date: the plan's dates are the card's (the earliest and the latest payment); an Instalments plan writes its first and last dates and the ones between follow.
13. **The active (typed-in) text holder shows one effect**: the primary edge and a ring of 3px at 70% (`--focus-ring-mix`, 2026-11-07) (a text box, a list's box, a date or month cell, a note, a settings field, a name in a frame). **One ring** for every cell (`cell-focus.css`): a box that holds a field (a date cell, the colour cell, a numbered list) draws it round the box with the cell's corners, and the field inside draws none. The pointer on such a box gives it the glow. **The Search box (Ctrl+K) is such a cell**: the Settings' cells' look (`cell-focus.css`: no stroke at rest, the recessed field on `--bg-surface`, the glow under the pointer, this ring) at a card cell's size and type (7.5px 10px, `--font-body`), in a dialog on the cells' tinted ground (`--bg-card-tint`) with a floating card's edge (`--box-border`), corners (`--radius-lg`) and drop shadow (`search.css`), in light, dark and sepia.
14. **A completed card is an ordinary card in four greys.** The ground (`--bg-completed-card`), the cells' ground (`--bg-completed-ground`), the line (`--line-completed`) and the text (`--completed-dim`, the card's own ink only on the Completed row). Its cells keep the shapes of an open card (recessed text cells, pickers with their chevron, the "+", x and calendar buttons, the tick boxes); "cannot be changed" is told by the cursor, not by flattening them. This is the one case where a disabled cell is not flat.
15. **What stands with a card takes the card's tone.** The tab, its bullets, the carousel arrows and the halo of the tile that opened it are measured from the open card (its ground, its edge, its ink); a completed card gives them the Completed greys (`COMPLETED_CARD` in `SectionTiles.tsx`). A new kind of companion is drawn from the same measure, never from the tile's hue.
16. **The halo and the underline follow the card.** While the arrows step through cards, the halo is on the tile or box that holds the card now shown (a founder's card is held as `founder-` and its id: both are looked for), and the line of the tile's list that opens it is bold and underlined; a card with no line of its own leaves them where the card was opened from. **The dialog follows too, and a shared tile is found by its line** (owner, 2026-11-16 19:18): the marked tile scrolls into view behind the card (smoothly, the least it can; at once where motion is reduced); where one tile holds several cards (one subchannel, each Offering's card: `lineCards` names each linked line by its card), the tile is haloed and the card's own line is marked, bold and underlined. **A table that holds the card frames its row** (owner, 2026-11-16 23:58 and 2026-11-17 00:16, "the whole row should be highlighted"): a row named by its card (`data-card-row`, a Journey Stage's subchannel) is framed whole, edge to edge across its cells (`data-opener-cell`; a cell that spans other cards' rows, the Channel of several subchannels, stays out), 4px in the links' blue (`--text-primary`, as `.jump-highlight` frames a link's target), drawn just inside the cells; its text keeps its own weight. The dialog scrolls to it as the arrows step.
17. **"No category" opens a card.** The line of a tile's list that names the cards still to be filed opens the first of them, floating; the arrows go through the others.
18. **A divider line runs from edge to edge of its card**: across every column of the card's grid, the x column too (`.business-capability-card-rule`).
19. **A milestone's name cell ends where every cell ends; its "+" (or x) stands in the x's column**, level with the head's x. A milestone with no date of its own takes the card's: the first the Start date, the last the End date, the ones between spread evenly (`paymentPlanLines`); a date typed stays.
20. **The blocks of a master tile** (a colour, an image) are tiles with one corner, 6px; under the pointer a block grows 10% inwards and keeps its inset light and shade (the drop shadow is added to them, nothing is lost). A typeface on the Typography tile is a text: under the pointer, and while its card is open, it is underlined and has no halo or block. The colour blocks stand in the order of the Colours card (the A colours first, then the B colours), three across.

**The rules every control and cell shares** (one component, token or class each):

- **Every control that is pressed has a tooltip in one place and one wording**, "Click to ..." (design Interface 8).
- **A tooltip opened by the pointer stands a line above the hovered text** (6px over its top, its left edge on the control's left edge), never over it; under it only where the window has no room above. **Every tooltip is the one `TooltipBox`, in that one place** -- an icon's, a rail bullet's name, an action symbol's (owner, 2026-11-21 16:02 UTC+2: "Tooltips should have the same style, and use the same rules about how and where they appear"; design Interface 8).
- **A card's title**: a typed name is the cell's value (18px, regular, the order number inside the cell); a name the app wrote (a channel's, a founder's, a delivery card's) is 18px semi-bold, the strong text colour, on the number's line. Never larger than the frame's own headings (a card's title is not a heading).
- **Which way a cell aligns**: text, names, descriptions, dates, links and the chosen value of a picker align LEFT; numbers (amounts, quantities, hours, percentages) align RIGHT, the cell ending where the card's cells end; tile titles and frame headings are CENTRED. One exception, by design: the Strategy System's Start and End month pickers read from the right so two stacked cells end on the same letter. A value the app placed (not typed or picked) takes the alignment of the cell it stands in.
- **A label over its control or beside it**: over, with 6px, for a wide control (a text, a list, a tick list, a field under "Links"); beside, in the label column (as wide as the widest label), for a single short cell.
- **A set of choices is tick boxes** (one or more of these: a channel's Offerings, "Linked to"), listed one per line, "All" first; a dropdown is for one value.
- **One label look, for every label**: a field's label, a tick's label (a tick field, an optional field's tick, a switch, a "Completed" tick) and a Legal Entity card's label are the same: regular, the body size, the card's label colour (`--label-color`); so are an optional field's ("Links", "Description") and a channel card's part labels ("Offerings", "Actions", "Expenses") (owner's list, 2026-10-20: "every tick's label is the field label"; 2026-11-19 reconciliation). A tick's text never takes the text colour a place gave it (`cell-focus.css`: `label:has(> .brand-tick-box)`). A Legal Entity card, floating or in its dialog, has the label colour of the Legal Entity page's frames (`--legal-label-hue`, the hue of its work type). Left as they are: the muted count beside a label (`field-label-muted`) and a typeface specimen's size label.
- **The pointer on a field is the glow only** (R1: 4px at 18%, the edge never changes, the recess kept); the primary edge is the ring's (R2). One rule for text boxes, text areas, name cells, pickers, number, date, month and colour cells, notes and lists.
- **Every cell in a pop-up has every reaction.** A pop-up inside a box that draws the ring (the colour picker in the colour cell) is not part of that box (`.date-cell-field > input`). The colour box (swatch) has its own halo and ring.
- **No wireframe at rest**: a list, an image's holder and the holder of an image's name and note have no stroke or dashes, drawn as a text box (recessed); a holder with a picture is flat; hover is the glow, focus the ring. **The Global Settings' cells are the Systems' cells** (recessed, no stroke, on the card ground).
- **A card that holds nested boxes answers the pointer as they do** (the Text Hierarchy's card as a Goal, an Objective, a Metric: edge and glow); its rows do nothing of their own.
- **A labelled button never changes size under the pointer** (bold by the thickening drawn on its letters; tracking stays). **There is one Back** (`BackButton`), and it narrows the same way everywhere: a rail's Back keeps only its arrow where the tab has under 160px beside the card (`carousel-rail-named` is absent), the page footer's Back keeps only its arrow where its word would stand over the page's card or what stands in the footer's band, measured with the word shown (`AppFooter`, `.workspace-footer-back-short`; owner, 2026-11-22 18:58 UTC+2: "If there is nothing to hide the BACK (word) at the footing, there is no reason to hide the word"; rule | was | now: the word hidden below 1700px whatever stood beside it | measured), so the word never stands over the page or is cut at the window's edge; the button keeps its name (`aria-label`) and, as an icon, its tooltip ("Click to go back"). **Refresh is a header-tool button** with an "i" holding its note, 12px under the text.
- **Lines are seen**: the divider, rule, hairline and heading-rule tokens and the System cards' rules hold 3:1 on their grounds in every reading mode (`tokens.ts` pairs).
- **A wired button has no fill at rest** (the "i", "+", x, an "Add ..." button): transparent, so it takes the colour behind it; its own fill returns under the pointer or when open (`section-tiles.css`, "GLOBAL: A WIRED BUTTON").
- **A status pill (Total Ownership) has no fill**: transparent like a wired button; its state is told by line, ink and a soft glow in its line colour (green when met, red when short or over; owner, 2026-11-09) (`section-tiles.css`, "GLOBAL: A STATUS PILL").
- **Hovering never moves anything**: a hover or focus reaction changes colour, shadow, text-shadow, outline or a transform (which takes no room) -- never a margin, padding, border width, size, font weight or letter spacing of something in a card. (Checked by hovering every control of an open card and comparing the boxes.)
- **A tick box under the pointer keeps its inner recess and takes the field's glow ring round it** (4px at 18%, `--choice-glow`, as the reactions table below says; ticked, the box is flat and keeps the glow).
- **Shared components** (every place that needs one uses it, never its own markup): Back (`BackButton`), remove (`RemoveButton`), funnel (`FunnelButton`), add (`AddButton`: wide, box, tile), number cell (`NumberCell`), tick box (`TickBox`), field label (`FieldLabel`), date (`DateCellInput`), month (`MonthYearPicker`), text link (`TextLink`), dropdown (`OptionPicker`), optional note (`OptionalNote`, one note class: `business-foundation-note`; the Note that ends each part of a card of questions), radio of short options (`FittingRadioChoice`: one row or one column, never a wrapped row), tick with its words (`TickField`), popup (`PopupPanel`, `usePopupDismiss`), Legal Entity row (`FieldRow`), free text (`TextField`: grows, list writing, its CSS height is the floor), name (`NameCell`) the folding card's head (`CardHead`: arrow, number, mark, name, actions, summary) tooltip (`TooltipBox`, `useAboveTip`), the card's outer box (`CardShell`) and the grid of tiles (`TileGrid`), label arrow (`FoldArrow` (variant label), also the Inventory menu's) and fold arrow (`FoldArrow`, cards and sections). **The parts are shared** (design Interface 1): one `Tile` and `TileGrid`, one card head and box (`BusinessCard`), `FieldRow`, `FieldHead`, `TickField`, `PopupPanel`; the controls in `components/controls/` (`AddButton`, `RemoveButton`, `NavArrow`, `RadioChoice`, `FoldHead`, `FunnelButton`...). A new tile or card is built from them; none draws its own control.

- **Every card is `BusinessCard`**: the Business, Brand, Strategy and Financial cards and the Legal Entity's (a requirement, an expense, a founder: `legal`, its own box, head and number, "legal-entity-*") are one component with one head (`CardHead`), one name cell (`NameCell`) and one outer box (`CardShell`); the legal one differs by its box's class only.
- **The funnel that leaves something out is always seen**: filled, its funnel in the card's ground; the global rule that clears every wired button at rest leaves a button that is switched on (`data-active`) alone.
- **One funnel**: `FunnelButton` (the "+" box with the one drawn funnel, filled while it leaves something out) opens every filter or sort: a founder's Responsibilities, the card notes, the Inventory, a section's sort.
- **Every text link is one component** (`TextLink`; the look and the kinds of link are Interface 6); a link with the keyboard's focus is bold and underlined but does not slide. A place passes only where it stands; a link is never a bare `<button className="...-link">`.
- **One underline everywhere** (the look of a text link is Interface 6, its reactions R5 and R6 of Interface 5): 1px thick, 1px under the lowest descender, no place sets an offset of its own (`cell-focus.css`, `*`). **A long text link slides by the scroll of its box** (`textLinks.ts`), under the pointer only, whatever the motion setting; a name that is a link keeps its number and the text goes behind it. **Its underline is never cut** (3px under the letters, taken back by the margin).
- **A text that underlines but clips its overflow** has room for its line (3px under the letters, taken back by the margin): a typeface of the Typography tile underlines on hover.
- **Where a link lands, it is framed in the links' blue** (owner, 2026-11-10: "the app should guide and direct the user to the related part/field etc. framing it in a related colour (blue like the link, and according the reading mode)"): every link that leads to a part (Search, the Review, the Glossary's "In the app", a Card Note, a competitor under a Differentiation, a reference in an "i") brings it into view and frames it with a 3px outline in `--text-primary`, the links' own colour in each reading mode, drawn just inside its edge (no scrolling box cuts it), held, then fading out (`.jump-highlight`, `land` in `jumpTo.ts`; 2.4 s); a question drawn by its children (its pair has no box) is framed by the band that holds it. A term or reference asked for in the Workbook takes the same frame (`.glossary-entry-target`). A Note's own link keeps the Notes' orange (`is-noted-hot`). Links wait for the part to be drawn (checked every 40 ms, up to 5 s), never a fixed time (`notepadPlaces.ts`).
- **Information, help and links start closed; content starts open** (`useItemFolds(openByDefault)`): Customer Engagement's items, the financial items and an Objective's Initiatives start open; so do Customer Engagement's, Financial Foundation's and Sustainability's sections (their work is in them).

### Interactions: one set of reactions per kind

This table is what the layout check runs against: it hovers up to three of each kind on every page and fails a kind that does not react as its row says. The same reactions by component, with the kinds of reaction named R1 to R14, are the lookup in Interface 5; where the two differ, the latest dated decision wins (`standard.md`, section 3), and the other is fixed to match it.

| Kind | Hover | Focus | Disabled |
|---|---|---|---|
| Text box (input, textarea) | the soft glow (18%) round it, the edge unchanged (R1; the primary edge is the focus ring's) | primary edge + ring (3px at 70%, `--focus-ring-mix`, the one effect of every text holder) | muted, no reaction (a completed card's cells keep their shape: rule 14) |
| Text holder (an image's name and description, one box) | as a text box, on the holder | as a text box | as a text box |
| Picker | as a text box | as a text box | as a text box |
| Tick box | its words underlined, the box's glow ring (the field's, 4px at 18%, no wire; whether or not the words are in its label: `.brand-tick` too); ticked, the box is flat (no inner light or shade) and keeps the glow | focus ring | not-allowed cursor |
| Radio (`RadioChoice`) | its words underlined, the round's glow ring (as a tick box: 4px at 18%, no wire); the round is drawn at 16px (`--size-radio`) in the tick box's look, from its tokens (`--choice-glow`, `--choice-ring`, defined once in `global.css`), with its focus ring and disabled look; chosen, the app's blue with a white bullet), so the ring is round | the browser's focus ring | not-allowed cursor |
| Date cell, month cell | as a text box (no stroke, recessed, glow, ring); never bold | as a text box | muted, no reaction |
| Calendar button (inside a date or month cell) | the "+" box's swap: ground, 2px stroke and symbol in the card's tone, filled under the pointer (outside a coloured card, the tone every cell has) | the same and a 2px outline | 50% |
| Calendar card, colour picker card | its day, month and arrow buttons: the "+" box's swap, every button with the same 2px frame so none changes size; no bold | outline | the day dimmed; a click outside, or Escape, closes the card |
| Text link (every one: `text-links.css`) | bold and underlined (the line 1px under the descenders); a text longer than its place moves right to left | outline | grey in a completed card |
| Link symbol | 20% larger about its centre, nothing moves; drawn in the card's tone | the same | n/a |
| Funnel (founder's Responsibilities) | the "+" box's swap | outline | n/a |
| The tile or box that opened the open card | a steady halo in the card's tint (not flashing); a link that opened it is bold | - | n/a |
| Add button | its ground | focus ring | muted |
| x (remove) | filled with the danger red (`--x-red`), no stroke, the X in the card's ground (`--x-ground`); at rest transparent (no fill), a 2px red stroke and a red X; the same on every x | outline, the focus ring ink, 2px, 2px off | muted |
| Fold arrow (`FoldArrow`, 36px) | its ground and the card-line edge -- **not** a founder's Responsibilities / Completed arrows, which have none | focus ring | n/a |
| Page tile (its slot: button, list, "+") | grows to 110% about its aimed corner (set once per stay), above its neighbours, a drop shadow growing with it | a 2px primary outline on the control that holds it (no growth) | n/a |
| Box in a dialog (subcategory, founder, add; a card has no inset light and shade, only its drop shadow) | soft glow (18%) beside its inset light and shade (no stroke) | a 2px primary outline | n/a |
| Item of a page tile's list | underlined | underlined and a 2px primary outline | n/a |
| "+" box (a page tile's, 36px; the Activity card's Add Team, 32px) | the colours swap (150ms): at rest the ground, a 2px stroke and the "+" in the darker tone; under the pointer the darker tone filled, no stroke, the "+" in the ground | the same and a 2px outline | muted, not-allowed |
| "i" of a coloured card | stroke gone, filled with the tint, the letter in the ground (a swap) | the same | n/a |
| Line under a parent's Status ("Open Activity “...”", an alarm when the child holds details) | bold (a text-shadow, nothing moves) while the pointer is on the Status cell or on the line; its link underlined | outline on a link | n/a |
| A child's name (the link to its parent) | underlined and bolder | a 2px outline | n/a |
| Label arrow (beside a label or a card title) | the outline triangle fills | the same fill and the focus ring (below) | n/a |

**Every focus ring reads 3:1 against what is around it, 2px at least** (WCAG 2.4.7, 2.4.13 as a guide; the standards report, group A 5, 2026-11-22; rule | was | now: a focused control shows where the keyboard is | the "i" kept a 4px glow of 1.5:1 (1.07 on a dark title band), a radio's 3px glow read 2.9:1 on a tinted band, the date cell, the label arrow, a brand's tick and the header's Workbook showed none, a dialog's page arrows read 2.6:1 on the scrim | the app's one ring, 2px of `--focus-ring-ink`, 2px off, on each of them (`global.css`); the "i" and the page arrows with a 4px halo of the surface under the ring, so it stands apart from any band or scrim; measured by Tab on every page and its first dialog in light, dark and sepia: every ring 2px and 3:1). **The keyboard stays in the topmost layer**: a dialog makes the page behind inert (the header stays live); a floating card, which leaves the page live for the pointer, keeps Tab inside itself (`FloatingCards.tsx`, `tabStops`; WCAG 2.4.3). **A hidden list is never a Tab stop**: a tile's list of plain lines is hidden from screen readers (its tile names it), so it takes no focus even where it scrolls (`TileList`; WCAG 4.1.2). **Text keeps 4.5:1**: a faded state is drawn by colour, never by opacity (an empty category's tile: its ground half way to the page's, its words in `--text-muted`; an empty category's line in `--text-muted`; the channel teal `--channel-marker` at 26% light); the words on a brand colour's box take the dark or the white ink, whichever reads more (WCAG 1.4.3; axe, every page and master tile's dialog of both examples in the three reading modes: 0, but for one user colour, #c8553d, where neither ink reaches 4.5, white 4.35). **Nothing is dragged**: cards keep the order they were made in, so their numbers (owner, 2026-11-22 09:59 UTC+2: "If we change the order of the cards, there will be a conflict with their order number"); the Inventory's Sort orders them by date (WCAG 2.5.7).

A tick box toggles only from the box itself or its words: its label is as
wide as they are (`width: fit-content`), never the empty space beside
them; Space toggles the focused box. A tick's "i" stands outside its label,
so reading the note never toggles the tick.

The check hovers up to twelve x and fails if any two turn different colours
(one reaction per kind).

**The one x.** Every remove button is the shared `.strategy-box-remove`
and nothing else (no second class adding a hover, a ring or a glyph size):
the Strategy Theme, Goal, Objective and Metric x are the Legal Entity
card's. Its look is the "+" box's swap of two colours (remove-button.css, app-wide): at rest there is no fill (transparent: the card's ground
shows, `--x-ground`), the stroke 2px and the X the danger red (`--x-red`); under the pointer or the focus no stroke, filled red, the X in the ground. The check reads a Legal Entity card's x at rest, under the
pointer and focused (square, edge, corners, ground, colour, ring,
transition and the two drawn bars) and fails any x on any page that
differs.

**Which x** (owner, 2026-11-14 12:29, 12:33 and 12:38). Two removes, one owner each (`remove-button.css`):
- **The framed x** (`RemoveButton`, `.strategy-box-remove`, 32px): a card's own delete, a row's or a box's remove, a journey stage's x -- everything that stands beside a cell, in the card's x column.
- **The in-cell x** (`CellRemoveButton`, `.cell-remove`, 24px target, `--size-cell-x`): an x INSIDE a cell, at its right end -- an action line of a channel's journey stage, and a channel expense's linked action (the chip, 32px tall: `--chip-height`). No frame, outline or fill: the X alone in `--x-red`, the same two bars as the framed x. Under the pointer or the keyboard focus it grows by a fifth (`--hover-lift`) and takes one soft haze of its red (a blurred drop shadow, no outline); the focus adds the focus ring (2px, offset 2px); with reduced motion it only glows.

- **A folded list on a card is one component** (`FoldList`, `FoldListLine`; owner, 2026-11-14 18:10 and 18:11): the fold arrow and the label on one line, the count after them where there is one, the lines under them 6px apart -- a card's Links, a relationship type's stage channels (each subchannel set in under its channel), a subchannel's Fees (its label the link to the Global Settings). A line is cut by an ellipsis, or wraps where its whole words matter (`wrap`). Grouped (`FoldListGroup`: a channel and its subchannels), a thin line parts one group from the next and each subchannel has the shown lists' bullet (owner, 2026-11-14 19:04).
- **A draft is written in by itself** (owner, 2026-11-20 18:35 UTC+2: "Instead of appearing, an Add a draft option, add the draft automatically"; 19:17: "Add only the draft and remove the Notes. The app user could add or change the text as he wishes"): a text the app drafts from the answers (the Vision, the Ambassador's texts, a source's Basis, a relationship's Purpose and each Approach, the Value Proposition, the Strategic Compass, an Objective's name) takes its draft while it is empty, and keeps following the answers while it still says what the app wrote; a text already saved stays as it is, and the text is always the founder's to change. No "Add a draft" link and no separate Notes (`useAutoDraft`, `AutoDraft`). Rule | was | now: "Add a draft" added a sentence when pressed | a link to press | written in, editable.
- **A text under its choices stands a field gap under them** (2026-11-20, batch C; the band rule, owner, 2026-11-10): the Mission, the Vision, the Values and the Ambassador's texts stand 12px under their choices' last band, the band's own 12px inside it (`.column-tile-content.legal-entity-field > .business-segment-grid-single + *`, one rule); rule | was | now: a field gap between a band and the next field | 6px, the column's label gap | 12px. In a pair dialog (Mission beside Vision, Values beside Principles) a band spans its column, as the other column's bands do (declared in the layout probe).
- **The Menu** (owner, 2026-11-21 00:56 and 23:44 UTC+2: "I want to place the global settings, Calendar, Notes, Focus, Search … under a common umbrella", "Include the Reading Mode"; his notes of 2026-11-21 03:40 UTC+2: "MENU" with the settings symbol, no inner title, "Appearance" one row with a side menu, each tool's symbol on its row): ONE header button, **Menu** (the sliders the Settings button had, and its word; the icon alone in a narrow header, as every header tool; `--icon-size`), opens the dropdown card every menu a button opens has (`.strategy-sort-menu`, `MenuOption` rows, `usePopupDismiss`), with no title: **Search** (Ctrl+K beside it), **Notes** (the count of notes beside it), **Calendar** (it opens the Calendar on the page it is pressed on, as Notes opens its panel: a side panel at the left in the Notes panel's look and behaviour, "Calendar" on top with its closing arrow, open until its arrow or Escape (owner, 2026-11-22 19:31 UTC+2: "the calendar should have it title on top. Redesign it so to look like the Notes right panel style and behaviour"; until then a rail of handles, closing on a press outside or the pointer leaving), its Escape kept to itself as the Notes panel's, `useEscapeCloses`; owner, 2026-11-21 04:05 UTC+2: "Selecting the Calendar should not move back to the beginning, but should open the side panel"), **Focus** (on an interview page only: focus mode is an interview page's), **Settings** (a gear; the Global Settings), then **Appearance** (the icon of the appearance chosen; Auto with the icon of what it comes to), whose reading modes open IN THE MENU'S OWN CARD, under its row: the card grows down by four rows, each mode's symbol under the word "Appearance", one ground and one shadow (owner, 2026-11-21 16:02 UTC+2: "The Reading Modes should be extended from the same menu card, hovering over the Appearance"; the side card of 04:05, joined to the menu's left edge, still read as two cards) -- Light, Sepia, Dark and Auto, the chosen one marked (`MenuOption marked`). Each row its tool's symbol, 24px, the header tools' blue and line (app-header.css, ONE DRAWN HEIGHT, ONE LINE). The reading modes open on the pointer, a press, Enter, Space or the right arrow (the chosen one takes the focus); the up and down arrows move among them; the left arrow or Escape closes them alone and gives the focus back to Appearance; Appearance's arrow turns down while they are open. The **Workbook** stays its own button (a page, not a tool). With no Organisation open the Menu holds Appearance alone. Rule | was | now: one umbrella for the tools | Search, Notes, Focus, Settings and the reading mode were five header buttons and the Calendar a rail tab on System Selection only | one Menu holding the six; Ctrl+K and Escape keep their keys; the Focus's "Exit focus" button stays where it is while focus is on.
- **A table's framed row stands in the middle of the view** (owner, 2026-11-20 18:35 UTC+2: "I want the indicator frame to stay within a level range, so the user would not need to look at the screen edges"): as the arrows step from card to card, the dialog scrolls the framed row to its middle, the same level each time (`SectionTiles.tsx`, `block: "center"`); rule | was | now: the dialog follows the arrows the least it can | the framed row ran to the window's top or foot | to the middle.
- **A journey stage's table is a record** (owner, 2026-11-14 17:44 to 18:14): a row for each subchannel, its expenses under its name with their costs; an action's cell as the subchannel card's -- its order (1.) at the top left, its number in the page (#14) at the bottom left, its words beside them, its symbols in a column at the right, the link above the tag.

- **A report's side panel stands in three groups** (the Inventory; owner, 2026-11-14 18:17): Show (Include, Belongs to, Status, Frequency, Linked, Costs and Channel Costs), Order (Sort and the cost order's arrows), Table (Columns, in the Table view only); each group's name above its blocks with a line under it; From and To only while Costs is ticked.
- **Lines, not bullets, in every table** (owner, 2026-11-14 18:19 and 19:08): two or more values in a cell (the Inventory's Offering, Cost per Offering, Journey and Group cells, the channels' tables, a journey stage's Offerings and expenses) stand one under another with a thin line between them (`Values`), as the actions do; a single value stands at the cell's padding.
- **A dividing line runs edge to edge** (owner, 2026-11-14 19:09, a general rule): a line between items -- of a table cell's list, a channel and its subchannel in a cell, the channels of a stage's fold -- runs across its whole cell or column, the padding taken back by the line and given to the words; the layout check fails a line in a table cell that does not meet both of its cell's edges ("a dividing line meets both edges of its cell").

- **A label's fold arrow stands on its first line** (owner, 2026-11-14 20:08): a label that wraps ("Support & / Follow Up") keeps its arrow centred on its first line (`label-arrow.css`: the arrow set down by half of what `--label-line` is taller than it).
- **A report's options are smart** (the Inventory; owner, 2026-11-14 20:08): an option of Type, Category, Group, Offerings, Journey Stages, Status or Frequency stands only where choosing it, with every other choice as it is, still lists a card; a ticked one stays, so it can be unticked.
- **A journey stage's table** (owner, 2026-11-14 20:08): its heading row between two bold lines (2px); a bold line under a subchannel's name where its expenses follow, edge to edge; each expense its name, then its cost on a line of its own.

### Picker options

- **One option is a record, not a dropdown** (owner, 2026-11-14 14:21: "Make it a rule"; 14:26): a picker (`OptionPicker`) with a single option shows it as the field's value, read only, in the read-only value's look (`.option-picker-record`), and saves it; a second option makes it a dropdown again. Where none is an answer too -- an optional link: an Activity's Resource, a legal expense's Linked registration -- it stays a dropdown (`keepChoice`); so does an option that does something ("+ Add", "Other…"). It applies wherever a value is required: a card's Group in Infrastructure & Capability and Offering's Capability, a Status with one choice, a Customer Source's Channel or kind narrowed to one, a Legal Entity's activity level with one entry (a Subsector), a filter with one value (the Operational Log's Process and Owner).

- **No bracketed text in an option.** A hint, a note or an explanation
  ("(common here)", "(not listed)") goes in the field's "i" instead:
  a Legal Requirement's Type says "Usually required here: ..." in its note;
  "Other (not listed)" reads "Other" (the field that follows names it; the
  saved value is unchanged).
- **Exception: an abbreviation in brackets stays** ("Private Company
