Under 358px of content the Frequency goes under the row; the Description box at the card's content width, two lines at least; rows 12px apart; every control starts at the one left edge (x 13 in the card). **The Responsible tick row is ONE ROW at every width**: the tick, its
  words and "i" in a column as wide as they need up to `--side-label-short` (177px), then the dropdown, which hugs and is cut to what is left -- never under the tick row (measured on an Activity, a Partner and a Provider in cards 480,
  440, 400 and 342px wide; the narrow-card rules that put a label above its field do not apply to a field with a tick, the Deposit and the optional notes excepted). **An Activity's Team and Responsible rows:** the label (the
  Responsible's with its tick) and its dropdown stand next to each other, 12px apart, on one centre line, in one shared column (the Responsible's row shares the stack's two columns: `grid-template-columns: subgrid`), so both dropdowns start at one x. **The Team's dropdown runs from
  its label's gap to the right edge of the name cell above it** (it is not limited to its longest option here: `OptionPicker fill="fit"`; the head's name cell ends 8px short of the x, and so does the dropdown), and at the x's own place -- the x's column, its size (32px), centred on the dropdown -- stands the **"+" box** (`.plus-box-small`, the page's "+" box at the x's size,
  named "Add to Team of Activity 1", replacing the old "+ Add Team (now "Add to Team")" rectangle) on the FIRST row while another performer can be added; a further performer's row repeats the label's empty place, the dropdown and, in the x's column, an x (32px) that takes it away. The label stands on the first dropdown's centre line. **The Completed tick** is the card's last row, at the left with the other ticks (the Responsible's and the Description's: one x). The single-founder hiding and the tick are unchanged.
- **Infrastructure & Capability: corners, cells, arrows, the dropdown list, the opened card's columns, the tiles and the Landing.**
  - **Corners.** Unchanged: every box, card and dialog keeps the radius it had (8px boxes and cards, 6px cells); nothing follows the box size.
  - **Recessed text and number cells.** A text or number input / textarea (not a picker, not a tick box) is the raised cell turned over: `--cell-inset-recess` (shade at the top left, light at the bottom right) on the cell edge (`--cell-edge`); a picker keeps `--cell-inset`; the focus ring stays over it; a disabled (frozen) cell is flat. The Workspace form and the Delivery cards take it through the same selector. The x (`.strategy-box-remove`), ticks, the "i", "+" boxes, arrows and other small buttons are NOT touched by the page-3 edges: the x is the app's one (remove-button.css) and no rule of `capability-edges.css` names it; the probe checks every x at rest has no inset shadow and looks like every other.
  - **Wired / filled arrows.** The founders' fold arrows (11 x 18 closed, 18 x 11 open at an 18px label) and the title arrows are the label arrow (see "The label arrow"): an SVG triangle, an outline (2px line inside the shape) at rest, the same triangle filled under the pointer and keyboard focus. Box, margins and place are identical in both states, so nothing moves. No ground or ring on hover.
  - **The dropdown list** -- and every menu a button opens (a sort, Export, a funnel's ticks, the reading mode), its rows the list's own (MenuOption, untracked) -- is a card of its own under the cell (above it where there is no room): 6px gap, no stroke (the boxes' inset light and shade plus the drop shadow), except a funnel's window, which stands in the card's colour as the Inventory's does (its tinted ground and its wireframe in the card's edge; the Notes panel's on the panel's neutral ground), its filters 12px from its sides and its blocks 12px apart, exactly the cell's width and left edge (inline width / min / max from the cell's box), a long option wrapping inside it with its full text as tooltip. A Status, Responsible or Provider picker `fill="fit"` runs from its label's gap to the right edge of the name cell.
  - **A titled section of a card stands under the shared double rule** (owner, 2026-11-20 19:45 UTC+2: "Regarding the offering, is missing a double line above the section"): the OFFERINGS band of an Activity's, a Resource's, a Partner's or Supplier's and a Legal card, as the Segment card's Profiles and the Registrations' Additional Legal Requirements (`EngagementBox`, `DialogSectionBand`); the layout check fails one without it.
  - **Infrastructure & Capability's cards: one band a section** (owner, 2026-11-18 21:45 and 22:00 UTC+2: "Start and End page should be a section, defining the type another, costs another, notes , another, team and responsibilities another", "I did not ask for titles"; this replaces, on these cards, "one question per band"): a Resource's, an Activity's, a Partner's, Supplier's or Staff card's and a founder's questions stand in sections -- **Dates** (Start, End and what they say), **Type** (Category, Group, Status), **Status** (what a Status opens, a band of its own under it: a Rent's landlord, a Partner's or Supplier's Category, Group and Name, an Activity and its category, the further Status rows; owner, 2026-11-19 16:52 UTC+2, 3.2: "whenever the Status is Activity or Partner & Supplier, the next auto-generated fields should have a different colour-tinted section"; 19:44 UTC+2: in the card's own hue, a step of its own -- the stronger tint a box's title band has, `--tint-box-title`, never a new hue), **Costs** (the cost rows, the price, the plan, a founder's pay and hours), **Options** (every optional choice between the price and the Responsibilities: the extras, a warranty, a rate of its own, an amortisation, a deposit left from earlier, a founder's Allowance, Commission and Bonus; owner, 2026-11-19 16:52 UTC+2: "place the Allowance, Commission, and Bonus in a common section", "Any information that exists between the Price and the Responsibility as optional choices should be in a common section"), **Team & Responsibilities** (the Team, the Responsible, a founder's roles and responsibilities), **Notes** -- each section ONE band with no title, its questions one under the other 12px apart, **with no line between them but between two questions that are both a radio choice or a tick box** (owner, 2026-11-18 23:56 UTC+2: "Do not place dividing lines in the section, when the fields are not radio choice or ticked boxes"; the card's thin line, 12px over and under, side to side of the band), **the Options section none at all** (owner, 2026-11-19 21:44 UTC+2: "Remove the dividing lines in the Allowance, Commission and Bonus section"), **the Team & Responsibilities section a line between each of its parts** -- the Team, the Resources, the Responsible -- the card's own rule (`.business-capability-card-rule`, its neutral colour, no tint), **edge to edge of the band**, through the x column's room (owner, 2026-11-20 18:35 UTC+2: "place dividing lines (no colour var) between them"; 19:00: "Dividing lines from edge to edge!", as section 6, 18 and the rule of 2026-11-14 19:09 have it; rule | was | now: no line between questions that are not ticks | the Team, Resources and Responsible ran together, then the first lines stopped at the name cell's right edge | parted by the card's rule from side to side of the band, on every card in bands; the layout check fails a band's divider that does not meet both sides, "a band's divider runs edge to edge"), the tints alternating band by band (`BandSection`, `EngagementBands`, `EngagementCard.tsx`). The card's order is kept: two neighbouring parts of a section are one band, a section with nothing to ask draws none, and a section the order splits stands as two bands (a founder's roles, then the pay, then the responsibilities; an Activity's Team, Resources and Responsible are ONE band, the Team's costs and the Resources in it: owner, 2026-11-19 00:20 UTC+2, "Team, Resources and Responsible should be a section"). **The Links are a band of their own colour** (owner, 2026-11-18 23:56 UTC+2: "Links should be have a different coloured band"; `BandSection` "links", from `linksSection`): the links' blue (`--text-primary`, each reading mode's) at a card band's strength (12%), not one of the alternating tints. **The Completed row is a band of its own colour** (owner, 2026-11-19 00:20 and 01:08 UTC+2: "Completed should have another coloured band"; the shared band saturation and lightness, hue 150: docs/design/foundation/01-current-design.md, "The band hues inside a card"), from side to side of the card, the card's line above it given up to the colour. **A founder's one Links list** (named Links since owner, 2026-11-19 21:44 UTC+2: "Rename the Responsibilities to Links"; its funnel also offers Team member and Responsible; owner, 2026-11-19 00:04 UTC+2: "A: One list, Responsible marked"; 01:20, the mark "option 2"): every activity whose Team holds the founder or whose Responsible the founder is, with the suppliers (S) and legal expenses (L) the founder answers for; the ones the founder is Responsible for carry the **responsible** mark icon (`MarkIcon`, Foundation 1) at the mark size (24px), "Responsible" its tooltip and its name for screen readers; a founder's card has no Links section (Staff, Partner and Supplier cards keep theirs). **The linked Offerings stand under a section heading** (owner, 2026-11-19 00:16 and 01:08 UTC+2: "Make the Offerings Label as section", A, "the section band: light capitals on the dark fill, like DESCRIPTION on the Profile card"): OFFERINGS is a section band (the Profile's DESCRIPTION band), its tick boxes and shares in the band under it, with no label of their own; the card's helper (`offeringsField`) sets the section straight in the card's body, where the bands read it (owner, 2026-11-19 16:52, 3.4: "I said to make the Offerings a section Title": the section stood inside the shared field, unseen, so no title band showed). A card's own divider line is no band. The layout probe's "a band holds one question" skips a section's band.
  - **Infrastructure & Capability's cards in bands: the bands win over the name cell's column** (owner, 2026-11-18 20:20 UTC+2). A Resource, an Activity, a Partner, Supplier or Staff card and a founder's card (`CardBody banded`, `EngagementBands`, the shared bands of Customer Engagement) have their questions under the head as bands: each question a band, the tints alternating from the first, a line that says something of the question above it (a total, an alarm, a note) in that question's band; the bands run across the content and the x columns out to the card's sides (`.card-box-open > .business-capability-slot > .business-segment-grid-single`, "The bands win over the name cell's column"), and what stands in a band ends at the name cell's right edge, the x column clear; only the band tints run to the card's sides (owner's screenshot, 2026-11-19 00:45 UTC+2, later than the 20:20 "bands win"): the rules below that end a field, the Performer's dropdown or the Frequency at the name cell's right edge keep that edge. The head keeps its grid (the number, the name cell, the x). A band is drawn only where it has something to ask (an Activity with no Responsible to ask has no empty band). **The Workspace's cards are banded too** (owner, 2026-11-22 18:58 UTC+2, his screenshots of three Workspace cards that differed: "The floating cards for Workspace"; rule | was | now: one layout for every resource card | a card of the Workspace category or a Premises card was kept unbanded ("never change its interface again"), so its Offerings had no title band and its rows no tints | every resource card in bands, the Workspace's too). The cost row's currency keeps its own width inside a band.
  - **The opened card's columns** (Resource, Activity, Partner, Provider, channel cards; not Delivery; for a card in bands see the rule above): a grid of three columns -- the marker (0 wide: the number stands inside the name cell, "Order number inside the name cell"), the content and the x -- the head on a subgrid. Everything under the head starts at the name cell's LEFT edge (x 245.9 at the 480px card) and ends at its RIGHT edge (634.3); the x column stays empty but for an Activity's "+" / x beside a performer; the Responsible row (Partner / Provider and Activity) ends at the name cell's edge; the Description box ends there too. A Partner's / Provider's Price (219 with its currency) and Frequency (hugging its longest option, 126px; the placeholder is just "Select") sit side by side while the content column has 358px (at the 480px card it has 388px), under each other below that. A child activity's head: the number at the card's padding edge (12px), 8px to the name, the name's text starting at the name column's left edge, both on one centre line (0.3px apart); the head 12px from the card's top. Probe rules 18c / 18f read all of it, including that no picker shows a cut value; the one exception is a Status picker holding its longest option (282px of text beside a 124px label in a 388px column), which ends in an ellipsis with its whole text in the list.
  - **Subcategory "+".** Each subcategory box of the four resource groups (fixed ones, and a founder's own Other) has a 32px "+" box at its lower right (in the box's own grid cell, 12px in) that makes a blank resource in that subcategory and opens it floating, captioned with the subcategory's name; left blank it is removed again (the subcategory stays). The four group tiles have no "+".
  - **Completed tile** (Infrastructure and Offering's Capability) exists only while at least one activity is completed; un-ticking the last one closes the dialog by itself. No "None yet".
  - **Founders tile** lists the names alone. **Capacity & Delivery tile** items are links: each opens that Offering's Delivery card floating, captioned "Delivery" (the tile's `group`). Its dialog: the Capacity band, the Skills band (LC1's ticks, each ticked skill's "Covered by:" line of links or "Not covered yet" in the muted line style, `.delivery-skill-cover`; the shared Notes), then the Delivery cards three a row. **A month's bar** (`delivery-budget-bar`, one for each founder and each person beside them who says the hours they can give) carries a fixed mark at 85% (`.delivery-budget-ceiling`, a 2px line in the text colour across the bar; Questions 19, section 10); its lines are `delivery-muted`. A Delivery card's order number stands at the card's padding edge (12px in, left-aligned); a person's "(founder)" / "(external)" is in the tooltip and the input's accessible name, not drawn. Opened floating, a Delivery card keeps its own wide label column (`--side-label-short`).
  - **Channel cards** (a Resource, partner or provider a Customer Channel made, in Offering's Capability) are auto-generated: no name cell and no x -- the number and the name are plain text (the name from the channel's cell, where it is renamed and where the card is removed); their other fields stay editable; the "For channel: ... Go there" line stays. A card of Infrastructure only linked from a channel is the user's own and keeps its name cell and x.
  - **Landing:** a "Reload" button after the theme switch in the header (reloads the app).
  - **Landing, the user's name**: the box alone, no label above it (the label "User Name" and the placeholder "Enter your name" said the same thing); the words inside it say what is asked and why, "Your name, as it should appear on your documents" (it starts the first founder's name on Identity › Legal Entity, which the documents print), and are its accessible name too (`aria-label`).

- **Infrastructure & Capability: names, one way to present a card, the cost row, the x, the hover lift, the focus ring, numbers, the help, lists, titles and the type scale.**
  - **Names.** Offering's Capability's third tile is **Partners & Suppliers**, and its grid has two boxes, **Partners** and **Providers**; Infrastructure's third section is "3. Partners & Suppliers" with the same two tiles, **Partners** and **Providers**. "Partnerships" is no longer said anywhere. The Status option "External Professionals & Partners" of a resource (an answer, not a section) keeps its words; stored keys are unchanged.
  - **One way to present a card.** A Resource, Activity, Partner or Provider card (channel-made, finished and the parent / child pair too) is ONLY a **floating card** on its section part of the page (top-aligned with the first tile row, its box name above it, a drop shadow, stacked when several are open), never shown inside a dialog and never centred in the page behind a blurred ground. A tile or subcategory box connected with exactly ONE card opens that card at once; one with none makes one blank card and opens it (taken away again if left blank); one that groups several opens the DIALOG, which is only a grid to choose from (subcategory boxes, card boxes with their add box) -- pressing a card box closes the grid and opens the card floating. Escape and a press outside close a floating card and return to the page (not to the grid) (no Back, the page not dimmed; 2026-11-19 reconciliation). Not these kinds, unchanged: the Founders dialog and the Delivery dialog (its cards in the dialog; its list links open one floating).
  - **The cost row** of a Resource, Partner or Provider card is ONE row: `[Qty] [Price .......] [currency] ... [Frequency]`, the labels (Qty, Price, Frequency) above their cells, the currency with none. Qty is the 56px of its label's column (`--qty-w`: three digits and the cell's padding), 12px to the Price, 12px from the Price to the currency; the currency's left edge is the side pickers' (Status's, Responsible's: the label column `--side-label-short`, 177px, and its 12px gap); the Frequency hugs its longest option (126px; its placeholder is just "Select") and ends at the name cell's right edge. Measured at the 480px card: Partner Price 245.9-409.9, currency 421.9-501.4, Frequency 507.9-634.3 (name cell 245.9-634.3); Resource Qty 951.9-1007.9, Price 1019.9-1115.9, currency 1127.9 (= the Status picker's left), Frequency to 1340.3 (= the name cell's right). Under 358px of content the Frequency goes under the row. The Resource's Status label column is 177px: the Status picker is 212px, the longest option ends in an ellipsis there.
  - **A card made from earlier answers has no x** (owner, 2026-11-20 18:35 UTC+2: "auto-generated from previous answers, so they do not need to have x (delete) button. Use that space (right edge) to widen the text cells"): a Customer Relationship card and a Customer Source the answers made (or one that took their channels) carry no x, and their cells take the x column's room (`EngagementCard` `madeByApp`, `.card-made-by-app`: `--card-x-column: 0px`); a source the founder added on its own keeps its x. Rule | was | now: every card has its x | the made cards showed an x and kept its column empty | no x, the cells to the card's padding.
  - **The x** (the one shared `.strategy-box-remove`, and the three older removes that share it) is a swap of two colours like the "+" box, app-wide: at rest it has NO fill (a wired button: transparent, it takes the colour of the card it stands on; `--x-ground` names that ground, set where each card kind sets its ground, the page surface where none does, and is the X's colour under the pointer), the stroke 2px (the "+" box's) and the X both the danger red (`--x-red`, `--text-danger`: #b00020 light, #ff9b96 dark); under the pointer or the keyboard focus no stroke, filled red, the X in the ground colour. 32 x 32 as before, no inset light or shade (`capability-edges.css` names no x).
  - **A floating card has no Back button**: it closes with a press outside it. The Back buttons that exist are the dialog foot's (`.section-tile-back`) and the one in the rail's tab (`.carousel-rail-back`); see "Navigation rails" and the Back button contract (`src/library/assets/backButton.ts`).
  - **The hover lift** of Infrastructure & Capability (the glow a tile, a cell, a list row takes under the pointer) is its old amount times `--hover-lift` (1.2): the tile and cell glow 18% -> 21.6%, the chosen list row 28% -> 33.6%.
  - **The focus ring (one ring).** Every cell you write or choose in (text box, text area, name cell, picker, number box, a numbered list's box, a date or month cell) takes ONE ring in every page and reading mode: the primary edge (`--line-primary`) and a ring 3px wide at 35% of the card's glow colour; no white outline, no second ring (`cell-focus.css`, loaded last). `--focus-ring-ink` stays the colour of the keyboard outline on buttons and links.
  - **Numbers on the right.** A numeric input of Infrastructure & Capability (inputmode decimal / numeric, type number: Qty, Price, Deposit, Salary, the hours) right-aligns its value with 12px of padding; text inputs are unchanged.
  - **The section help drop-down** (1. Infrastructure, 2. Offering's Capability) is 480px wide (the card's width where that is less), centred under the title row.
  - **Lists on the tiles** hold every item and scroll inside their bounds (under the title's underline, above the "+" box): a thin themed scrollbar, the wheel scrolls the list before the card behind, the keyboard walks the links; there is no "+N more".
  - **Titles and captions** (app-wide): `--title-ink` for the page title, column titles, section headings, tile and dialog titles and the header's name, `--floating-caption-ink` for the caption: light #303a49, sepia #3b2f25, dark #cdd1d8. Measured contrast, before -> after: light column title 13.0 -> 10.2, section heading 15.7 -> 10.2, tile title 14.0 -> 9.1; dark column title 10.7 -> 9.7, section heading 12.1 -> 9.7, tile title 11.4 -> 9.1, caption on the scrim 20.0 -> 13.1; sepia 10.5-11.3 (kept). The caption in light and sepia stays white: it stands on the dimmed page (5.7 and 6.4:1). Body text keeps its ink.
  - **The type scale** on Infrastructure & Capability: only 14 (`--font-small`: list text, summaries, a title that dropped a size), 18 (`--font-body`: tile titles, card names, labels, fields), 24 (`--font-h3`: section headings, dialog titles, captions), 28 (`--font-h2`: column titles) and 36 (`--font-h1`: the page title). The one deviation found, the dialog's visually hidden name at the browser's 16px, now takes 18. Probe rule 18i reads every text.
- **The edge of an opened card and its cells (no stroke).** The opened cards -- a founder's, a resource's, a partner's and a provider's, an activity's, and, named in the same request, the **Workspace form and the Delivery cards** -- their CELLS (inputs, pickers, text boxes, name cells, the x) have no stroke. The edge is the box's inset light and shade
  (`--box-inset` for a card, `--cell-inset` -- the lighter twin -- for a cell); the 1px border they keep for the box model (so nothing moves) is painted in the light on its top and left and the shade on its right and bottom (`--box-edge`, `--cell-edge`: `border-color` of four values), so the inset shadow runs on into it and there is no
  hairline of plain ground outside it. Pointed at, a cell keeps the soft glow (18%) around it; focused, the ring (70%, `--focus-ring-mix`) -- accessibility -- over the inset edge; disabled, flat (no light, no shade); the x is filled red under the pointer, no stroke. Tick boxes keep their own box, the dashed add boxes their dashed edge. All in `capability-edges.css`; the Workspace form's and the Delivery cards'
  share is ONE block at its foot ("THE WORKSPACE FORM AND THE DELIVERY CARDS"), with the same tokens, nothing else of them changed: delete that block and they have their strokes back.
- **The "i" of a coloured card.** Every "i" on this page (`InfoNote`): at rest it has NO fill (a wired button: an outline and a letter; the ground of the surface it stands on shows through, and `--info-fill` names that ground: the page card's, the dialog's, an opened card's own; on a box name over the dimmed page, a dark ground) with a 1.5px stroke (drawn as 1px at 100% zoom on a 1x screen: a border is drawn in whole device pixels) and its letter in the darker tint of the card's hue
  (`--info-ink`, `--theme-text-ink`; white on the scrim); under the pointer or the focus (or while its note is open) the stroke is gone and the two swap -- filled with the tint, the letter in the ground colour. The size (24px, the library's) is unchanged. Letter against ground at least 3:1 in both states.
- **The label arrows have no ground, ring or bolder glyph under the pointer.** The label arrows of a founder's card (Responsibilities, Completed) and of the titles (1. INFRASTRUCTURE, 2. OFFERING'S CAPABILITY) only fill their outline triangle under the pointer and the keyboard focus (focus draws no ring: the filled triangle is the focus). They keep their size, their name, and -- the founder's -- no shift on a click.
- **The column title's "i".** After the title's text, 6px (`--info-gap`, like every "i"; the title row's own gap is 8px and the note takes 2px off it), and before the label arrow: what the column is ("Existing or planned resources, activities, and partnerships & providers the organisation needs ..."; Offering's: "What each Offering needs on top of ..."): the notes the page had before it was drawn as tiles. Where the old page had an "i" the tiles have it still: on the column titles, the section headings, the dialogs' headers (the tile's, then each subcategory's), the fields' labels; the Workspace's, which has no dialog header, and the other boxes' are said after the box name above a floating card.
- **Title sizes** (type scale tokens only): page title 36 (`--font-h1`) > column title 28 (`--font-h2`; "1. INFRASTRUCTURE") > dialog title and
  section heading 24 (`--font-h3`; "6. Digital Infrastructure", "1. Resources") > tile, subcategory / card box title, subcategory heading, card
  name 18 (`--font-body`; weight 600 on tiles and boxes, 700 on headings; 14 for a title too long for its room and in a box under 120px) >
  labels 18 (lighter weight and muted colour) > lists, summaries, notes 14 (`--font-small`). (The column title and the section headings are scoped to this
  page; the other pages' are unchanged.) Equal sizes with different weights are deliberate: the scale has no step between 18 and 24.
- **Title Case.** Every multi-word title the app itself writes -- tile, box and dialog titles, the add-box labels, the placeholders of an unnamed card -- capitalises the first
  letter of every word except the symbols "&" and "/": "Channel Resources", "Channel Partners", "Digital Infrastructure", "Partners & Suppliers", "Cooling & Air Conditioning",
  "Heating & Gas", "Online Presence & Communication", "Sales & Customer", "Partners", "Add Other", "Add Resource", "Add Activity", "Add Partner",
  "Add Provider", "Unnamed Subsection", "Unnamed Activity", "Untitled Category"; single words are unchanged. It is the visible text only (what is stored is as it was) and what the user
  types is never altered.
- **Infrastructure & Capability scrolls in its cards.** In a window from about 812px wide (the page card 700px) and 560px high (the two
  cards side by side) the PAGE does not scroll: the app header, the page heading with its
  underline and the footer stay, and each card keeps its title row ("1. INFRASTRUCTURE
  ▸", "2. OFFERING'S CAPABILITY ▸", on the card's own ground) fixed at its top while the
  card's BODY (`.card-scroll`: `overflow-y: auto`, `overscroll-behavior: contain`) scrolls on
  its own beneath it. The body's top edge is the clip line, 6px under the title row's
  12px gap (its 6px padding and -6px margin give the tiles' hover glow room), so what
  scrolls up is cut there, never behind or over the title; no ghost text, no
  overlap. The cards stand from 12px under the heading's underline, as tall as their
  content, at most down to 12px above the footer; the guidance drop-down floats above
  the body (`z-index: 20`, taking no room) and the dialogs and their backdrop cover
  everything as before. Offering's Capability shows all its tiles without a bar unless the
  window is too small. Measured: 1600 x 1000, the card area 173 to 924px, Infrastructure's
  body 694px high (1278 of content), Offering's 502 (no bar); 1366 x 800, 173 to 724px,
  494px (1157 of content) and 453 (no bar). The document is exactly the window high (one bar
  at most, the card's). Rails and links: a rail bullet scrolls its card's body to the
  top; `jumpTo` scrolls the element into view inside its `.card-scroll`. Stacked (under 812px)
  or in a window under 560px high the page scrolls as the other pages do and the body does not.
- **The Founders & Ownership dialog.** The same frame as every dialog: the founders' table, then the founders as master tiles under the Profiles band (`SubTiles`); a tile opens that founder's card in place of the tiles (the heading reads "Founders & Ownership / Maya Chen"), its rail names the founders, and Back and Escape return to the tiles first.
  **A founder's card: ONE LEFT EDGE, one marker column.** Padding `--card-padding` (12px) + `--founder-inset` (4.5px) + `--founder-marker` (26.3px) on its left: the
  CONTENT EDGE is 43.5px in (the border included). Every text -- the name, the roles' text, the labels (Salary, Working Hrs ...), the left column's cells, the share line, the
  Responsibilities label, the list items' text, "Completed N" -- starts at the content edge; the head's number "1.", the roles' bullets, the
  Responsibilities fold arrow, the list numbers and the Completed arrow all start at ONE x, the marker column's (17.2px from the card's edge, measured on the glyphs); a wrapped line of an
  item aligns under its first line's text (hanging). A two-digit number is wider than the column: the column grows and the list's text follows it. **The head:** the number and the name are on
  ONE line -- one line height (28px), so the same baseline (246.5) and vertical centre (240.2) -- and the number, in tabular figures (every single digit 18.3px), ends **8px** (measured
  8.01) before the name. **Both fold arrows are the label arrow** (`FoldArrow` (variant label), see "The label arrow": an outline triangle in the card's tint that fills under the pointer and the keyboard focus, no ground and no ring): the
  Responsibilities row and the Completed row are the same row of one component. **The arrow does not move when it is toggled:** open and closed it has ONE margin, so its box and the label after it stay where they are; the glyph turns about its centre and is shifted by 0.1945em when open, so its left edge stays on the markers' x. The glyph starts on the markers' x and the label after it starts where a tick box's words start (26px from the edge). Lists: line height 1.45 (`--section-tile-line-height`, 26.1px at 18px), 6px between
  items (item pitch 32.1px, a wrapped item 52.2px + 6), 12px between blocks (the Completed block 12px under the list, its struck items 6px apart
  under it). The head: number, 8px, name; the roles 12px under it. **Four cells of one size** -- `--founder-cell` (108px, what "Annually" needs) x 45px
  high -- in two columns **12px apart, both at the left**: Salary | Frequency over Working Hrs | Delivery Hrs (the right column NOT pushed to the card's right edge; measured cells at x 43.5
  and 163.5 of a 297px card); **where the row holds a label beside its cell (the Founders dialog's one founder at 1366), each field is one row, its label beside its cell** (owner, 2026-11-19 13:34 and 13:45 UTC+2: "make the fields align horizontally, to save space", "This is the proper layout for the floated cards"; "A field on one row, its cell at its step"); the 492px floating card keeps Salary | Frequency and Working Hrs | Delivery Hrs side by side, and there each label stands over its cell, left-aligned: "Salary (£)" and "Frequency (i)" on one line, **"Working Hrs (i)" and "Delivery Hrs (i)" on TWO lines** ("Working" /
  "Hrs (i)", the "i" glued to the last word), and the second column is as wide as "Frequency (i)" (120px, 12px wider than its cell), so no label leaves the card or touches the other. Then
  ONE short muted line, the share of the working hours ("70% of working hours"; nothing at all while the working hours are blank or 0); then the Responsibilities row: its fold arrow,
  "Responsibilities (i)", and only the RATE ("0/6"). The Responsibilities list is COLLAPSED by default and **every time** (reset when the dialog opens, when a founder's box opens its card
  and when a floating founder's card opens). Equal heights are not required.
- **Closing** leaves the page as it was: the focus returns to the tile that
  opened the dialog, the scroll position and every fold are kept, nothing is
  re-created. A link to a card inside a tile (Review, search, a channel's
  "Go there", the Financial pages) presses the tile first (`data-holds` and
  `data-anchor` on the tile, `jumpTo.ts`).
- **The check** (section 18) measures the tiles (a square of `--section-tile-size`, one size per card, the group's shape -- a row of up to three, a 2 x 2 square,
  two rows; the title's last line on the third-down line, at least 12px from the top, underlined; the list a small muted
  centred block inside the square, contrast 4.5:1; the "+" of an empty section under the title, 24px, "No items yet"; the Completed tile's grey and its place in the footer band; every part of a page tile -- title, list, items, "+" box -- inside its square (no overflow); the "+" box 32px at the lower right, 12px from the edges, the list clear of it; each tile grown to 110% inside its card), the title
  arrows (hit area, 8px after the title, centred on its line, glyph size, the card's title first and 12px above its content, an open help floating and
  taking no room), the first tile rows of the side-by-side cards (one y), and opens every tile's dialog in turn
  (`__layoutProbe.checkWithTiles(spec, { settle, only })`), measures it (a rectangle from the underline to the footer, centred; Back at its lower left; the
  header one line with its slashes; the content centred across and at the top), unfolds everything in it, checks the dialog's cards against every rule, and opens
  the boxes level by level -- the first two founders' boxes of a 9+ set, EVERY subcategory box of Utilities, Workstation, Equipment, Digital Infrastructure and
  Offering's Partners & Suppliers, and the first three card boxes of each list -- measured, unfolded and checked, then Back to the level it came from; an
  empty list opens on its blank card, which is checked too and removed with Back. Then each page tile's first list item and its "+" box are pressed in turn (`checkFloating`; a child activity's link opens the stacked pair, which is checked: parent above child, 12px, one centre line, a disabled name, no x): a floating card is a dialog with a drop shadow, no dimmed backdrop and no Back button (2026-11-19 reconciliation), wholly in the window, `--open-card-width` wide (every kind, a founder's too), its box name centred 12px above it and outside it, centred over its page column (or the tile group for the Workspace), a card added with the "+" has its Name focused and, left blank, is removed again; the Workspace tile opens its form alone. It checks the card boxes (one side, the largest that fits, centred block,
  12px apart, the add box half the side at the top left of its cell and last, the shape of the group), the title sizes (page 36, column 28, dialog and section
  24, tiles / boxes / names 18 or 14, lists 14, the "+" 24), the opened card's controls (Quantity 56, Price 128, nothing stretched, `--open-card-width` at most, centred), the
  solid ground of the dialog and of its cards (no transparency, no blur), the one dialog frame, Infrastructure & Capability's scroll, the founder card (every text at the
  content edge, markers and fold-arrow glyphs at the marker column, the head number and name on one baseline and centre with 8px between, both fold arrows the standard 36px with their ring inside the card, four cells of 108px x 45px, left cells at the content edge, the right column 12px after the left, Salary / Frequency labels one line and Working Hrs / Delivery Hrs two, labels never touching, lists 1.45 with
  6px between items), and the opened card of a resource. Rules measure inside the scroll containers (a body is measured at its top). `checkWithTiles` leaves what it opened in
  `__layoutProbe.stats`.

## 9. Tiles: kinds, titles, lists, marks, hover and size

- **Diagram tile** (`SectionTile.diagram`, class `section-tile-diagram`), a category of Master Tile of its own. It stands for a drawing that opens in its dialog, not for a list of what a section holds: today the Roadmap, a Gantt diagram. It is a wide banner (442 x 102px beside the Strategic Period's dates) with a large title (36px) in its middle, across and down, and no list, not a square of the page tile size; the tile anatomy rules, the square rule and the one-size-per-group rule do not apply to it. Its dialog covers the page (`wide`).
- A tile's title has its first line 24px from the top (12px on a dialog's small tiles); a tile's list shows whole lines only, its scrollbar has no arrows and shows on hover.
- The title: capitals, 18px, semi-bold, letter-spaced, centred, its first line 24px from the tile's top on every page tile of every size, and 12px on a dialog's small tiles (its cards and sub tiles: owner, 2026-11-09, so four lines of the list fit above the tile's foot), underlined by a hairline (40% of the text colour). A title of two lines keeps its first line there; its rule and what follows it stand lower.
- **Titles only over what they title** (owner, 2026-11-19 22:48 UTC+2: "Titles - only when they have been defined, or already exist."): a sub-title on a tile's face (the Segment Description's PROFILES) stands only over lines it titles (`titledLines`, `SectionTileGeometry.tsx`), a dialog's titled section only where it holds something (`DialogSectionBand`), a card's titled band only where its section has a question (`EngagementBands`, as before). Test: `tileParts.test.tsx`.
- **Incomplete card**: `card-tile-incomplete` on the slot (red flashing glow around the tile; steady under prefers-reduced-motion), `section-tile-list-alert` on a line of a page tile's list (red, bold, flashing, first in the list). The tooltip says "Missing: <fields> (not counted until it is fixed)".
- **A card's fields show what it lacks exactly when its tile flashes** (`MissingScope`, `freshCards`): a card just made and still open is quiet; every other card, one lacking its Name too, shows its missing fields in red with their short line. (It was quiet for any card that lacked its Name when it opened, while its tile flashed.)
- Category/group titles uppercase (`.section-tile-category .section-tile-title`, `.dialog-heading-sub`); an empty group tile has opacity 0.5.
- **Add Card**: ONE tile (`AddTile`, `.add-card-tile`) ends every section/category/group grid, labelled "Add Card" (tooltip names what it adds); half the tile side; at the bottom right (last grid column, the free place of the lowest row else a row of its own; right edge = last column's tiles', bottom = row bottom); no scale on hover, ground turns semi-transparent and its + grows 10%.
- **Categories**: every category has its tile from the start (empty at 50%); Offering's Resources and Partners & Suppliers views add a CHANNEL category tile (channel teal, one constant `CHANNEL_TILE_HUE`); a channel-made card's floating card takes the same hue. Main page tile titles are capitals; dialog header wraps (no ellipsis), all names the title's size.
- **Growth**: an area whose tiles do not fit grows down (page scrolls; dialogs have `min-height`, their overlay scrolls). The two page cards start their tiles 12px under their titles (no 36px spacer row).
- Tiles per row: `useTilesAcross` (+2px tolerance) gives 3 across at every window width from 1280px (tile 176/191/203/215px at 1280/1366/1440/1600+).
- **Page cards**: `.section-tiles-quad` (four tiles): side = min((box width - 12) / 2, window height left under the title) -- the dialogs' tile (`pageTileSide`) stays the three-across side; tile radius token `--tile-radius` (8px) for tiles and the Add Card. Costs row: `--cost-x` is the label column and its gap (189px; Qty 56, the currency over the Status picker's left edge, Frequency to the name cell's right edge).
- **A tile's title is in capitals, but a card tile's** (owner, 2026-11-20 21:27 UTC+2: "All tile titles are capitalised, except the tiles that open floated cards"; `card-tile.css`, one rule): a page's Master Tiles, a dialog's tiles (a Journey Stage, a channel, an Offering, a Strategic Theme), the category and sub tiles; a card tile (`CardTile`, one card, opened floating) keeps its name as written. Rule | was | now: one case for a kind of tile | the capitals were set per page (the Business pages' main tiles, the categories), so the dialog tiles that open a dialog -- the channels, the Offerings, the Strategic Themes, the Journey Stages -- stood in Title Case | capitals; the layout probe fails a tile title off the rule ("a tile's title is in capitals", "a tile that opens a floating card keeps its title as written").
- **Tile title** (`TileTitle`): a Master Tile's title on the page wraps to three lines at most, as wide as its longest line (section 8, "Tile anatomy"; 2026-11-20); a tile in a dialog (a sub tile, a card tile) keeps ONE line, `white-space: nowrap`, sliding under the pointer (owner, 2026-10-15: "a title that is too long moves horizontally instead of being cut off"), an ellipsis at rest; 18px on every tile, tooltip with the full text; the same top on every tile of a size (`--tile-lift` is the one margin). Page 4-tile blocks: list 18px from 250px a side, else 14px.
- **Hover**: a page tile keeps its size and lifts its shadow (section 8, "The reaction (hover)"; a card tile in a dialog keeps its own `scale(1.1)`, card-tile.css); the Add Card never scales; its hover is: stroke gone, fill `color-mix(currentColor 16%, transparent)`, label and "+" in the card's dark tint (`--plus-fill`).
- **One face for every tile** (owner, 2026-11-18 22:56 and 23:08 UTC+2: "fix the listed items on the financial tiles, to follow the other tiles. That should have been done already without saying", "Financial tiles' lists should follow the shared tile list"): a tile that says names and what is said of them -- a figure, a type -- lists each name, then that in bold under it, and the tile list's own line between one and the next (`stackedFace`, `SectionTileGeometry.tsx`, the one helper): Sales Forecast, Customer's Relationship and the financial tiles (Revenue Streams; Costs; Funding; Cash Flow; Break-Even & Sustainability: Questions 20, section 13), never in two columns. **Kept in two columns:** a funding source's status and its count, inside Funding's Sources (owner, 2026-11-18 23:18 UTC+2: "Capital & Funding could be as before"), Channels & Journey's stages and their counts, and a founder and the share (`TileList` pairs, a line between the rows: owner, 2026-11-13 13:56, "Fix the structure of the master Tiles, use divide lines"); where a figure of such a list has words, the list stands as the one face instead. The layout probe fails a two-column list with a figure of words and any two-column list on the financial pages ("One face for every tile").
- A tile's list line can carry a mark (`SectionTile.itemMarks`): **empty** (the category has no card) text and bullet in `--text-muted`, the bullet a hollow ring (owner, 2026-11-22 19:37 UTC+2: "the listed items on the tiles should also be indicated as empty"; the shape says it, never the colour alone, WCAG 1.4.1), never faded by opacity (4.5:1 on every tile ground; 2026-11-22, the standards report, group A 3: at opacity .7 it read 3.0 to 3.2:1); **teal** (Offering's Resources: the Channel line) text and bullet in `--channel-marker`; **channel** (Offering's Activities / Partners & Suppliers: the line holds cards a channel made, its channel expenses; owner, 2026-11-20 12:49 UTC+2) two stacked bullets in the one bullet column, the line's own on top, the teal one 2px under it, both `--bullet-size`, centred on the line. No mark changes a line's text x or height; the probe's list-line rules hold.
- A tile title cut by an ellipsis slides left under the pointer (a scroll of the title's box, `useTitleSlide`) and back on leave; its size and position never change; nothing moves under `prefers-reduced-motion`.
- `TileList` measures each line's bottom from the top of the LIST (not of its layer), so the list ends at the foot of the last whole line that fits, plus 4px for descenders; no line is ever cut or taller than the list.
- A list line of a card with the channel tone is teal (`--channel-marker`) unless it is incomplete (red); `--text-muted` for empty categories.
- **The text on a column page's tile** (Definition, Purpose, Ambassador, Legal Entity ...) is plain text: no bullets, the lines running down to a line above the tile's foot, the last line fading (`.column-tiles-page .section-tile-list`). A press on it opens the tile.
- **Large tiles** (`SectionTile.large`): two of them stand side by side, as large as the four of a 2 x 2 page; three take the 2 x 2 with its fourth place empty (Legal Entity, Customer Engagement's chapters), the tiles keeping their size. A tile's own picture (`SectionTile.picture`) fills its square under the title.
  - Brand Style: Colours (every colour as a block on the tile) and Typography & Text Hierarchy (the typefaces). Their dialogs open boxes that are pictures (`PictureBoxes.tsx`): a colour's box is covered with the colour and carries its name, role and code; a typeface's box carries its name, role and a specimen. A box opens its card floating. The Text Hierarchy stays the table it was, under the typefaces, in the Typography dialog.
- A picture (colours, typefaces, images, an icon) stands in the same box of its tile, in its middle; the centres of the pictures of tiles side by side are one line.
- **A title with a forced break** (`\n`) is drawn on its lines (Typography & Text Hierarchy) and stands with its first line where the other titles stand. It belongs to a large Master Tile on the page only: a sub tile's title and a card tile's title are one line ("Tile title", above: cut with an ellipsis that slides under the pointer, the full title the tooltip), never broken by hand.
- **A tile's lines**: a list (founders, registrations, expenses, themes, roles and skills) has a bullet on each line (`ItemMark.bullet`); a list that already numbers its lines (Values, Principles) has none; text (Description, Philosophy, the gaps) has none; a line that stands alone is centred (`ItemMark.centre`). A line may carry bold words between pairs of asterisks; a bold word is a name or a price. A line between two topics is a hairline. Text on a column page ends at a whole line, a line above the foot (no fading).
- **A "+" on a card tile** in a dialog (`CardBox.plus`, `CardTile`, the page tile's `TilePlus`) adds to that card and opens it: an Offering's relationship type, its Customer Source (Customer Engagement, with All).
- **A suggestion's add is on the tile** (Customer Engagement): a card tile lists, faded, what the earlier answers suggest for it ("Suggested: Personal Assistance", a Customer Source's channel); the line is a link that adds it and opens the card. A floating card carries no add.
- **A remove on a title band stands on a light band**: a card whose parts are removed one by one (an Offering's relationship types) draws each as a box with the light box title band carrying its name, its "i" and its x (as a Customer Source's), never the dark section band, where the x cannot be read.
- **No "From your answers or All"** (owner, 2026-11-16 16:31): the choice is gone from Customer Engagement; every channel comes from the Foundation's answers and has no add of its own; every x shows; a channel removed comes back from the folded "Removed" list (the shared FoldList, Restore as a text link) at the foot of the channel dialogs; the Customer Sources' pickers stay narrowed to the Offering's own channels.
- **A "+" on a tile** (`SectionTile.add`) adds a card and opens it: Expenses, the Strategic Period (a Theme), Colours, Typography, Brand Images. Where a kind has a fixed number of cards (the colours, the typefaces) the "+" is hidden once all are there and returns when one is removed.
- **Business tiles**: a priced card's tile shows the price in bold and no longer says "Costs".
- **Two columns on a tile** (a name and its share): an item with \u0001 between the parts; the second column starts at one x, the separators in one column. On a narrow tile the two columns share its width, never narrower than their longest word, and wrap; the list never reaches past the tile's left edge (`safe center`).
- **A tile's title** stands with its first line at one level on every tile (a title of two lines has its rule, and what follows it, a line lower); its list or picture follows its rule. A frame's heading (a page of several sections) is in capitals, in the centre; the frames start and end where the Business System's do.
- **A name takes two rows at most** (`CardNameInput`), then scrolls. **A long name never covers its number**: on a tile (a card tile's title) or a card's head (a name that is a link) the number stays where it stands and the text slides behind it (`.section-tile-title-number` / `-text`; a link name is clipped at the number's right edge, `text-links.css`).
- **A tile's content may be linkable** (`SectionTile.links`, `SectionTile.cardFor`): a listed line opens its card (expenses, requirements, themes) or a section of its dialog (`{ box }`: the Registrations tile's three lines open that sub tile); a block of a tile's picture names its card with `data-card` (a colour, an image, a typeface). A press anywhere else on the tile opens the tile. Text on a tile scrolls like a list.
- **Sub tiles** (`SubTiles.tsx`) are category tiles one level in: a one-line capital title (a long one cut with an ellipsis that slides under the pointer, as every tile title; never a forced break), a list whose lines are links only where a line names a card (a requirement), a "+" (`SubTile.add`) where they take cards (Additional legal requirements). A sub tile with a "+" or circles keeps its list clear of them (`section-tile-slot-plus`, as every tile with a "+"). It lists EVERY item (no cap): where they do not all fit, the list scrolls, as every tile's list does.
- **The blocks of Colours and Brand Images** are squares with the same space around the set as between them; under the pointer a block takes the tile's own reaction (a 10% growth inwards, from the corner, edge or centre of its place, and the tile's drop shadow); a typeface is underlined.
- **A Business card tile** lists, under its name, where it belongs (plain lines, no bullets), then what it says (status, dates, warranty, cost) with bullets (`tileMarks`). A card that names a partner or supplier in a further status row is linked to it as well (a circle each).
- **A Business card tile lists its dates first** (`FloorRow.dates`): both days, or the Start date alone; "From" only where there is no End date and the cost runs on a schedule (monthly, quarterly, annually).
- **A Foundation card tile tells its card's core, a line each** (owner, 2026-11-19 16:52 UTC+2: "For each tile in the dialogue cards, check what core information they could carry on them as listed items"; `CardFaces.ts`): a Profile its age ("36 years old"), its kind of work and its first goal; a Competitor its Type, its reach and its size; an Offering its Type, its form, whether it can be bought today ("Available now", "Still being built") and how it is paid for -- each in its options' own words, as the card shows it (the effective answers, starts included); a line with no answer is left out. The other dialog tiles already list what they hold (the walk of 2026-11-19: Registrations, Expenses, the channel and relationship tiles, Demand, the resource and activity groups, the Delivery and Inventory cards, the funding and revenue cards, the Themes).
- **Small tiles (`small` on SectionTiles).** The Themes of 2. Goals & Objectives and the Roadmap keep the page's small tile size whatever their number (four are not drawn as the 2 x 2 of large tiles); the grid stands in the middle of its card.
- **Deactivated tiles** (a channel or subchannel not used yet) are drawn in the grey of every deactivated tile (`section-tile-completed`); pressing one makes it and opens it.
- **Tables with equal columns** (the Engagement Strategy) set the width on the header cells and put the gap in the cells' padding; labels shared by every column (Goal, Approach) stand in a row of their own.
- **A tile not used yet** (a channel, a subchannel the category offers) looks empty, as every tile with no card in it (`section-tile-slot-empty`): its ground half way to the page's, its raised edge and shade at half strength, its words in `--text-muted` (owner, 2026-11-22 19:14 UTC+2: "What I want to fix is the empty tiles, how they used to look like before"; rule | was | now: WCAG 1.4.3, 4.5:1 for the words of anything that can be pressed | the whole tile at half opacity until the morning of 2026-11-22 (its title 2.5:1), then faded by its ground and words alone, which read as a full tile | the edge and shade at half strength too, the words kept at 4.5:1); pressing it makes the card.
- **Values placed by the app** (`business-capability-static-value`) take the cell's height (44px), one line and an ellipsis with a tooltip.
- **Partner and Supplier tiles** begin with their dates, as Resource and Activity tiles do (`capabilityFloor.ts`).

---

- **A Capability's page** (owner, 2026-11-23 15:32 to 19:20 UTC+2; SystemSelection.tsx): the title is the Capability's name alone, in its colour, with its symbol; the label arrow beside it opens the Capability's description as a **pop-up of the title** (12px under its rule, from the page's left, half the page wide, over the chapters, never inside them), in a lighter tint than the frame it opens over (2.5% of the Capability's colour on `--bg-page`) with a 1px edge in that colour. The Systems stand in ONE chapter frame headed SYSTEMS in the Capability's colour, at the page's left, hugging its tiles: two by two for three or four Systems, else in one row. Each System tile: its name without its order number, its symbol under the title (12px under the title's rule, the same gap above and below), then a list headed PAGES, CHAPTERS or SECTIONS (underlined in the list's colour: `systemContents`), each line a link to its page or place, its bullet the line's status in the tile's hue; the System's own word is left off a line and an abbreviation carries its full words as a tooltip. The rail and the arrows, and the left and right keys, go round the six Capabilities.

## 10. Dialogs, floating cards, layers and the navigation stack

- **Layers**: page -> section view -> category view -> group view -> floating card -> (stacked pair). A card floats over the view it was opened from, which stays mounted under it (inert). **Back** and a click on the dimmed backdrop remove the TOP layer only; a dialog's Back / backdrop go one level up (`useDialogInnerBack`). **Escape** dispatches `CLOSE_TILES_EVENT`: every layer closes (a blank card is removed as on closing); a popup open inside takes its own Escape first.
- **Popups**: the "i" note is portaled to the dialog's overlay (else the body), so no clipping parent or `backdrop-filter` box cuts it; it keeps the hue of its button. **Pop-ups are six categories** (Interface 7, the pop-ups).
- **A popup stands above the card's own controls and arrows, and is placed again when its size changes**: the colour picker's container takes `z-index: 100` while it is open (its popup is inside the card's stacking context, where the card's x and the carousel arrows are later in the page and would otherwise paint over it), and `useFloatingPanel` observes the panel's size, so the HEX, RGB and HSL forms (which differ in height) are placed again, below or above their control, never over it.
- **Page-wide dialogs** (Inventory, Completed): from the top of the page cards down to 12px above the window's foot, over the footer's band (owner, 2026-11-12: "should reach the bottom part of the screen"), centred, their tiles scrolling inside the body down to 12px above the dialog's lower edge, as from its sides; the funnel's window opens beside the dialog where there is room, else inward under the funnel, and the dialog never narrows for it; a section dialog grows down.
- **Floating card**: no Back button; no `inert` on the page; the scrim (`.floating-overlay`) has `pointer-events: none`, the card and the notes in it `auto`; a `mousedown` outside the card (and outside a listbox/menu) closes the TOPMOST card (`FloatingCards`); a dialog's backdrop ignores a press while a card floats over it.
- **No dialog for a single card** (owner, 2026-11-14 13:54): a tile whose dialog would show nothing but its cards (`lone`: no suggestion, no "+", no other part) and holds ONE opens that card floating at once (`SectionTiles`, the shared rule of a tile with one card): a channel with one subchannel in From your answers with nothing suggested; an Offering with one relationship type where no "+" stands. A dialog with another part (Sales Forecast's Expected Sales, All's adds) keeps it.
- **A rail's tab keeps 12px from the lit bullet's grown edge** (owner, 2026-11-14 13:54): the lit bullet grows by `--rail-dot-grow` with a bloom of `--rail-bloom-width`, reaching `--rail-dot-overhang` past its 12px box (`bullet-rail.css`); a tab's padding is 12px plus that, and the gap round the arrow in the tab's list counts it too (`CarouselArrows`).
- A dropdown list (`OptionPicker`) inside `.business-capability`, a tile dialog/overlay or a sort menu is drawn free (`useFloatingPanel(..., free)`): fixed, window-placed, never clipped by a scrolling box; the cell's width, flipped above or capped and scrolling when there is no room.
- **The header over the layers:** with a dialog, floating card or Inventory open, `.app-header` has `z-index: 1001`, is never `inert`, and the element at its centre is its own (probe rule 17e2). A press in it does not close a floating card; Escape typed in it with a header popup open is the popup's.
- Declared exceptions added to the picker rule: the Category pickers (` category:`), an activity's Resource pickers (` resource N:`) and a rent's supplier name (` rent:`), like the Group and Name pickers already named: a long list or a name the user gave may be wider than the 212px cell.
- **A layer's tab stands behind its layer, edged in the layer's own colour** (a dialog's or a floating card's bullets and names, `z-index: -1` in the overlay, `--tab-edge` at full strength), as a chapter's page tab stands behind its frame; the layout probe checks both.
- **A page of tiles fits the window, never scrolls with nothing to show**: what adds to it without being a chapter stands in the footer's band, level with Back (Inventory and Completed on Infrastructure & Capability; "+ Add Segment" on Foundation, at the page card's left edge, from 1212 x 640px); so every page's chapters and master tiles are one size at a window size.
- **The page title stays in place**: the System's name, the page's and the line under them stand under the app header while the page scrolls (sticky, on the page's ground: `.interview-header`); a dialog or a floating card stands under that line, never over it.
- **A dialog over a card taller than the window** covers only what the window shows, under the header and the page title, above the footer (`dialogFrame`).
- **One card**: every floating card is `--open-card-width` (492px) wide, a founder's too (owner, 2026-11-19 01:41 UTC+2: "All the floated cards, should have the same width"; the shared floating card sets it, no caller passes a width), with the card ground, a hairline, 8px corners and the same padding; a colour's, a typeface's, a requirement's and an expense's card stand their rows on the name's columns, so everything under the name starts and ends where the name does. A drop-down is the app's own (OptionPicker), including a founder's Skills kind.
- **Every floating card has the card padding**: the card's own 12px inside its 1px edge, so the name cell, the cells and the x stand 13px from the card's outer edge, a Legal Entity card's included.
- **A card stands on its tile's tint** wherever it is drawn (floating over a tile or in a dialog): the Brand cards (a colour, a typeface, an image, the Text Hierarchy's box) too; the page's neutral card ground is for a card on the page only. A channel expense's x (take it off the channel) is twice its size under the pointer.
- **A floating card has one ground**: round, with its hairline and shadow; the tile content that holds it (`.column-tile-content`, `.brand-style`, `.brand-inventory`) has none, so no square box shows behind its corners.
- **A tile that holds several cards opens a dialog of them; a tile that holds one opens its card** (owner, 2026-11-13 04:58: "Tiles that could contain more than one floated card, should also open to dialogue cards"): its dialog shows a card tile for each (three a row, the shared card list with its Add Card where cards can be added), and each opens its floating card. A channel opens its subchannels so; an Offering in Customer's Relationship opens its relationship types so (a type's card shows that type alone); an Offering in Sales Forecast, one card, opens it at once.
- **A card is tinted by its tile**: a card floating over a tile, the "i" of a tile or a card, a Back button under the pointer, and the Text Hierarchy's box all take the tile's hue; a floating card is one box with 8px corners and one shadow. The hue is the one of the section whose tile opened it (the dialog's), whatever hue its own content carries (`--floating-hue`, section-tiles.css).
- **A dialog darkens the page behind it, a floating card does not** (`--bg-overlay`; `--floating-scrim` is clear). The names above the cards keep their own dark ground. Every floating card's box (Business, Legal Entity, Brand and Strategy) has the inner light and shade (`--box-inset`) and its drop shadow.
- **A dialog's header is one row** (`DialogHeading`): the root name (Resources, Activities) keeps its size; the names after it are one size lower (`--font-body`); a trail too long for the row is cut at its left (the last name stays in view, the full text in the tooltip).
- **The "i" takes its card's hue in every reading mode**: its stroke, letter and fill are set on the button itself (capability-edges.css), so they read the hue of the card or tile the button stands in (a dialog's hue is set on the card inside it, below where the pair used to be set, which made the "i" red on a green or blue card). The caption above a floating card keeps its own pair. Checked on the Business, Identity, Brand and Strategy dialogs and cards, in light, sepia and dark.
- **Carousel arrows** (`CarouselArrows`, SectionTiles.tsx): a "‹" at the left and a "›" at the right of the card or the opened section that was opened last, 44px circles over the page (fixed, measured from the box they flank). A floating card steps through the cards of its tile (of its box, where it was opened from one); an opened section steps through the section's tiles that have something to open; both round the ends. A dialog under a floating card has its arrows and its rail hidden: only the layer opened last shows them, whether or not the card has cards beside it, and the dialog's come back when the card closes (owner, 2026-11-17 00:22, "Hide it"). Alt + ← / → does the same. A parent and child pair has none yet. **The arrows never move**: both stand level with each other at the one arrow level ("One arrow level" below), on every dialog and card, whatever the page tab beside them holds; they stand over the tab (z-index 9, the tab 8).
- **One arrow level** (owner, 2026-11-18 21:34 UTC+2: "A: Level page arrows everywhere"; the arrows never move, 2026-11-09): every page arrow and every dialog's and floating card's carousel arrow has its centre at ONE height in a window, the same on every page (`arrowLevel`, `SectionTileGeometry.tsx`; the page arrows through `--arrow-level`, `PageArrow.tsx`). The rails do not follow the arrows: every rail starts 12px under its frame's top ("One rail for every layer"), and where a tab stands under its arrow the arrow takes one line's place in its list ("Page tab"). It is the level of the gap between the two rows of a page of four tiles at that window -- the pinned page title's underline where the title is one line (a long title that wraps, "Costs, Funding & Position" at 1366, has its further lines taken back), the frame's 12px gap and its head (61px), one tile as a page of tiles sizes it, half the gap between the rows -- worked out from the window, never measured from a page's own tiles, so no page (one chapter, three rows, no tiles) moves it. With no page title (the Handbook, the Workbook), where a page's title would end at that window -- the header's foot, and the title's 70px row under it where the title is not in the header -- so they stand at the pages' level too (owner, 2026-11-26 18:05 UTC+2: "The Navigation arrows should be at a specific level per screen size"; `pageHeadEnd`). The layout probe checks that the arrows shown are level and stand at the same height on every page of a window ("One arrow level").
- **On a Capability's page the arrows stand between the Systems' rows** (owner, 2026-11-23 19:16 and 19:48 UTC+2: "the navigation arrow to be in the mid between the Tiles"; "Can we be consistent and use the same sizes?"): the SYSTEMS frame is a chapter's frame and its tiles take the one master side (section 4, "The page's sizes"), so its rows stand where every page's do and the gap between them is at the one arrow level. The layout check's `checkCapabilityArrows` fails a page where they part by more than 2px.
- **The page's rails stand inside the title rule's ends** (owner, 2026-11-23 17:28 to 18:16 UTC+2: "Remove the PAGES and the names on the rails"; "Page symbol, rail bullets, and navigation arrows, should align in the centre"): a page rail is its bullets alone at every width, each name in its bullet's tooltip (`BulletRail`, always compact); the rails stand `--rail-inset` (54px) inside the ends of the page title's rule and the chapters between them (bullet-rail.css); the page's symbol, its rail's bullets and its arrow are centred on one vertical line.
- **The side panels and the layer ladder** (owner, 2026-11-23 17:28 and 17:43 UTC+2: "Align the Notes and Calendar titles in the middle"; "The headers shadow should shade both"; "check and fix the hierachy"): Notes (right) and the Calendar (left) are drawn beside the app header, in its layer, under its shadow; each panel's title is centred in a three-column head (`.notepad-head`), its rule level with the page title's rule (`useRuleLevel`); the page does not move or narrow when a panel opens. One ladder: the page's rails and arrows 21, a title's help 22, the Calendar 27, Notes 29, the header and its Menu 40, the overlays 1000 (`src/styles/layers.test.ts` checks it).
- **The Calendar's funnel is the shared `FunnelButton`** (owner, 2026-11-23: "Use only existing components!! The calendar funnel button does not match to the existing one"): outlined at rest, filled only when a choice differs from the defaults; its menu holds folded groups (`MenuHead`): Show, Dates (a `RadioChoice` of periods ending in **Other**, which opens the start and end dates), Calendars (the user's Calendar) or Include (an Organisation's), and the date presentation. The user's Calendar offers Month, Today, This week, This month and Next 30 days, and opens on the calendar named **User**.
- **Carousel arrows stand `CAROUSEL_ARROW_GAP` (12px) from the card or dialog**, in the card's ground outlined by its edge (rule 15); none while a card is being made (fresh); a dialog with sections of its own (Registrations) steps through them first (`useDialogInnerStep`), then goes on to the next tile.
- **Only the app header cuts a floating card**; it scrolls to the window's bottom.
- **A rail whose names wrap** shows its bullets alone (class `bullet-rail-compact`): the title and names are hidden and a name appears beside the bullet under the pointer.
- The carousel arrows are the card's ground outlined by its accent colour, and the page's own arrows are hidden while they show. Going back into a tile of sections opens its last section; from the Registrations dialog's grid the right arrow opens Registration Numbers.
- A floating card's overlay starts under the app header and scrolls: the header is the only edge that cuts a card.
- A picker's list starts at its cell's left edge and is as wide as its longest option (never narrower than the cell). Rail names appear inwards. The app header throws a drop shadow.
- Carousel arrows step within the deepest open level (sections, categories, groups) before leaving it.
- **A card opened from a floating card.** A tile inside a floating card (an Objective on its Goal's card) opens its own card in the same place (OpenCardContext is provided by the floating layer). A card alone shows its place in its list (Objective 3), and its x removes it (no blank put in its place).
- **A section of a dialog stands on the dialog** (the Value Proposition, the Segment's Description): no frame of its own.
- **The Value Proposition dialog reads in three steps**: the text (its draft written in by itself, section 6, "A draft is written in by itself") in an untinted band; then Emotional Needs and Social Needs side by side in the Segment Description's two columns (`business-segment-grid`, the 1px divider), a band each, the two one tinted band across as tall as the longer list; then Open items, untinted. The columns follow the dialog's width (the `card` container, as a founder's two-column rows): side by side from a dialog of 600px (656px at every window from 768 up), one under the other below it, where the run goes on band by band (Social untinted, Open items tinted). `business-proposition-needs`, 13-business-foundation.css.
- **The "i" note opens above its "i"** where there is room, below where there is none.
- **A floating card's ground is checked in all three themes** (light, sepia, dark): the card and the dialog it was opened from take their ground from the same hue, the card one step deeper; a card's own neutral ground (`--bg-card-tint`) is never drawn on it.
- **A card's navigation** (Goal, Objective, Metric with two or more under one parent): the left arrow under the "+", the right under the x, the bullet rail under the right arrow (one bullet each, this card's lit), sticky while the card scrolls; an arrow or a bullet goes to that card (scrolled to where it stands, opened where it is a tile's floating card). Outside the card, beside its x, a symbol of four arrows pointing at the centre shows the cards as tiles (the Metrics of an Objective switch to tiles; a Goal's or an Objective's own floating card is closed).
- **Global: arrows that step through a list carry a rail.** Under the carousel arrows of a card or a section dialog (`CarouselArrows`, SectionTiles.tsx), under the right arrow, a vertical rail has one bullet for each of the list (this one's lit); pressing a bullet goes there, and hovering it shows a tooltip with the name (a section's title; a card's group and number where the tile gives no name). Any new set of arrows that steps through a list gives the list's items (`CarouselStep.items`, `current`, `go`).
- **Arrows and rails 12px from the frame**: `--rail-offset` (12px, `bullet-rail.css`) places the Pages and Sections rails' bullets and the page arrows; `CAROUSEL_ARROW_GAP` (12, `SectionTiles.tsx`) places a dialog's or card's arrows. The same on every page.
- **Rails**: a card's carousel bullet names the card (`SectionTile.nameFor`, else the name its tile showed (`cardNames.ts`: every card tile registers its name as it draws), else the tile's line that opens it); a tile's bullet names its tile. The bullets run in the tiles' reading order. An inner level of a dialog (the categories of a section, the groups of a category, a dialog's sections) replaces the rail with its own tiles' names while it is open (`useDialogInnerStep(step, rail)`).
- **A card's bullet in the rail never says "Card 2"**: its name, else the line of the tile that lists it. **A floating card lacking an answer** says so in the FIELD it lacks (the cell, or the ticks of Offerings, flash red, with the line saying why) and by its red bullet in the rail -- never by a frame round the whole card; **the tile that holds an incomplete card keeps flashing red while the card is open** (a card just made and still open is not flagged). **A card floats in the hue of its tile** (Inventory and Completed cards take their origin's).
- **A card opened from the Inventory or the Completed tile is alone**: no carousel arrows and no rail, whichever page the card belongs to (a legal expense opened from the Inventory is asked for with `alone`).
- **A card opened from a link is that card alone**: a legal expense opened from a founder's Responsibilities (or from the Inventory) is the one card, over the card it was opened from: not with its requirement, with no arrows and no rail of its own, and the arrows and rail of the card under it are not drawn while it covers them (`.floating-overlay:has(~ .floating-overlay)`).
- **One rail for every layer** (owner, 2026-11-18 20:00, 20:01 and 20:13 UTC+2: "All rails should have same width, same side, in between dots, and top and bottom gap", "Make it part of the rail component", "Floated cards - Rails, only with dots and tooltips. Dialogue and Chapters - Rails with dots and names (full size) only when screen size permits"). The rail component (`CarouselArrows`) owns the whole geometry; no page, dialog or card passes a size or overrides it (`bullet-rail.css`, "Every layer's tab is one geometry"):
  - **Dots always.** A floating card's rail has its dots alone, each name in a tooltip, at every width. A dialog's rail has its names beside its dots (`.carousel-rail-named`) only where the room beside the dialog holds the named tab; else its dots alone, with tooltips.
  - **Names whole**: never cut, never wrapped. The named tab is one width for every dialog, `--rail-tab-named-width` (388px): the longest tile name lit ("Typography & Text Hierarchy", 320px with the bold tracking), its 12px gap, the 12px dot and the padding on both sides. With today's page width the names first show at a window of about 2176px.
  - **Two widths only**: the dots-only tab, `--rail-tab-dots-width` (the 12px dot and the padding, 53px), and the named tab.
  - **One padding** on every side, `--rail-tab-padding` (12px plus `--rail-dot-overhang`, 20.5px); **one row** of 16px with or without its name (the 12px dot and 2px above and below; a name takes no more of it); **18px between rows**, so **one pitch of 34px**; Back 12px under the last row; the tab starts 12px under its layer's top -- **every rail**, the page's Pages and Sections rails too, 12px under the top of the frame they are attached to (owner, 2026-11-19 12:52 UTC+2: "Why the rails (chapter/page and dialogue card) have different Top gaps", 13:04: "Fix it in the root"; they stood 24px under the arrow level); one placement for every rail (`useRailSlot`, shared by `BulletRail` and `CarouselArrows`): no caller sets an offset. **The page is a layer too**: while a dialog or a floating card is open, the page's rails and arrows stand hidden -- only the layer opened last shows its arrows and rail ("Carousel arrows") -- so no page rail shows beside or under a layer's tab.
  - **The page's rails too** (owner, 2026-11-18 21:49 UTC+2: "Check the page (chapter) rail width"): the Pages and Sections rails (`BulletRail`) take the same geometry -- the tab padding, the dots-only width, and the named width with each name one 16px row where the room beside the frame holds the named tab and every name fits whole (from a window of 2560 at today's page width; else the bullets alone, the names in tooltips). Was: 12px padding, 37px dots-only, as wide as their names (209 to 387px), 25px rows with names.
  - **A page rail sizes to its longest name lit** (owner, 2026-11-19 22:47 UTC+2, the SEGMENTS rail: the lit "2. Designers & Boutique Hotels" ran to the tab's edge): `BulletRail` measures every name as it is when lit (bold, with the bold tracking of section 6), its dot and gap, and the padding; the rail is the named width at least and as wide as that needs (`--rail-own-named-width`), so switching the lit item never changes its size; on the names' side, by the window's edge, it keeps 12px (the grown dot's overhang stays on the dot's side). Where the gutter cannot hold it, the dots alone. Was: the named width always, a lit name past the padding.
  - The layout probe checks the widths, the padding, the rows, the gaps, the top and that no name is cut or reaches past the padding when lit, the page's rails too ("Every layer's tab is one geometry", "The page's rails too", "a page rail holds its lit name inside its padding").
- **Scrolling is global** (`useScrollRules.ts`): the wheel over an open layer scrolls the layer (also over a dialog's blank body, where `overscroll-behavior: contain` stops the chain), never the page; **nor does the app scroll the page under an open layer**: the tile that opened a card is brought into view only inside a layer (`SectionTiles.tsx`; 2026-11-20: at 768, where the stacked page is taller than the window, a page tile scrolled into view slid the page under its card, off the row the card stands on); every scroller whose content is taller than itself (`main.conversation-content`, a dialog's body, a tall card's stack) gets `data-reserve`: 25vh of bottom padding; a dialog taller than the window (the Theme card too) is scrolled by its layer to a quarter of the window past its end (`--reserve-end`).
- **Page tab** (`bullet-rail.css`, `useRailTab.ts`; `.carousel-rail-tab`): a rail stands in a tab touching the frame (absolute, the dots-only or the named width: "One rail for every layer"), `--rail-left-*` / `--rail-right-*` colours read from the frames. **Its list flows round the arrow**: where the tab stands under an arrow, its lines (the bullets, then Back) run in order at their own pitch and the arrow takes exactly one line's place among them: the line that would reach it continues right after it, with the list's own gap above and below the arrow; to keep those gaps the list is set down by less than one pitch (`CarouselArrows.tsx`, `slot`). No line is hidden and the list has no other gap; a tab that ends above the arrow is unchanged. The probe checks that the two arrows are level, that nothing covers an arrow and that no line of the tab lies under one.
- **A tab follows the reading mode and takes its edge from the layer's ink where the layer's hairline is a translucent light**; a name in it never changes width (it is lit by a shadow, not a heavier weight) and keeps 12px from its bullet (the item's gap). The Back button of a dialog stands in its tab after the last option, and the dialog's foot has none while the tab does.
- **A floating card's page tab is placed by the card's frame** (12px under its top), never by the box name that stands above it; its ground is the first fully opaque box inside the card's body (a caption pill or a tooltip is never one), so Identity, Brand and Business cards all have their tab.
