- **A page tab keeps one place**: its top is the top of the layer's frame **with every scroll undone** (the layer's own scroll and the window's, where the layer moves with the page; `titleTopOf` in `CarouselArrows.tsx`, which remembers its last measure to tell a layer that moves with the page from one fixed to the window), kept inside the window (under the app's header, above the window's foot). It therefore does not move when the dialog is scrolled or re-measured, and never ends under the header or over the page title. Its width is the rail's own: the dots-only tab, or the named tab where a dialog's names show ("One rail for every layer", above); a name is never cut.
- **Where the arrow stands in the tab, the bullets AND the Back button continue below it**, and the arrow over a tab is the tab's edge colour in a brighter version (55% of it mixed with white), in every reading mode.
- **The Back in a layer's tab is built like a rail item** (`.carousel-rail-back`): an arrow box as wide as a bullet (12px, the arrow on the bullets' line), the item's 12px gap, then the word in capitals; at rest in the tab's edge colour (the card's mid tone); under the pointer and the focus in the colour of the card's "+" box (`--plus-fill`), bold (a shadow and the bold tracking, so the word never changes width), and the arrow grows 20% forward; no underline. A dialog with no tab has its Back at its foot (`.section-tile-back`): a "<" and the word in the default ink, which take the tile's tint and go bold under the pointer. The Back of a layer is described in the contract `src/library/assets/backButton.ts`.
- **The page tab stands on the side of its layer nearer the window's edge** (a layer in the left half: at its left, mirrored; a dialog's names beside the bullets only where that side holds the named tab, else in tooltips; a floating card's always in tooltips). Every page tab starts 12px under the top of the frame it is attached to (a floating card's own frame, a dialog's panel, the page's first or last chapter frame for the Pages and Sections rails; owner, 2026-11-19), its arrow taking one line's place in its list where the tab stands under it.

---

## 11. Business cards: Infrastructure & Capability, Customer Engagement

- Cards' x (`.card-remove`, `.profile-card-remove`) is `--bg-danger-strong`; a cell's or field's x keeps `--x-red`.
- **A channel card's Journey Stage and its x** (owner, 2026-11-15 15:01): the stage's words (a record line) and its x on one centre line (`.business-subchannel-card .business-channel-pick-stage`, `align-items:center`); the layout check: "a channel card's Journey Stage and its x are on one centre line".
- **A relationship type's unfolded stage** (owner, 2026-11-15 15:01): each channel's name semi-bold (600, with the bold tracking), its subchannels in the body weight; a bold line (2px, the card's divider colour) under the stage's row, edge to edge, while it is unfolded.
- **An Other written in is a numbered list** (owner, 2026-11-15 16:43), on every question that ends in Other (C2, C3, C4, Box 4, 7a and 8a, O20 to O22): the shared `NumberedListInput`, one name a row, as "Tasks she wants done".
- **A subchannel named under a Profile's Other is said as any listed one** (owner, 2026-11-20 18:35 and 18:38 UTC+2: "exactly like the other channel cards"): Substack or WhatsApp groups, written in a Profile, head their card as Pinterest does -- the name as the bold title, the x beside it -- never as a cell to type in; the Profile is where the name is changed. Rule | was | now: a written-in subchannel is named in its card | a name cell, asked again | the Profile's name, said (`useEngagementChannelBoxes`, `journeySuggestions`).
- **The cards of a list share one left and one right edge** (owner, 2026-11-16 14:24): every line of a numbered list stands between the same two edges; a link line keeps inside its card's rounded corners (the connection lines take the card's own corners). **An action's card holds two lines**: "1.", its text and its x on the top line; its order number and its marks on one bottom line, clear of its foot (`.numbered-list-row.is-action-line`). The layout check: "the cards of a list share one left and one right edge", "a card's link line keeps inside its rounded corners", "an action card's order number and marks share one bottom line" and "... stand clear of its foot", in a subchannel's card opened on Customer Engagement in each theme.
- **An Other's list shows an empty name row at once**, focused when Other is ticked (`autoFocusFirst`), in the list's own field look.
- **A subchannel's tile title may take two lines** (owner, 2026-11-15 16:42; a declared exception to the one-line tile title): wrapped, then cut by an ellipsis, its whole name the tooltip (`.subchannel-tiles`). The tile lists only the Offerings the subchannel serves.
- **Shaded once, and only where nothing shades it already** (`measureShade`, `data-tile` on the dialog): (a) over a chapter: the chapter's frame, with its corners; (b) over the Inventory's or the Completed's tiles: the whole tiles that lie behind the card, as one rounded block, or, with no tile behind it, the card and 12px round it; (c) beside a category's dialog: the dialog's own dim, the card adds none. Fixed to the window, measured again on scroll; a press on it closes the card.
- **The shade behind a floating card is a shadow, not a solid colour** (owner, 2026-11-18 21:50 UTC+2: "It should be a shadow, not a solid colour!"; it replaces the solid cover of 2026-11-15 16:42): the shared shade (`.floating-chapter-shade`) is the shadow tone once (`--shadow-18`), see-through, over the chapter's frame, its size and corners, **never blurred** (owner, 2026-11-18 22:36 UTC+2: "do not blur the background"): the chapter shows through it; the rest of the page clear.
- **No shade over a shade** (owner, 2026-11-18 22:05 UTC+2: "When the background has already shadow, and open a floated card, does not need an extra shade"): a card opened from a dialog of a category stands over a chapter the dialog's own dim already shades, and adds none (`measureShade`, `FloatingCards.tsx`; (c) of "Shaded once, and only where nothing shades it already", above). The layout probe fails a chapter shade while such a dialog is open.
- The link line of a connected card (`.business-capability-card-link`) is a flex row: prefix, the name (a button the ellipsis cuts), closing quote; one line.
- One resource card (`resourceCard`); the Workspace is a category of it (its fields stand in the card flow like the others').
- A new resource card is filed under `unassigned` (Category) / `unassigned` (Group): placeholder "Select", incomplete until chosen.
- A new Activity / Partner / Provider card starts at Category "Select" (incomplete till chosen), like a Resource.
- **Resources rows (activities)**: a row is a dropdown (`OptionPicker`) with the link symbol, one row per resource, "+" adds a row; never a tick list. Team use the same row. A people card in Offering's Capability uses `.business-capability-people` in `section-tiles.css`.
- **Group label order in a card**: Category, Group, Name. A partner or supplier card has one Group dropdown and no Category row (see "Group, not Category" below).
- **Cost row additions** (cost option, billing type, rate override, useful life, warranty, amortisation period, payment plan, extras, dated notes) sit inside the existing cost row on the same two-column label rhythm (`--space-field`, `--space-label`); the rate override's placeholder shows the Global Settings default.
- **An activity's Resources list** is a list of rows like the Team's (`.business-capability-resource-row`): dropdown, units box (56px, `.business-foundation .interview-input.business-capability-people`), link symbol (36px), and the "+" or × in the head's x column. Every row keeps the places of the units and of the link (`.business-capability-row-spacer`), so the dropdowns of a list end at one x; the field extends into the x column by the column's width and the gap (`margin-right`). The estimated cost of each linked card is a muted line under the list (with two or more, then the sum), never beside the row. The check tests it as "every Resource dropdown has one left and one right edge" and "the + or the x beside a Resource stands in the head's x column".
- **The Team's link symbol**: beside a partner or supplier performer, between the dropdown and the "+" or ×; the dropdown then ends one link place (36px and the 8px gap, 44px: `--link-slot`) before the name cell's right edge, in every row of the list.
- **One label column** for the Team and the Responsible stacks of an activity (`--activity-label-column`, = `--card-label-col`, 177px), so the Team's dropdown and the Responsible's name start at one x.
- **The link symbol stands at the LEFT of its dropdown.** It is drawn first in the row and its slot (36px and the 8px gap, `--link-slot`) hangs out to the left of the dropdown into the label column, which is `--card-label-col` wide (177px) in an activity. Every picker still starts at the column's edge; a row without a link keeps the empty slot (`.business-capability-row-spacer`). It applies to the Team, the Resources of an activity and the Name rows of a status. A founder performer has a link too (it opens the founder's card).
- **The Resources of an activity**: the tick, the label and the first dropdown are on ONE line (`OptionalField` with `inline`, subgrid of the activity stack); the dropdown starts at the Team's and the Responsible's x. The cost lines stand under the dropdown.
- **End date is optional**: a tick before its label (`BusinessField` with `tick`); the date field shows once ticked, and unticking clears the date.
- **Linked Offerings**: with several, the percent each carries is a whole number of two digits (`.business-capability-share`, 56px) at the end of its own row, its right edge at the "+" and x column. The old rows below the list (a wide box and a text) are gone.
- **Group, not Category, on a partner or supplier card** and on a resource's Partners & Suppliers status: ONE Group dropdown listing every group of the place (Partners' first, then Suppliers'); the group picked says which of the two the card is. The Type field of a Pre-Operational activity is removed.
- **The payment plan** (`.business-capability-plan-row`): a payment is one row, amount | percent | date (an Instalment's order number before them: `1.5em 88px 80px 1fr`). Deposit & Balance: two rows. Instalments: a one-digit box (`.business-capability-plan-count`, 56px) beside the type makes that many numbered rows. Milestones: two rows each, the stage with the "+" (an x after the first) and then the values. The amount and the percent are one figure (typing either sets the other); the total must be 100%.
- **A card's cells start at one left edge.** An activity's Start date, End date and Group start where its Team and Responsible do (`--side-label-short` 177px for the card). A founder's Start date: its label ends where the Salary cell ends, its cell starts where the Frequency cell starts (`--founder-cell`, 108px, for both). A Legal expense's Date, Cost and Frequency stand in one row, each label over its cell: the Date is the date cell's own width (`--control-date`, 160px), the Frequency as wide as its longest option (`hug`), the Cost takes the rest, `--space-cell` (12px) between them; its Responsible row stands on the same columns, the tick and its words in the Date's column, the picker from the Cost's left edge to the Frequency's right edge.
- **"Responsible" is the same field on every card**: a tick (on until taken off; off clears the choice) and the picker beside it (`BusinessField` with `tick`); the Legal expense's "Responsibility" is now that field, shown where there is more than one founder.
- **One set of measures for a card's fields** (`global.css`): `--card-label-col` (177px, the label column of every side-label row, so Start date, Group, Team, Responsible and Frequency start their cells at one x on every Business card; the Revenue Stream (10.5em, Questions 20), Delivery and Customer Source (13em) and founder (8.8em) cards keep their own documented columns, the owner's dated decisions, 2026-11-19 reconciliation), `--card-cell-gap`, `--card-cell-narrow` (108px, a founder's Salary | Frequency pair) and `--card-x-col` (the delete x's column). A card's content runs from the name's left edge to its right edge; a founder's card, with no name cell, keeps the same left (after the number) and right (the x's column clear).
- **Links between cards, both ways**: an activity's Team, Responsible and Resources rows open the founder, partner or resource (a pair of floating cards); a partner's card lists the activities that name it as performer, a resource's card the activities that list it, each a link.
- **An optional field is a tick box** (`OptionalField`): the Legal requirement's Reference or Number, like its Description; a founder's Skills kind drop-down is one width on every row, the empty one included.
- **Links between cards, checked in every direction** (Lumen & Loom, 62 cards): a resource links to its supplier, to the activity its status chooses and to the activities that list it; an activity to its performers, its Responsible founder, its partners and its resources, and back to the resource that chooses it; a partner or supplier to every resource that names it (by its status or a further status row) and every activity it performs; a founder's Responsibilities to its activities; a Customer Channel's card to its channel; a tile's circles show what a card is connected to. Each opens the other card with this one (a pair).
- **The "Open ..." links of a card are last in it** (`linksSection`): one is the line itself; several are a folded section named Links, its label at the left with the other labels, the fold arrow after it and the count beside it. A card's circles (the linked indicators on its tile) match its links: every activity a resource is listed in, and every resource an activity lists, is a circle on both tiles (`floorRows`).
- **The Links fold arrow stands at the left of its label**, hung in the margin, so the label starts where the card's other labels do.
- **The Links of a card are laid out as a founder's Responsibilities**: the same row (fold arrow, label, count) and the list under it, its lines 6px apart.
- **A link line is a kind letter, then the name**: R. Resource, A. Activity, P. Partner, S. Supplier (the noun's first letter, one fixed column), then the name as the link; no "Open", no quotes (its tooltip and accessible name keep the full "Open Resource “Name”").
- **Every cell of a card is `--card-cell-height` (44px) high**, a date cell included. A Status picker is narrower than the other side-label cells on purpose: its "+" stands in the x column (159 + 40 = 199).
- **A Legal requirement's or expense's floating card stands on the Business card's geometry**: the same three columns, name cell and side-label rows (label in `--card-label-col`, cell to the content's right edge). A colour's and a typeface's card share one label column, as wide as its longest label ("Colour (i)"), then 12px, then the cells, which both end at the name cell's right edge. A card a Customer Channel made shows its Category and Group as text where the other cards' cells stand.
- **Legal Entity links**: an expense shows a link symbol beside "Linked registration", a requirement beside "Add to Legal Entity Expenses", a linked number a symbol hung at its left (`OPEN_EVENT` opens the other card or section). On the tiles a link is a circle (the tile's circles at its lower left), never a line made a link: the Registration Numbers tile's lines name no card, so they are plain lines and a press on them opens the tile.
- **A legal card floats on 3. Infrastructure & Capability** (a founder's "L." link, an Inventory tile): the Legal Entity editor is mounted with its tiles not drawn (`cardsOnly`) and asked for the card (`OPEN_EVENT`); the card stands over the page's second column, and what is typed is saved to the Identity System's answer (`changeLegalEntity`).
- **A linked requirement and expense (Legal Entity) are drawn together only where a link was pressed** (the link symbol, a card's link); opened by its own tile or box, a card is alone.
- **The cost row**: the currency cell is 66px (its left edge stays at the label column), the Frequency takes the rest of the row, to the content's right edge.
- **A frequency belongs to its group**: a card offers only what its group offers (a card with no group the groups of its master tile together); a card that CHANGES its category or group starts its Frequency again ("Select", or the only one its new group offers; its unit and a named "other" with it, the price stays), and one the group lacks, found on an older card, moves to the group's first (Interface 4, the frequency options). A hand-made landlord or lessor card shows its group's own extras.
- A side-label cell (Category, Group, Responsible, Status) keeps its one fixed width and one line; a sole-option Group stays a disabled cell like the Category's, never plain text.
- Linked resources of an activity: one line each (name left, cost right), "no price yet" where none; the Total ends the list. Rate override is optional (a tick).
- Payment plan rows: the cells stand in the columns of the cost row above them: [amount: from the content's left edge (after an Instalment's 18px order number) to the Price's right edge] [percent: a 44px square (like the instalments' box) from the currency's left edge, its "%" sign (14px, `.business-capability-plan-percent-sign`) after it] [the date cell, 160px, to the content's right edge]; 12px between columns; a milestone's name runs the card's name cell with its "+" in the x column. The date cell is 160px everywhere (the Start date cell), so in the 480px card it starts 15px left of the Frequency.
- **Payment plan squares and Milestones:** the Instalments' number and a payment's percent are the one square cell (`.business-capability-years`, `--control-square`, 44px), the same as the Rate override's. A Milestones plan starts with TWO milestones (as Deposit & Balance has two rows); each milestone has its order number ("1.") before its name, and its amount, percent and date row stands in the Instalments' columns under it (an empty 18px cell in the number's place). Equal instalments are split to one decimal (3 payments: 33.3, 33.3, 33.4), so the figure fits the square; a longer figure typed by hand shows as far as the square holds.
- **A link in a founder's Responsibilities** (an activity, a partner, a supplier or a legal expense) opens the linked card ALONE from the founder's card in the Founders' dialog (the dialog already shows the founder: `openPair(founderId, id, true)`, `openLegalExpense(id)`), and BELOW the founder's card from the founder's FLOATING card (the two stacked: `openPair(founderId, id)`, `openLegalExpense(id, founderId)`).
- **An empty card is taken away when it is left**: a card added by a "+" and left blank is removed when its floating card closes (`TileAdd.isBlank`); a Theme left with no name, no description and nothing under it is removed when its dialog is left (`SectionTile.discardIfBlank`); a Goal, Objective or Metric left for another card or for the tiles while it is the same as a new one is removed (`useDiscardBlank`, `groupView.ts`). The last card of a group stays.
- **A card made by another card's Status** (a Partner, Supplier or Activity made with "Other…") carries `madeBy` (the parent's id), starts on the parent's Start date, and serves the Offerings its parent serves, following them as they change (`withInheritedOfferings`).
- **A change that leaves a generated card with no use** (the parent's Status, Activity, Partner or Supplier changes, or the parent is removed, and no other card names it) is asked about first; Continue removes the generated card, Cancel changes nothing. What goes with a change on the screen (a name box opening) follows only once it is made (`onChange(next, after)`).
- **A generated card adds, never removes**: it serves what the cards connected to it serve (the card that made it, every other card that names it), the user may add others, and nothing a connected card serves is taken off it (the ticks keep them); what it gains never changes those cards (`connectedOfferings`, `withInheritedOfferings`).
- A requirement ticked "Add to Legal Entity Expenses" makes one expense (named as it is, text-link name, no Linked registration); the two open together, the requirement above. A linked registration number carries its link symbol inside the right of its cell.
- Activity costs: the partners and suppliers named as Team are costed after the last performer; a rule stands over the Total.
- Customer Engagement: "Channel" is the old Channel Category and "Subchannel" the old channel; "Channel expenses" has Cost (currency in its heading), Frequency, and a Channel column of channel / rule / subchannel.
- Customer Relationship: per relationship type a block (type beside its label, Purpose, then the Engagement Strategy with the stages side by side, each Goal with its ticks under the label); "+ Add Relationship Type" follows the last block.
- Channel expenses: "Channel Expense" column; a percentage's symbol is in the Frequency column ("% of Rev") and its behaviour is Variable; several subchannels are a bullet list; actions two to a row.
- **A channel opens as tiles of its subchannels**, each opening a card with the table of that one subchannel (1300px or more wide, the width the table needs).
- **A subchannel's card** names the subchannel in its table head ("Instagram"); its channel's row and the "+ Add Subchannel" row are not drawn; its x removes it.
- **Goal & Approach** are one title with an "i" beside each word; the Engagement Strategy has no row of labels.
- **A Delivery card follows the opened card's columns** (marker, content, x): everything under its head starts at the name's left edge; each person's hours stand in the label column and cell column of the card's other side-label rows. (This replaces the earlier exception that left the Delivery card out.)
- **A subchannel made by pressing a tile not used yet** opens its card at once and, left with no name, no link and no stage, is taken away when the card is closed.
- **A page of small tiles on a wide card** (the Roadmap) keeps the 215px tile of every page. **Sales Forecast**: the channels that bring the customers stand in a row of their own under each Offering's sources (the ticks in a line), not in a column of the table.
- **Order number inside the name cell (every opened card)**: the number is drawn over the left of the name cell (10px in) and the name's text starts at 38px; the marker column is gone (`--card-marker-col` and `--card-marker-gap` 0, `--card-x-column`; the x column measures 44px: the 32px x and 12px after the name cell, 2026-11-19 reconciliation), so the name cell and everything under it start at the card's padding and the cells widen by the 40px the marker column took. The list of card kinds and their heads: `docs/ai/08-card-kinds.md`. The founder's card (no name cell) keeps its marker edge.
- **Channel card without wires**: the parts of the card (Offerings, a Journey Stage with its Actions, links, expenses) have no border or padding; they stand on the card's 12px, end where the name cell does (44px before the card's padding: the 32px x and a 12px gap, 2026-11-19 reconciliation; a stage's × stands in that column), 12px apart (6px between a stage's picker and its actions).
- **A category with one group** shows that group's tile (`SubcategoryBoxes`), so arrow navigation into it never makes a blank card.
- **Channel card**: Offerings are tick boxes (`BusinessTicks`: "All Offerings" first, at least one ticked); "Links" has its cell under its label; labels and part labels are the global label style; a name the app wrote is 18px semi-bold.
- **A channel and the cards it made share its Offerings**: ticking Offerings on the channel writes them to its Resource, Partner and Provider cards (their "Linked to" is locked there); "All Offerings" is ticked whenever every Offering is.
- **A channel card is divided into sections by one tint**: Offerings and Links/Expenses on the card's ground, the Actions between them on 12% of the card's label colour, edge to edge, straight lines.
- **A delete x of a block (a Relationship Type) stands at its top right corner**, 12px from both edges.
- **A channel card's Expenses section** keeps the card's margins: its content and its divider run between two edges 12px from the card's sides (the x column included); every block under the heading -- an expense, the shared ones, the cost line -- is 12px under the one above.
- **The dividers of a channel card** run the band's whole width (inside the card), are all 1px, and have 12px over and under them (vertical margin only).
- **A channel card's name** (said or typed) starts at the card's content edge (12px from its side, where the labels start; a cell's text is inset as every cell's is); "Other" is a text holder; a subchannel shows no order number; its stage picker hugs its longest option and stands at the right, 12px from the x.
- **"# Link an action" lists the actions of the resource's own channel first** (in its stage order), then the other channels' (in the table's order), up to 12; a typed number comes first.
- **The add-expense list** is: Blank expense, then the channel's typical costs one by one (no "all" option), then ONE group "Link existing" (the company's own cards, in the default blue; pointing at one says which channels it already serves). An option under the pointer gets bolder.
- **The name of a card made automatically and linked to its source** (an expense made by a requirement, a card that opens its source) is in the default blue, plain at rest, bolder and underlined under the pointer.
- **A Journey Stage cell and its Action cells are one part, top down: 6px between.** An action cell has the default cell hover (primary edge and soft glow) and the focus ring.
- **A channel (Social Media) has subchannels (LinkedIn, Facebook ...).** A new subchannel card's top cell is a drop-down only of its channel's subchannels that are not used yet (aligned right, 12px from the x); "Other" turns it into a text holder (focused) for the user's own name; a name typed that is not in the list stays a text holder; a name from the list is said. No order number is shown for a subchannel.
- **A subchannel's tile** is ONE tile per subchannel name; its lines are the Offerings, each a link to the card that serves it (numbered 1., 2. ... where the subchannel has several cards, the Offerings of one card sharing a number) or, faded, a press that makes a card of that subchannel for it. A new card's name cell is a drop-down only ("Other" opens a text holder).
- **A subchannel is used once by an Offering, but may have many cards** (each with its own actions and costs): on a card, the Offerings another card of the same subchannel already serves are blocked -- grey, unticked, "Already uses this subchannel" beside them -- and "All Offerings" is blocked while any is.
- **The action cell being typed in** has the common focus frame: 2px in the primary colour drawn inside the cell, with the default soft glow.
- **A Customer Relationship block's first row is one row**: [Relationship Type label + its drop-down] [Purpose label + its cell, the rest of the width] [x], level with the cells' first line, 12px apart.
- **A channel card's Actions and Expenses headings** have the "+" at the right (above the x column), a full-width 1px divider under the heading with 12px over and under the "+" button, no "Add ..." button, and the divider under "Expenses" only where an expense stands under it.
- **A legal card has one ground**: the tile content around it has none (no square box behind its round corners).
- **x column 44px** (`--card-x-column`): a card's x stands in its own column, 32px wide and 12px from the cell beside it, 12px from the card's edge; an x never lies on a cell (the layout probe's "a remove x keeps 12px from the cell beside it and never lies on one"). A channel card's parts are tinted bands (`--part-tint-a`, `--part-tint-b`) with a hairline between Journey Stages.

- **The Customer Engagement page: its tables and the action markers** (written 2026-10-17, updated 2026-10-29): The layout of the Customer Engagement page: its tables, the Segment card, the action markers and Sales Forecast. This is the page's specification. The rules of the rulebook (sizes, gaps, the one x, the reactions) apply to everything here.
- **The Customer Channel table**: "1. Customer Channel" and "2. Customer Relationship" hang their fold arrows in the Segment card's arrow column (under its arrow), so their numbers stand at the Segment's number. In the Customer Channel table every cell (heads, category rows, nested channels, cells) has 12px (`--space-field`) between its edge and what stands in it, at the sides and top and bottom. While that table has its four columns (its box 1300px or wider) JOURNEY STAGE and CHANNEL are FITTED to their dropdowns: the Journey Stage picker is as wide as its longest option ("Support & Follow Up", hugging, sized for every stage so it does not change width as stages are chosen) and its column exactly that picker, the 12px gap, the x and the 12px cell padding at both sides; the Channel column fits the widest category or channel name preset ("Booking / Registration Page", the nested mark, the "i" and the x beside it) the same way; the Journey Stage column is also never narrower than its "+ Add Journey Stage" button on ONE line (the button's measured width, 272px at 18px, and the cell padding at both sides: 299px). A stage row is ONE block: the dropdown FILLS the track from the cell's 12px padding to the x (never narrower than its longest option; it does not hug), the x 12px from it, and the Add button spans from the dropdown's left edge to the x's right edge, the same widths on every row; OFFERINGS and ACTION share the rest, 45% / 55%. The widths are MEASURED in the font the table is drawn in (`useChannelColumns.ts`, sums in `ChannelTableColumns.ts`, set as `--col-channel`, `--col-stage`, `--col-offerings`, `--col-action` on the table and again on resize), never guessed; no picker shows a cut word. They are measured from a hidden copy of one stage row, so they are the same whether or not any category is open (a table with every category folded has no stage row to read) and the Channel Category picker ("Physical Delivery", the widest) never wraps in its category row. Narrower, the table stacks as before. The Customer Relationship table keeps its own columns.
- **The Channel resources table**: Under that table, "Channel resources" is a fold of its own (the section-head pattern: arrow in the card's arrow column, caps title, the table starting at the title's text; open by default, not drawn when the Segment has no channel resource): a read-only table (`.channel-resource-table`, derived by `buildChannelResourceTable`, never stored or counted) on the channel table's ground, hairlines and 8px corners, 12px (`--space-field`) from every cell's edge to its text, text wrapping, scrolling only inside its own box under 1050px; its columns are Resource, Cost, Behaviour, Channel, Offerings, Journey stages and Actions (no Status, "Where it lives" or "Also reaches" column). A cell holding two or more values (the Channel cell: the channel the resource was made on first, then each other channel it shows in through an action; Offerings; Journey stages) lists them one under the other with a thin line between them, no bullets, each line across the whole cell (`.channel-resource-lined`; owner, 2026-11-14 19:08 and 19:09), every item styled alike; a single value is plain text at the cell's padding (`.channel-resource-single`) so the text of every cell of a column starts at ONE x. The Actions cell has NO bullets: its "#N" buttons sit side by side and wrap between buttons (`.channel-resource-chips`), each button one line (`white-space:nowrap`). Column widths come from the content (`.channel-resource-col-*` minimums; Cost and Behaviour on one line); the table fits 1600px with no inner scroll and wraps inside its box below that. A row's name carries the resource's warm colour (4px stripe and dot) and a hover wash, and its footer line is a summary of the rows, not a forecast (its Offerings column names only the Offerings the card links, "—" for none or for an Infrastructure / Workspace card, never "All Offerings").
- **The Customer Relationship table**: In the Customer Relationship table, an Offering's block starts at the "1." of "1. OFFERING -" (the PROFILES: row and the Relationship column's cells); the kind word and "PROFILES:" (colon right after, 16px before the first link) are caps titles, and the Offering's name is an underlined link to it on the Foundation page, like a Profile's name; a Relationship Type's arrow stands under the Offering's (the band's 12px inset, the same 36px arrow, its 4px gap), and its picker [x], then its Purpose, then "+ Add Relationship Type" (from the picker's edge to the x's) start at that number; those cells stand 12px apart (the Purpose's label 6px from its box) and the x 12px from its picker.
- **The Engagement Strategy**: The Engagement Strategy is two parts (the table's second and third columns): the Journey Stage and its Goal / Approach. The stage band (fold arrow, name) and the GOAL (the "Goal" label with its "i", then Get / Keep / Grow) are cells of ONE row, the Goal centred on the band's 36px line like the stage name (mid-line rule), in the band's ground; the Approach (label and text box) follows under the Goal in the same column, beside the kind-of-channel lists. Under a journey stage, the kind of channel ("Social Media") and its list start at the stage's TEXT ("Discover", past its fold arrow and gap): the label's text and each bullet's left edge stand on that x. A stage's fold arrow folds ONLY those kind-of-channel lists; the Goal and the Approach stay (a folded stage keeps one row, with the Approach in the second part). Every Journey Stage band STARTS FOLDED (the item folds, `useItemFolds`: closed until opened; the choice is kept for the session as the other item folds are).
- **The Segment card**: The Segment card has NO head row of its own: its fold arrow (which still folds the whole card) stands in the card's arrow column next to "1. SEGMENT", the first reference column's heading, and the card's body (the references, then the two sections) starts at the body edge, as under any head. The references stand straight on the card in no box and have no Show / Hide button: three columns, 12px apart (`--space-column`), SEGMENT (the card's number before it, "1. SEGMENT", number then word at the 8px head gap), PROFILES and OFFERINGS, all caps titles, headings on one line and baseline; every arrow, the Segment's too, starts folded. Each column has ONE left edge: its title's text ("1." / PROFILES / OFFERINGS), every link's text and every details block's text (headings and paragraphs) start at it. Each link's fold is the ordinary 36px fold arrow at the RIGHT of the link's text (8px after it, centred on the link's FIRST line; if the name wraps it stays on that first line, at the link box's right edge, and never follows the name or the unfolded details down). Its right edge never passes the column's right edge, which is 12px (`--space-column`) short of the next column's hanging rule (the last column, Offerings: 12px short of the Segment card's right side, inside its border -- the same 12px rule): the link is at most the column less `--reference-arrow-room` (36px + 8px) and wraps inside that. Not an arrow in front of it, so no arrow column is reserved; the details stand directly under the link, and their vertical rule is not content: it hangs 12px before the text edge (the Segment's in the card's arrow column; the Profiles' and Offerings' in a 12px strip reserved in their tracks). The three contents are equal in width, the columns 12px apart. Folded, the card is one row: its arrow, "1. SEGMENT" and the Segment's name (so it stays identifiable); its accessible names are the arrow's "Segment N" and the card's group name "Segment N: name".
- **Action markers**: Code: `ActionMarkers.tsx`, `ActionOrder.ts`, `numbered-list.css`. The markers are drawn in the Customer Channel table.
  - In an Action list the x (32 x 32) is pinned to the box's right edge on every row, 6px in from the top and right; the row's symbols stand under it (below).
  - SHAPE says the kind of tie, COLOUR says which one. A SHARED symbol (link icon) stands on every row of an action placed in more than one row; a LINKED-TO-RESOURCE symbol (tag icon) on every action linked to a resource (a resource's own card has NO symbol: its summary line says "no actions", "action" or "N actions" in words). Each shared action takes one of six COOL hues (`--shared-hue-1..6`: teal, blues, violets; position among the Segment's shared actions in table order), each resource one of six WARM hues (`--resource-hue-1..6`: amber, orange, rust, red, rose, pink; position among the Segment's linked resources), as `.tint-shared-N` / `.tint-resource-N` (global.css; the card-family tokens `--tint-ink`, `--tint-mark`, `--tint-ground`, `--tint-wash` from `--theme-*`, never a per-theme value). Glyphs and lines >= 3:1 and chip text >= 4.5:1 in light, sepia and dark (greens are left out of the palettes: they fall under 3:1).
  - PAST SIX the hues come round in a PATTERN with a contrasting second colour (`--pattern-alt`, colors.css: dark ink in light / sepia, light ink in dark, >= 3:1 on the surface, as every hue's ink is; `looks.test.ts`): the 7th to 12th place in diagonal 4px STRIPES (`.tint-pattern-stripes`: the hue against `--pattern-alt`, repeating-linear-gradient 45deg, 4px bands), the 13th to 18th in a CROSS-HATCH (`.tint-pattern-hatch`), then round again (`markHue`, `markPattern`, `markTint`, `connectionPaint` in ActionOrder.ts; the paint is `--tint-fill`). The pattern is on the symbol (a 2px ring of it round the glyph), the 4px lines, the resource card's stripe, its chips' left edge and the Channel resources table's stripe and dot.
  - The same colour is on the symbol, on a 4px CONNECTION LINE inside the action cell (shared action: the RIGHT edge, identical on every row it stands in; resource: the LEFT edge, one segment per resource, split top to bottom in equal parts when an action has two or more; both lines when it is both) and, for a resource, on its card (4px left stripe, tinted chips). Never colour alone: the shapes differ and every symbol has an aria-label.
  - An ACTION LINE is two text lines high at least (70px), and under its list number stands its TOTAL ACTION ORDER NUMBER ("#12", small, muted, bottom of the cell): the count of the Segment's DISTINCT actions down the table (category, channel, stage, line), a shared action numbered where it first appears and showing that SAME number on every row it stands in (1..N, no gaps); numbers never change while rows are folded or isolated. Typing "#12" (hash and digits) in an action line offers action 12 as its one suggestion and Enter or a click shares it there (the same action; a number that refers to nothing, or to an action the row already holds, offers nothing and the text stays as typed).
  - A line left EMPTY disappears when focus leaves it (a moment later, so a click beside it is not lost) unless it is the row's only line, which keeps its one empty line; Enter still opens a new line to type in.
  - The x (32 x 32) stays 6px in from the top and right and the SYMBOLS (24 x 24 hit area, 16px glyph, absolutely placed so nothing moves on hover) stand UNDER it: one symbol directly under; two, text of 1 or 2 lines: side by side under it; two, 3+ lines: one under the other; two, one line that fits with both beside the x: side by side level with it (the list sets `data-marks` = under | pair | stack | beside on the row from the text's measured size, at a width that does not depend on the placement, so it cannot flip back and forth). The text keeps its full width except the 16px (pair) or 48px (beside) the symbols need. Pointing at (or focusing) a symbol shows the app's tooltip (`.app-tooltip`) ABOVE its ACTION CELL (the line's box), its RIGHT edge on the cell's right edge (below the cell only with no room above, still right aligned), nowrap, hugging its one line -- "Shared in N places", "Linked to <resource>", "Linked to N resources" -- and Escape closes it.
  - A CLICK ISOLATES (one at a time; a second click, Escape (consumed, so it is not Back) or the note in the title row restores everything and returns the keyboard to the symbol; `aria-pressed` on the pressed one): every action line not involved collapses (height and opacity, ~.2s, none under reduced motion; hidden to the keyboard once shut), the stage rows, channels and categories left with none are not drawn (categories folded shut are shown open meanwhile, their fold untouched), and EVERY Add button/row of the Segment's table goes (+ Add resource, offering, journey stage, channel, channel category, Link an action). NOTHING IS CREATED while a Segment is isolated: no creation path works in its table (Enter in an action line opens no new line, the Links tick of a channel cannot open a new box and its list takes no new line, a click-to-pick "Link an action" in progress ends); the text already there stays editable. A CLICK ON EMPTY SPACE also restores everything (focus back on the symbol, as the other exits): anywhere that is not a control or a cell's content -- the page's background, the table's head, a blank part of a cell. A button, field, action line (number, text and symbols), resource card or chip, list, tooltip or label keeps the isolation; so does a press that began on one and was let go outside it.
  - Shared isolation also hides the resource cards and "Costs: ..." lines not linked to the action. Resource isolation (from an action's symbol, or the "action" / "N actions" words of a resource card's summary line) draws only the isolated resource card(s) in the channel cells (no other card, no cost summary), each with ALL its actions as chips, the chip of the action clicked from ringed in the resource colour and bolder (none, if clicked from the card). The note at the title row's far end names everything, wrapping rather than cutting: "Shared: Instagram, Pinterest x" (the channels holding the action, in table order) or "Linked: Shopify Basic, Klaviyo x". A resource card's chips read "#<total order number> <action words>" on one line, ellipsised (the full text is the title); typing "#12" or just "12" in "Link an action" finds an action of the Segment's WHOLE table -- any channel's, not only the card's own (the exact number first and selected, then numbers that start with it, then words); the click-to-pick takes any action line of that Segment's table. A resource may so be linked to an action that stands only in another channel: its card stays in its own channel's cell and shows in the cell of every channel whose rows hold the action.
  - A card shared into another channel's cell reads its name and cost, then "via shared action" under them, in words (no symbol): "shared action" is a link (the notepad-text-button style of "# Link an action") that goes to that action's line and puts the caret in it, the same jump as the "#N" buttons of the Channel resources table; shared through several actions it reads "via shared actions #3 #7", one such link each. Isolating a SHARED action shows every card linked to it in every channel kept: at home in the channel it was made on, "via shared action" in the others.
  - Starting an isolation takes the top of that Segment's Customer Channel section (its title row, with the note) to just under the app header (smooth, none under reduced motion); ending one scrolls nothing. Pointing at a shared row lights its twins, and at a resource card or symbol its actions, on the tint's wash.
- **Sales Forecast**: what the block is for and where its Expected Sales are used are the points of one "i" on its title, not paragraphs around its fields. A Customer Source card's fields stand on one row (Customer Source, Estimated, then the figure or the calculation's figures), each as narrow as it can be: the pickers hug their longest option, a number takes the step its largest value needs (XS to six characters, Code to ten), a cell gap apart, labels above, tops aligned; a row too long for the card wraps from the same left edge (at 768px every type with a calculation does, "Other" does not). The Basis stays under them at the card's width.
  - Under the sources and their add button, one row: Customers Buy (its picker hugging its longest option) and, for an Offering kept until cancelled, Cancel / Month (%) (XS), labels above, tops aligned; then the Expected Sales, Assumptions and Channels That Bring Them.

---

## 12. The Inventory help tile

**Where the Inventory and Completed tiles stand (Infrastructure & Capability)**

- **Both are help tiles in the footer's band** (`.workspace-footer`), level with the page's Back link, centred under the two cards, a gap equal to the cards' own between the two tiles. They take no room above the footer, so the chapters keep all of it and Infrastructure & Capability does not scroll where nothing needs it; the cards keep one 12px line above the row. The row is measured again when the tiles have their size, so it stands at the footer level.
- **The footer lets the pointer through**: only its Back link and its buttons take it, so the tiles in its band can be pressed.
- **The page's Back (in the footer) is the default blue** in every mode (`--text-primary`); the card's tones are for the Back inside a card's tab.
- Both tiles are rectangles with their title only: no list, no rule under the title. **In the footer's band they are the size of "+ Add Segment"** (owner, 2026-11-22 18:58 UTC+2: "When laptop (smaller screens) and the Inventory and completed button/tiles are rectangular, they should have the same size (Hight and width) with the Add segment button"): `--band-button-width` (215px, global.css) wide and `--add-button-height` (44px) high, and "+ Add Segment" that wide. The Inventory tile grows outwards to the left (about its bottom-right corner) under the pointer.
- **The Completed tile** (`.section-tiles-completed`) stands outside both sections and exists only while a card is completed. Everything that belongs to a completed card is the Completed grey: its tile (also in the Inventory, rule 36), the tags above it, the arrows of the Completed dialog, the wireframe of a completed floating card. Rule 36 and the Completed grey of section 8 are the same grey.
- Not re-checked: while the Completed tile exists, the rule "Offering's Capability shows all its tiles without a bar" is waived (the card scrolls inside).
- The same window gives the same four tiles on every page (section 23, at the end of this rulebook).

**The Inventory dialog**

- **Bar**: the count (with its "i" for what is incomplete), the legend (four underlined text options, `TextLink`, pressed = underlined, in the Type order: Resource, Activity, Partner, Supplier), the views, the text size and the funnel. **Tiles / Table** is a `RadioChoice`; **the text size** is three "Aa" radios in 14, 18 and 24px (`TABLE_SIZES`). Tiles, Table and the three "Aa" stand one 16px gap apart, the same between all five, and the space before the first, between the two groups and before the funnel is equal.
- **A bar shown is grabbed with the hand, and the Inventory's bar is the app's blue**: the app hides every scroll bar but where a list scrolls on its own (the Inventory, the Forecast's table); over such a bar the pointer is an open hand, and a closed one while it holds the bar (`ScrollbarGrab.tsx`, `[data-on-scrollbar]`, `[data-scrollbar-grabbing]` in global.css). The Inventory's bar is 8px in the links' blue of the reading mode (`--text-primary`); its track starts at the first row's top; from the last tile to the bar 12px (the scroll box's right padding) and from the bar to the card's edge 12px (the dialog's padding). Its tiles, as many across as fit at the page tile's side, grow to fill the row to its last pixel (`TileGrid` `fill`): squares a little larger than the page tile, never by more than one tile and gap shared across the row (the layout check's "card tiles are squares of the page tile's side" allows that, there only).
- **The filter and sort window opens outward, always**: right of the dialog; else left; else the dialog narrows (its left edge kept, back to full when the window closes) to leave room; inside the dialog only below a 640px dialog. It opens at the dialog's top (no gap above), its top on the table's top and its foot at the dialog's foot; it has a maximum height and scrolls inside. It takes the **card's tint** (ground, line, ink, its glow and focus ring, its arrows), not the app's blue or a neutral.
- **Width**: as wide as its dropdown cells need (a cell's closed width and 12px each side, about 244px). A line that is longer is one line cut by an ellipsis; the From / To cells do not widen the window (`contain: inline-size`). The old fixed 250px menu (`.strategy-sort-menu-wide`) keeps only the rules that the window shares with it.
- **One 32px pitch**: every tick and every order stands on it (a cell is 32px; a tooltip is a 32px cell). Spacing is `--space-field` (12px) and `--space-label` (6px) only; Status and Frequency sit close to their labels (the label's line is no taller than its letters); a tick's words are the text colour and their tails (g, j, p, q, y) are not cut.
- **Controls are the app's**: ticks are `TickField`, the dropdowns `OptionPicker` (`hug`, `tight`, `keepWidth`: they keep the width of their longest option and do not take the row), the range `NumberCell`s (From and To share the window and stand a cell gap apart), the chip's x a `RemoveButton`. The cost order is two `FoldArrow` (`variant="label"`, `direction`, `pressed`: outlined, filled while pressed or chosen), a cell gap apart. Include, Sort, Belongs to and Columns have a fold arrow after the label (`MenuHead`); they start folded, and the state is kept while the Inventory is open (owner, Thanos, 2026-11-12 13:44: "Funnels should be folded."). Belongs to, Include, Status and Frequency list only what has cards. The "i" is filled with the ground it stands on (`--info-fill`).
- **Funnel blocks**: Include, Sort, Belongs to (under Offering, the Organisation's Offerings by name, ticks, before Type), Columns, then Status / Frequency / Linked / Costs, and directly under the Costs tick, above its range, the **Channel Costs** set-up (a tick, shown where there are channel expenses: only the channel expenses, the highest cost first, the columns Type, Group, Cost, Frequency, Behaviour, Offering, Journey and Actions; owner, 2026-11-12 23:54). The four with a fold arrow (Include, Sort, Belongs to, Columns) start folded and stay as the user leaves them while the Inventory is open; Status / Frequency / Linked / Costs stand open, with no fold arrow; range labels From / To; buttons read "High to low" / "Low to high"; the tick "Linked". A Columns list (ticks, `BusinessTicks`, one per column) chooses which columns after Name the table shows. Isolating the costs hides Status and Linked from the table (the user can tick them back); turning it off shows them again.

**The table**

- **Columns, in order**: Name (always shown), Type, Section, Category, Group, Cost, Frequency, Cost Nature, Behaviour, Status, Offering, Journey, Actions, Linked (`TABLE_COLUMNS`; Offering, Journey and Actions off at first). A channel expense's Type has "Channel" under its own, and its Group the channel over its subchannel, a rule between; each of those names keeps one line, so the two cells' lines stand level. The Cost heading carries the currency's symbol, as every amount's label does ("Cost (£)", `labelWithCurrency`). Cost Nature and Behaviour are classified by the same rules as the Financial Foundation (`inventoryClassification`). Linked shows only the link bullets. The columns are named (`data-col`), not counted.
- **Channel is not a column.** A Customer Channel's name follows its card's group: the card's Group reads "Channel · Website › Online Store" (one row, cut by an ellipsis past 20rem).
- **Name** is the app's `TextLink` at the table's size, one row cut by an ellipsis between 14 and 22rem; the column and the heading are kept while the table scrolls sideways (it does, where it does not fit). The default text is the small one below 1900px of window width.
- **The fixed Name column stays on top**: its cells lay the row's tint (zebra, hover) over the table's ground, opaque, so the cells scrolled under it never show through; its heading is fixed to the top and the left, above the other headings; the row's colour bar stays on its left edge (owner, 2026-11-12 23:54).
- **Grouped rows and wrapping cells** (`data-grouped`, `data-wrap` on the shared table; owner, 2026-11-13 05:23): a group's cell merged over its rows (rowspan; a channel over its subchannels, no letters), the striping by group (`data-band`), the pointer's row still lit; with `data-wrap` every cell wraps and nothing is cut by an ellipsis, a name link included (owner, 11:44: "The texts in the table, should be wrapped, not cut using dots"): every column but the actions is as wide as its words, a text of up to 8em on one row and a longer one wrapping at 8em, and the actions take the rest. With `data-bare` the table is a dialog card's own make (owner, 11:44: "there is no reason to have the table in a frame"; 12:32: "the dialogue card still has borders!"): no line round it or between its cells, its heading a band of the card's hue and its rows bands, running edge to edge of its dialog as a band does (`--space-cell` each side), its first and last texts where the dialog's title stands. The dialog keeps the one edge and shadow every dialog has. The Journey Stages table is all three; its dialog's head carries the Table / Tiles choice on the title's row, and its last column is "Actions" (the stage is the dialog's title).
- **The relationship card's stage grid** (owner, 2026-11-13 11:44): Journey Stage 7.5rem ("Evaluation" and its "i") and Goals 5rem ("Grow" and its tick), the Approach the rest, its text box filling the column.
- **A box's title band with an x** (EngagementBox; owner, 2026-11-13 12:32: "follow the rules (colour bands, x buttons)"): its figure and the x share the band's own row and line, the x in the card's x column (its right edge the name cell's, as every floating card's x), the figure just before it. A relationship type's own card has the default card head (its number in the Offering's dialog, its name, its x while the Offering has another type); its bands follow the head, the first tinted.
- **The owner's points of 2026-11-13 13:56.** A `data-bare` table has one unbroken line between every two columns (heading and rows, the card's divider colour), a line between two rows of one group (`data-follows`; the merged cell undivided), every cell's text at its top, and its dialog ends with its last row (no room kept under it; the dialog's foot takes no row). The relationship card's column lines run unbroken through every band, and its heading row is a band of the card's hue with bold headings, as the shared table's heading. A tile's two-column list (name and figure) has a hairline at the top of every row after the first, taking no room; its figure keeps its unit whole ("40 a month" breaks only after the number). A tile listing a name with a longer answer (an Offering and its relationship types) sets the answer bold under the name, the tile list's divider between one and the next.
- **The owner's points of 2026-11-13 16:12 and 16:35.** A stage table's actions are numbered from 1 in each row (the shared value list's `numbered`), a line between one and the next across the cell. An Offering in Sales Forecast is a tile opening its dialog: Expected Sales a section of the dialog itself (its title band, then how its customers buy, the result and the Assumptions; then the Channels That Bring Them), then a card tile per Customer Source (each opening that source's card, its kind the name, its figure under it, its x), with All the suggested sources faded and the Add Card tile. A list of ticks with a whole choice (the Inventory's Offerings) starts with All ticked; ticking one leaves All, ticking All or unticking every one comes back to it.
- **Target size, WCAG 2.2 SC 2.5.8 (Level AA) (owner, 2026-11-13 16:50).** A row of a tile's list is at least 24px high (`--tile-row`: the list's line height, or 24px where the text is smaller), its bullets, dividers and the room under it following the same row, so a row that is a link is a 24px target clear of the next. A card tile's link circle (12px) takes the click across a 24px square round it, the circles 12px apart. The probe's rule E checks both.
- **The lines of a grouped table** (owner, 2026-11-14 09:31): within a group (a channel's subchannels) the line between two rows is the heavier rule, 2px; between one group and the next a double line of two regular rules (3px double); both in the card's divider colour, the merged cell undivided.
- **The merged column** (owner, 2026-11-14 11:15): the line after it is the heavier rule (2px), heading and rows; its words wrap at 6.5em. A tile's two-column line that names a card or a box is a link by its name, the name wrapping as a plain one; bold words in a link keep the bold's own spacing. The funnel's window grows to its longest line up to 22rem.
- **Table mode** uses `--table-ground`, `--table-ink`, `--table-line`, `--table-tint` and `--table-hover` per theme (ink at least 7:1), a sticky heading and the three text sizes (`--font-small`, `--font-body`, `--font-h3`). Its look is the channels' table's (owner, 2026-11-12 19:54): a hairline between every two cells (the cells drawing their right and bottom edges, the table its top and left, round at its corners), the heading a band of the card's hue with its words wrapping, and a 4px bar of each row's colour down its left edge (a channel expense's of the channels' palette, any other card's its place's hue). Every table of a dialog that lists cards is this table (`capability-overview-table`): the Journey Stages dialog's stage table too, at the smallest of the three sizes.
- The legend says Supplier (it said Provider); "Partners and Providers" is "Partners & Suppliers" everywhere ("&" in names, owner 2026-10-29/30, 2026-11-19 00:24). The Offering's Type list has one "Partners & Suppliers" option whichever of the two the place has.
- **Words**: every tooltip of the Inventory is one sentence that begins "Click to ..." (design Interface 8).

---

- **The channel expenses are the Inventory's** (owner, 2026-11-12 18:41 and 19:54: "the Channel costs could fit well in the inventory"; "There is no need having the Channel Cost tile anymore"): its Channel Costs set-up; Customer Engagement has no tile of its own for them. An action's number in the Actions column opens its channel on Customer Engagement.

## 13. Identity, Brand and Strategy: column pages, Legal Entity, Brand Style, Founders Profile, Strategic Period

- **A strategy card's navigation** (`CardNav`, in the margin lane at the card's right edge): the two arrows are the page arrows at 15/22 of their size (their shape, box, grow-by-a-fifth and ring), in the colour of the head's "+" button (`--accent-ink`), not the card's ground, so they stand out from it; the rail's bullets take the same tint. The arrows stand on the centre line of the card's Description label; their vertical sides stand 12px from the card's edge, one on each side: the left arrow's on the x's right side, the right arrow's on the left side of the symbol of four arrows above it (the owner's marked picture). The symbol and the rail stand 12px from the card's edge.
- **A strategy card's head**: the "i" is 6px (`--info-gap`) after its word, like every "i"; the "+" is 12px after the "i" and the x 12px after the "+" (the owner).
- **Channel expenses (Customer Engagement)**: "+ Add expenses" and "Typical costs for this channel" are card-list add buttons in the channel cell, same size as "+ Add Journey Stage"; template cards use the channel teal tone (`--channel-marker`) and the cascade link rules above.
- **A column's guidance is the "i" beside its dialog title** (`DialogHeading info`), never bullets under it. The guidance of the Brand pages (Style, Inventory) and of the Themes (beside the Add Theme button) opens in a note of double width (`InfoNote size="double"`, 480px).
- **A founder's card in its dialog** stands one field under the other (`.founders-profile-row`), so every row has the room it was drawn for.
- **A page of one chapter** (the Strategy's Situation & Direction, the Financials' Financial Foundation, any column page drawn as tiles with a single frame) **stands at the left**, its frame as wide as one chapter of a page of two, where chapters stand side by side; in the vertical layout, the page's whole width, as stacked chapters are (section 23) (a chapter of TWO GROUPS, the Segment's, is not such a page: it spans the page, below, and in the vertical layout stacks its groups; the Financials' one chapter of three tiles is such a page since 2026-11-20 21:33, section 23) (the page's grid less its gap, halved: `calc(50% + 7px)` of the spanned grid), so the tiles are the same size on every page; a page of several chapters has its frames side by side across the page. **The Founders & Ownership dialog is a nested card**: the Ownership in the dialog itself, the founders' profile tiles under it, a divider line between them (`SubTiles` with its `header`: the line is `.sub-tiles-rule`, edge to edge, 12px either side), each opening the founder's card.
- **The Organisation tile's dialog** (design Questions 2, 8.5): two columns of sections in bands (the left: Name, Country with its State or Region, Address; the right: Legal Form, Registration Date, Opening Date, Activities; below both, a divider line and the Notes. The stage follows the Opening Date and the choice questions are on the Positioning tile: design Questions 2, 8.2), the sections 12px apart in both columns, a label 6px over its control, a 1px divider (`--line-divider`) down the middle of the gap with 12px clear on each side. The rows of the two columns fall on the same lines through the same rhythm (an open activity's head row is 44px), never through space put in by hand. **Activities** are the Team rows (`business-capability-performers`): the sub-label (Primary, Secondary, Tertiary, Quaternary) at the left with the label arrow inside it (glyph on the content edge, words 26px from it), the "+" in the first row's x column and the x in the others; a folded activity shows its code (with its scheme's initials, bold) beside the sub-label and the last category under them (a plain `marquee-text` line); an open one shows its dropdowns under their level names; everything under a sub-label starts on the column's left edge and ends 8px before the x column, which is also where every other control of the right column (Business stage) ends; a dropdown's list is as wide as its longest title (the cell's width at least, 560px at most) and wraps a longer one. Customer type and Market type are `RadioChoice`, one option under the other.
- **The Positioning tile's dialog** (Questions 8): two columns of question bands with the 1px divider, grouped by relationship. The Seller & Buyer Relationship stands first, on the left; each relationship chosen asks its questions together, in their order (B2C: the buyer's life stage, the buyer's category, the buyer and end user relationship, the end user's life stage; B2B: the scale, the buyer's category, the buyer and end user relationship). With both chosen, the B2C group stands on the left under the first question and the B2B group on the right above the Purpose, in two EQUAL columns (`legal-entity-audience-both`, as the Segment card's), so the columns hold about as much; with one, its group is on the left (as wide as its longest option) and the Purpose alone on the right. A double rule stands between one group and the next in a column; every other band of a column is tinted. Under 901px it is one column.

- **Legal Entity** is one large tile, **Registrations & Expenses** (the owner's approved plan of 2026-11-18, step 3; it was Registrations and Expenses, and before 2026-11-16 a Capability tile too, removed then: the skills the business still needs are asked on Capacity & Delivery), with two sub tiles: Registrations and Expenses (owner, 2026-11-19 16:52 UTC+2: the Registrations and the Additional Legal Requirements one dialog card); the Expenses' help is the dialog's "i". **Founders & Ownership is a master tile of Definition & Purpose's Definition chapter**, after Organisation and Positioning: its answer is still the Legal Entity's and the Founders Profile's, and its fields keep the Legal Entity section's keys (a field page of their own, `legalEntitySectionNumber`); its face lists the founders, each a link to the founder's card in its dialog (`foundersTileFace`); its dialog is drawn by the Legal Entity editor (`foundersOnly`): the owners' table, then the founders' tiles under the section "Profiles" (`DialogSectionBand`, owner, 2026-11-09); a link to it (`legal-section-1`) opens Definition. Registrations & Expenses holds two sub tiles: Registrations (one dialog card: the former sections 3. Formation & Registration and 4. Tax & Business Registration as its sections, a band each, then the Additional Legal Requirements, a titled section of nested card tiles, `DialogSectionBand`; its id stays `registration-numbers`) and Expenses (card tiles). **Each of these tiles lists exactly what its dialog holds, in the dialog's order and words**: the Registrations & Expenses tile its two sub tiles ("Registrations", "Expenses"); the Expenses sub tile each expense by its name; Registrations every row with something written, "name: number", with no status line (the registry as the dialog shows it, "Companies House: 16234587"; the further numbers; the tax number under its label, "Tax Identification Number (UTR): 3456789012"; the consumption tax where it is asked and ticked, "VAT: GB 412 3456 78"; the further tax registrations, "PAYE Employer Reference: 120/AB45678"), then every requirement by the name its box shows ("Unnamed requirement" where it has none), each a link to its card. Every line is reachable: the list scrolls (`LegalEntityEditor.tsx`; the test reads both examples). A requirement's card tile says its Type and whether it has expenses; an expense's says its start date and its registration.
- **A legal requirement's Type cell** takes all the room after its label.
- **Quillfield (the example) is a sole proprietorship**.
- **A legal requirement's or expense's card in its tile** is a grid of the head's own columns, and everything under the head stands in the name's column (`.column-tile-content .legal-entity-card`): the Type, the Date and the Responsibility have their label beside the control, to the name cell's right edge; the Reference or Number and the Linked registration are as wide as the name cell; the Cost and the Frequency share the row. "Add to Legal Entity Expenses" is one line.
- **The Style page carries the Icon & Logo and the Brand Images** (the Inventory page is gone): four large tiles. The Inventory's answer is saved as the Style answer's `inventory` (StorageService moves a saved one; the examples were moved). The page's sections are `BRAND_STYLE_PAGE_SECTIONS`.
- **A colour or typeface card's Role and Colour cells** are one width (the Date step) and start together; the colour is written as a hex (# and six digits); the picker also writes it as RGB or HSL. The Typography tile sets its typeface names at the body size.
- **A row that stands at the right stands whole** (label and cell together): the Role and Colour rows end at the name cell's right edge.
- **The colour picker's hex** stands beside the code's picker (80px picker, the hex the rest of the row, both 44px) with no label. The code's picker, the hex and the channels are cells of one height.
- **Image, Icon and Logo have one layout**: the label over, the holder with its x beside it (12px after its right edge, level with its top; in an image's card the x stands in the card's x column), the file's name under, a Notes field last. A wide picture scales in proportion.
- **The Icon's description is a Notes field** behind its tick. **A generated coloured box matches its card's colour exactly** (the Roadmap's corner takes the card's ground formula).
- **Founders Profile**: a founder's tile lists the roles, a line (`TILE_DIVIDER`), then the skills and degrees, with bullets; the dialogs have no frame; the Skills' kind drop-down is half width.
- **Help notes**: the Brand pages' and the Themes' note is 480px (`InfoNote size="double"`); the Strategic Period's title note holds the period and the Themes guidance, the one beside Add Theme the common Themes. The period's Start, End and the plan's dates stand in the dialog's second column.
- **No Sections rail on a page drawn as tiles** (`drawnAsTiles`); the sections are still the page's (the rail items name them elsewhere).
- **The colour map** (`WorkColours.ts`, docs/design/foundation/01-current-design.md, part I) colours the tile pages: Definition, Ambassador and Style Create (purple), Purpose Decide (orange), Legal Entity and Founders Profile Define (green), Situation & Direction Discover (blue). A page's colour says the work done in it; a tile, its dialog and its floating cards take that hue.
- **A page can hold several sections** (`PageGroups.ts`): the Identity System's ONE page since 2026-11-20 (owner, 13:02 UTC+2: "Legal Entity - Move the master Tile to the 1st page, in the Definition Chapter. 1. Identity System will have only one page."), named as its System, as Brand's and Strategy's are; its chapters and their tiles are `specifications/01-identity-system/01-definition.md`, section 1 (the one owner): Purpose's Mission & Vision draws the two texts saved in Definition under Definition's keys (`pageSections`, Questions 9), and the Legal Entity's tiles stand in the Definition chapter, drawn by its editor and keeping its answer and keys (`handsTiles`, `joinedChapter.ts`, a tile's `section`; rule | was | now: one page for each System's purpose | a page "Legal Entity" holding one Master Tile in an otherwise empty frame | its tile in the Definition chapter's fourth place, the Legal Entity section drawing no frame); Brand's one page (Ambassador, Style) has no page name; the Business System's page 3 "Financials" (Revenue & Pricing, Costs, Funding & Position; 2026-11-19, Questions 20, section 11), drawn as ONE chapter since 2026-11-20 (owner, 02:11 UTC+2: "Maybe they should all be under only one chapter"; `oneChapter`, `joinedChapter.ts`): one frame headed "Financial Foundation", its Master Tiles in Questions 20, section 14, every Master Tile keeping its size (section 23). **A chapter of two groups spans the page as a Segment's chapter does** (`.segment-chapter`, `grid-column: 1 / -1`): its two groups stand where a page's two chapters stand, so a dialog covers its group's half and a floating card opens on the other half (section 10); rule | was | now: a page of one chapter stands at the left | the Financials' chapter in one column, half the page, its groups a quarter each, its dialogs and cards cramped and a card over the dialog's tiles | the chapter across the page, as the Segment's (2026-11-20); Costs, Funding & Position draws no frame and hands its tiles to the chapter. The title keeps the plan's months. Each section keeps its number, its title and its saved answer; the frames stand side by side, each with its heading ("1. Definition") on top of its tiles, as the Business System's Infrastructure & Capability page does. The first section is the page's own; the others are companions, not pages of their own (the pages rail, the arrows and "What it covers" skip them).
- **Foundation & Engagement, one Segment at a time** (`PageGroups.ts` `oneSegmentAtATime`, `segmentPage.ts`; the owner's approved plan of 2026-11-18, step 2; owner, 2026-11-19 13:40: "The chapter should be 2 rows of 4 columns, with a dividing vertical line in the middle, that would separate the foundation and the engagement"; 13:46: "Use the divide line as a border for the chapters. So to avoid changing the layout of the dialogue and floated cards, because they master tile get in the same chapter"): the Business System's sections 1 and 2 are one page, "Foundation & Engagement". It shows ONE Segment, the one lit on the page's right-hand rail, titled Segments (`BulletRail`, the slot a page drawn as tiles leaves empty); the other Segments are drawn and hidden, and a jump to a part of one shows it first. A Segment is a CHAPTER of TWO SUBCHAPTERS side by side (owner, 2026-11-20 13:28 UTC+2: "Segment - chapter, Business Foundation, and Engagement - subchapters"), drawn exactly as a page's two chapters stand -- Business Foundation's 2 x 2 (Segment Description, Competitors, Offerings, Value Proposition) and Engagement's three in a 2 x 2 (Channels & Journey, Customer's Relationship, Sales Forecast) -- each an anchored card, so every dialog and floating card behaves as on any page of two chapters (section 10) and every Master Tile is the shared size (section 23: 326px at 1920 x 1000, 210px at 1366 x 768). Round both stands one frame, drawn as a chapter's frame in the Segment's own hue (`segmentHue`, by its id), its edge inside so it takes no room; its head over both is the Segment's number and name, one centred heading ("1. London Homemakers"), the name written there; a Segment the user added has the x in the frame's top-right corner, one the app made from the Identity System's answers (`madeBy: "identity"`, read by its id) has none. Between the chapters, their border: a 1px line in the card's divider colour (`--band-divider`), down the middle of the gap, from the top edge of the top tiles to the bottom edge of the bottom tiles. No sub-headings.
- **Without a Sections rail, no rail title** (`BulletRail`).
- **The Registrations & Expenses dialog** shows two sub tiles (`SubTiles.tsx`): Registrations (one dialog card: the registry's numbers and the tax's, a band each, then the Additional Legal Requirements as nested tiles; owner, 2026-11-19 16:52) and Expenses. The Legal Entity's tiles are in sentence case, with no order numbers; card tiles of requirements and expenses have centred lines with no bullets.
- **Column answers as parts**: Purpose' Values and Principles are numbered lists; a pair tile's dialog holds its two texts one under the other, each with its heading and "i", a divider between them; the Ambassador's Imprint is two texts, the First and the Lasting impression (the one answer, "First impression: ... Lasting impression: ..."). (The Founders' Capability, two texts, is removed: the skills the business still needs are LC1 on Capacity & Delivery; owner, 2026-11-16 20:25.)
- **A page's name in the pages rail** is its group's (`PageGroups.ts`): Definition & Purpose, Organisation, Brand System, Foundation & Engagement, Financials; its frames are numbered 1, 2 within the page, with their own names where given ("Founders & Capability").
- **A frame's heading** (Identity and Brand pages of several sections) is the column title of the Business System: 28px, and its tiles start at the same distance below it.
- **Registered is worked out, never chosen** (`formationStatusOf`): a registration number in the first row says Registered, none says not yet (a form with no company registry, a sole trader, counts as registered once it has a date). The Status is text; the Number is always asked; the Formation date shows once there is a number; the other numbers (a trade mark) never set the status. A number that looks short is questioned, not refused.
- **The registration authority is the country's own, filled in and shown as text** (`registrationAuthorityFor`): the user cannot mistype it. A country the app does not know leaves it a cell to write.
- **The registration numbers** (the former Formation card): no Status (the number registers it); the Registration Date is not here, it is on the Organisation tile, always shown and independent of the number (design Questions 2, 8.5); a list of further numbers takes at most five (`MAX_MORE_NUMBERS`), the registrations' and the tax registrations' alike.
- **A registration number takes only what its country's numbers hold** (`registrationNumberRule`, from the stated shape): "8 digits" takes digits only, no more than 8; "8 characters" letters and digits, no more than 8; a shape with separators takes what it shows. Further numbers (a trade mark) take up to 30 characters. The UK's UTR (and Brazil's CNPJ) follow the same rule through `taxIdNumberHint` (10 and 14 digits).
- **A founder's card, everything after the number**: the roles' bullets and the Responsibilities / Completed arrows start at the content edge (where a name's cell would), with their words after them, not hanging in the number's column. A founder's Skills kind drop-down is 14em on every row and its list as wide as the cell, so no kind takes more than two lines.
- Foundation & Engagement: each Segment is a chapter, of two subchapters in one frame, Business Foundation and Engagement (owner, 2026-11-20 13:28 UTC+2: "Segment - chapter, Business Foundation, and Engagement - subchapters") (above, "Foundation & Engagement, one Segment at a time"); its Business Foundation subchapter has four tiles: Segment (Description and Profiles), Value Proposition, Competitors, Offerings. Legal Registrations' tiles and Expenses' tiles carry circles for what they are linked to.
- Legal Entity arrows: the page has one tile, Registrations & Expenses; Definition's between Organisation, Positioning and Founders & Ownership; inside Registrations & Expenses between its two sub tiles; between the requirement cards; between the expense cards. A second bullet in a list means a channel's cards only; a link is a circle.
- Foundation & Engagement: each Segment's Business Foundation subchapter has Customers (Description and Profiles as sub-tiles), Value Proposition, Competitors and Offerings; every part is a floating card on its own tile.
- Strategy: a tile per Theme, its Goals nested in the Theme's card (several Goals: one at a time with the card's arrows, or as tiles inside the card; no Goal floats; Questions 21, 2.6, "The nested cards stay as built"); the Roadmap is one large tile, drawn in its dialog. Delivery shows a tile per Offering's Delivery card.
- Strategy cards (Goal, Objective, Metric) have no fold arrow and no grip; a card head is one row: number and "i", the name cell, its buttons. Generated tiles (Goals, Objectives) have a label above them; a Theme tile has a "+" to add a Goal. The Roadmap page has no Sections rail.
- Value Proposition is a section of a Segment (a tile that opens a dialog and is stepped through by the arrows); a single founder is a section of Infrastructure, likewise.
- **One founder**: Salary, Frequency, Working Hrs and Delivery Hrs stand beside their labels, in cells of the same width as before.
- **Strategy cards (Theme, Goal, Objective, Metric) heads**: the order number stands inside the name cell, then the kind label with its "i" (capitals), a "+" and the x; the "+" adds a NEW CARD OF THE SAME KIND beside this one (a Theme, a Goal, an Objective, a Metric); there are no other "+ Add" buttons. What stands under a head starts at the name cell's left (12px) and ends 12px before the x (a 56px margin at the right).
